Implement the following plan:

# Plan: Add checkpoints.json to Trails + Semantic Trail Titles

## Context

Two issues with the trail system:

1. **Missing `checkpoints.json`**: The web app (PR entirehq/entire.io#738) expects each trail on `entire/trails` to have a `checkpoints.json` linking checkpoints to the trail. The CLI only writes `metadata.json` and `discussion.json`.

2. **Useless trail titles**: `AutoCreateTrail` uses `HumanizeBranchName()` which produces garbage like "Dipree/blacksmith agent integration". The user's prompt is available at TurnStart and should be used instead.

## Part A: checkpoints.json

### A1. Add types to trail package
**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/trail/trail.go`

```go
type CheckpointRef struct {
    CheckpointID string    `json:"checkpoint_id"`
    CommitSHA    string    `json:"commit_sha"`
    CreatedAt    time.Time `json:"created_at"`
    Summary      string    `json:"summary,omitempty"`
}

type Checkpoints struct {
    Checkpoints []CheckpointRef `json:"checkpoints"`
}
```

### A2. Update trail store to read/write checkpoints.json
**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/trail/store.go`

- Add `checkpointsFile = "checkpoints.json"` constant
- Update `Write()` signature: add `checkpoints *Checkpoints` parameter. When nil, write empty `{"checkpoints":[]}`. Write blob to `<trailPath>/checkpoints.json`
- Update all `Write()` callers: `trail_cmd.go:AutoCreateTrail`, `trail_cmd.go:runTrailCreate`, `store.go:Update`
- Update `Read()` to return `*Checkpoints` as 3rd return value
- Add `AddCheckpoint(trailID ID, ref CheckpointRef) error` — reads existing, prepends new ref (newest first), writes back via `Write()`
- Update `Update()` to preserve existing checkpoints (read them, pass through to Write)

### A3. Link checkpoints to trails on condensation (manual-commit)
**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go`

Add `Prompts []string` field to `CondenseResult` (in `manual_commit_types.go`). Populate it in `CondenseSession()` from `sessionData.Prompts`.

**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`

In `condenseAndUpdateState()`, after successful `CondenseSession()` (line 811), call:
```go
appendCheckpointToTrail(repo, result.CheckpointID, head.Hash(), result.Prompts)
```

New helper (in `manual_commit_hooks.go`):
```go
func appendCheckpointToTrail(repo *git.Repository, cpID id.CheckpointID, commitSHA plumbing.Hash, prompts []string) {
    branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
    if branchName == "" {
        return
    }
    defaultBranch := GetDefaultBranchName(repo)
    if branchName == defaultBranch {
        return
    }
    store := trail.NewStore(repo)
    existing, err := store.FindByBranch(branchName)
    if err != nil || existing == nil {
        return
    }
    var summary string
    if len(prompts) > 0 {
        summary = truncateForSummary(prompts[len(prompts)-1], 200)
    }
    _ = store.AddCheckpoint(existing.TrailID, trail.CheckpointRef{
        CheckpointID: cpID.String(),
        CommitSHA:    commitSHA.String(),
        CreatedAt:    time.Now().UTC(),
        Summary:      summary,
    })
}
```

### A4. Link checkpoints to trails (auto-commit)
**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/auto_commit.go`

In `SaveStep()`, after successful metadata commit (after line 163):
```go
appendCheckpointToTrail(repo, cpID, codeResult.CommitHash, nil)
// Summary: use ctx.CommitMessage since prompts aren't extracted at this point
```

Actually, auto-commit's `StepContext` doesn't have prompts. Use `ctx.CommitMessage` as summary, or pass nil and skip summary.

## Part B: Semantic Trail Titles

### B1. Pass prompt through to trail auto-creation
**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/lifecycle.go`

```go
// Line 149: change
autoCreateTrailOnTurnStart()
// to
autoCreateTrailOnTurnStart(event.Prompt)
```

Update `autoCreateTrailOnTurnStart` signature to `(prompt string)`. Pass through:
```go
AutoCreateTrail(repo, branchName, baseBranch, prompt)
```

### B2. Use prompt for trail title
**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/trail_cmd.go`

Update `AutoCreateTrail` to accept `prompt string`:
```go
func AutoCreateTrail(repo *git.Repository, branchName, baseBranch, prompt string) error {
    ...
    title := titleFromPrompt(prompt)
    if title == "" {
        title = trail.HumanizeBranchName(branchName)
    }
    ...
}
```

Add helper:
```go
func titleFromPrompt(prompt string) string {
    if prompt == "" {
        return ""
    }
    line, _, _ := strings.Cut(prompt, "\n")
    title := strings.TrimSpace(line)
    if len(title) > 80 {
        title = title[:77] + "..."
    }
    return title
}
```

### B3. Update CLAUDE.md
Document `checkpoints.json` format and prompt-based title generation.

```
     1→// Package trail provides types and helpers for managing trail metadata.
     2→// Trails are branch-centric work tracking abstractions stored on the
     3→// entire/trails orphan branch. They answer "why/what" (human intent)
     4→// while checkpoints answer "how/when" (machine snapshots).
     5→package trail
     6→
     7→import (
     8→	"crypto/rand"
     9→	"encoding/hex"
    10→	"fmt"
    11→	"regexp"
    12→	"strings"
    13→	"time"
    14→)
    15→
    16→const idLength = 6 // 6 bytes = 12 hex chars
    17→
    18→// ID is a 12-character hex identifier for trails.
    19→type ID string
    20→
    21→// EmptyID represents an unset or invalid trail ID.
    22→const EmptyID ID = ""
    23→
    24→// idRegex validates the format: exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.
    25→var idRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{12}$`)
    26→
    27→// GenerateID creates a new random 12-character hex trail ID.
    28→func GenerateID() (ID, error) {
    29→	bytes := make([]byte, idLength)
    30→	if _, err := rand.Read(bytes); err != nil {
    31→		return EmptyID, fmt.Errorf("failed to generate random trail ID: %w", err)
    32→	}
    33→	return ID(hex.EncodeToString(bytes)), nil
    34→}
    35→
    36→// ValidateID checks if a string is a valid trail ID format.
    37→func ValidateID(s string) error {
    38→	if !idRegex.MatchString(s) {
    39→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid trail ID %q: must be 12 lowercase hex characters", s)
    40→	}
    41→	return nil
    42→}
    43→
    44→// String returns the trail ID as a string.
    45→func (id ID) String() string {
    46→	return string(id)
    47→}
    48→
    49→// IsEmpty returns true if the trail ID is empty or unset.
    50→func (id ID) IsEmpty() bool {
    51→	return id == EmptyID
    52→}
    53→
    54→// Path returns the sharded storage path for this trail ID.
    55→// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.
    56→// Example: "a3b2c4d5e6f7" -> "a3/b2c4d5e6f7"
    57→func (id ID) Path() string {
    58→	if len(id) < 3 {
    59→		return string(id)
    60→	}
    61→	return string(id[:2]) + "/" + string(id[2:])
    62→}
    63→
    64→// Status represents the lifecycle status of a trail.
    65→type Status string
    66→
    67→const (
    68→	StatusDraft      Status = "draft"
    69→	StatusOpen       Status = "open"
    70→	StatusInProgress Status = "in_progress"
    71→	StatusInReview   Status = "in_review"
    72→	StatusDone       Status = "done"
    73→	StatusClosed     Status = "closed"
    74→)
    75→
    76→// ValidStatuses returns all valid trail statuses in lifecycle order.
    77→func ValidStatuses() []Status {
    78→	return []Status{
    79→		StatusDraft,
    80→		StatusOpen,
    81→		StatusInProgress,
    82→		StatusInReview,
    83→		StatusDone,
    84→		StatusClosed,
    85→	}
    86→}
    87→
    88→// IsValid returns true if the status is a recognized trail status.
    89→func (s Status) IsValid() bool {
    90→	for _, vs := range ValidStatuses() {
    91→		if s == vs {
    92→			return true
    93→		}
    94→	}
    95→	return false
    96→}
    97→
    98→// Metadata represents the metadata for a trail, matching the web PR format.
    99→type Metadata struct {
   100→	TrailID     ID         `json:"trail_id"`
   101→	Branch      string     `json:"branch"`
   102→	Base        string     `json:"base"`
   103→	Title       string     `json:"title"`
   104→	Description string     `json:"description"`
   105→	Status      Status     `json:"status"`
   106→	Author      string     `json:"author"`
   107→	Assignees   []string   `json:"assignees"`
   108→	Labels      []string   `json:"labels"`
   109→	CreatedAt   time.Time  `json:"created_at"`
   110→	UpdatedAt   time.Time  `json:"updated_at"`
   111→	MergedAt    *time.Time `json:"merged_at,omitempty"`
   112→}
   113→
   114→// Discussion holds the discussion/comments for a trail.
   115→type Discussion struct {
   116→	Comments []Comment `json:"comments"`
   117→}
   118→
   119→// Comment represents a single comment on a trail.
   120→type Comment struct {
   121→	ID        string         `json:"id"`
   122→	Author    string         `json:"author"`
   123→	Body      string         `json:"body"`
   124→	CreatedAt time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
   125→	Replies   []CommentReply `json:"replies,omitempty"`
   126→}
   127→
   128→// CommentReply represents a reply to a comment.
   129→type CommentReply struct {
   130→	ID        string    `json:"id"`
   131→	Author    string    `json:"author"`
   132→	Body      string    `json:"body"`
   133→	CreatedAt time.Time `json:"created_at"`
   134→}
   135→
   136→// commonBranchPrefixes are stripped from branch names when humanizing.
   137→var commonBranchPrefixes = []string{
   138→	"feature/",
   139→	"fix/",
   140→	"bugfix/",
   141→	"chore/",
   142→	"hotfix/",
   143→	"release/",
   144→}
   145→
   146→// HumanizeBranchName converts a branch name into a human-readable title.
   147→// It strips common prefixes (feature/, fix/, etc.), replaces dashes/underscores
   148→// with spaces, and capitalizes the first word.
   149→func HumanizeBranchName(branch string) string {
   150→	name := branch
   151→	for _, prefix := range commonBranchPrefixes {
   152→		if strings.HasPrefix(name, prefix) {
   153→			name = strings.TrimPrefix(name, prefix)
   154→			break
   155→		}
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	// Replace - and _ with spaces
   159→	name = strings.NewReplacer("-", " ", "_", " ").Replace(name)
   160→
   161→	// Trim spaces and capitalize first letter
   162→	name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
   163→	if name == "" {
   164→		return branch
   165→	}
   166→
   167→	// Capitalize first character
   168→	return strings.ToUpper(name[:1]) + name[1:]
   169→}
   170→
```

```
     1→package trail
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"errors"

    10→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint"

    21→	discussionFile = "discussion.json"

    24→// ErrTrailNotFound is returned when a trail cannot be found.

    26→
    27→// Store provides CRUD operations for trail metadata on the entire/trails branch.
    28→type Store struct {
    29→	repo *git.Repository
    30→}
    31→
    32→// NewStore creates a new trail store backed by the given git repository.
    33→func NewStore(repo *git.Repository) *Store {
    34→	return &Store{repo: repo}
    35→}
    36→
    37→// EnsureBranch creates the entire/trails orphan branch if it doesn't exist.
    38→func (s *Store) EnsureBranch() error {
    39→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.TrailsBranchName)
    40→	_, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
    41→	if err == nil {
    42→		return nil // Branch already exists
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	// Create orphan branch with empty tree
    46→	emptyTreeHash, err := checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, make(map[string]object.TreeEntry))
    47→	if err != nil {
    48→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to build empty tree: %w", err)
    49→	}
    50→
    51→	authorName, authorEmail := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)
    52→	commitHash, err := s.createCommit(emptyTreeHash, plumbing.ZeroHash, "Initialize trails branch", authorName, authorEmail)
    53→	if err != nil {
    54→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create initial commit: %w", err)
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commitHash)
    58→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
    59→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set branch reference: %w", err)
    60→	}
    61→	return nil
    62→}
    63→
    64→// Write writes trail metadata and discussion to the entire/trails branch.
    65→func (s *Store) Write(metadata *Metadata, discussion *Discussion) error {
    66→	if metadata.TrailID.IsEmpty() {
    67→		return errors.New("trail ID is required")
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	if err := s.EnsureBranch(); err != nil {
    71→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure trails branch: %w", err)
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	// Get current branch tree
    75→	ref, entries, err := s.getBranchEntries()
    76→	if err != nil {
    77→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get branch entries: %w", err)
    78→	}
    79→
    80→	// Build sharded path
    81→	basePath := metadata.TrailID.Path() + "/"
    82→
    83→	// Create metadata blob
    84→	metadataJSON, err := json.MarshalIndent(metadata, "", "  ")
    85→	if err != nil {
    86→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal metadata: %w", err)
    87→	}
    88→	metadataBlob, err := checkpoint.CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, metadataJSON)
    89→	if err != nil {
    90→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create metadata blob: %w", err)
    91→	}
    92→	entries[basePath+metadataFile] = object.TreeEntry{
    93→		Name: basePath + metadataFile,
    94→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
    95→		Hash: metadataBlob,
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	// Create discussion blob
    99→	if discussion == nil {
   100→		discussion = &Discussion{Comments: []Comment{}}
   101→	}
   102→	discussionJSON, err := json.MarshalIndent(discussion, "", "  ")
   103→	if err != nil {
   104→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal discussion: %w", err)
   105→	}
   106→	discussionBlob, err := checkpoint.CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, discussionJSON)
   107→	if err != nil {
   108→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create discussion blob: %w", err)
   109→	}
   110→	entries[basePath+discussionFile] = object.TreeEntry{
   111→		Name: basePath + discussionFile,
   112→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   113→		Hash: discussionBlob,
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	// Build tree and commit
   117→	newTreeHash, err := checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)
   118→	if err != nil {
   119→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to build tree: %w", err)
   120→	}
   121→
   122→	authorName, authorEmail := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)
   123→	commitMsg := fmt.Sprintf("Trail: %s (%s)", metadata.Title, metadata.TrailID)
   124→	commitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, authorName, authorEmail)
   125→	if err != nil {
   126→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create commit: %w", err)
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	// Update branch ref
   130→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.TrailsBranchName), commitHash)
   131→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
   132→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update branch reference: %w", err)
   133→	}
   134→
   135→	return nil
   136→}
   137→
   138→// Read reads a trail by its ID from the entire/trails branch.
   139→func (s *Store) Read(trailID ID) (*Metadata, *Discussion, error) {
   140→	tree, err := s.getBranchTree()
   141→	if err != nil {
   142→		return nil, nil, err
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	basePath := trailID.Path() + "/"
   146→
   147→	// Read metadata
   148→	metadataEntry, err := tree.FindEntry(basePath + metadataFile)
   149→	if err != nil {
   150→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("trail %s not found: %w", trailID, err)
   151→	}
   152→	metadataBlob, err := s.repo.BlobObject(metadataEntry.Hash)
   153→	if err != nil {
   154→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read metadata blob: %w", err)
   155→	}
   156→	metadataReader, err := metadataBlob.Reader()
   157→	if err != nil {
   158→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open metadata reader: %w", err)
   159→	}
   160→	defer metadataReader.Close()
   161→
   162→	var metadata Metadata
   163→	if err := json.NewDecoder(metadataReader).Decode(&metadata); err != nil {
   164→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to decode metadata: %w", err)
   165→	}
   166→
   167→	// Read discussion (optional, may not exist yet)
   168→	var discussion Discussion
   169→	discussionEntry, err := tree.FindEntry(basePath + discussionFile)
   170→	if err == nil {
   171→		discussionBlob, blobErr := s.repo.BlobObject(discussionEntry.Hash)
   172→		if blobErr == nil {
   173→			discussionReader, readerErr := discussionBlob.Reader()
   174→			if readerErr == nil {
   175→				//nolint:errcheck,gosec // best-effort decode of optional discussion
   176→				json.NewDecoder(discussionReader).Decode(&discussion)
   177→				_ = discussionReader.Close()
   178→			}
   179→		}
   180→	}
   181→
   182→	return &metadata, &discussion, nil
   183→}
   184→
   185→// FindByBranch finds a trail for the given branch name.
   186→// Returns nil, nil, nil if no trail exists for the branch.
   187→func (s *Store) FindByBranch(branchName string) (*Metadata, error) {
   188→	trails, err := s.List()
   189→	if err != nil {
   190→		return nil, err
   191→	}
   192→
   193→	for _, t := range trails {
   194→		if t.Branch == branchName {
   195→			return t, nil
   196→		}
   197→	}
   198→	return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil, nil means "not found" — callers check both
   199→}
   200→
   201→// List returns all trail metadata from the entire/trails branch.
   202→func (s *Store) List() ([]*Metadata, error) {
   203→	tree, err := s.getBranchTree()
   204→	if err != nil {
   205→		// Branch doesn't exist yet — no trails
   206→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilerr // Expected when no trails exist yet
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	var trails []*Metadata
   210→	entries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)
   211→	if err := checkpoint.FlattenTree(s.repo, tree, "", entries); err != nil {
   212→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to flatten tree: %w", err)
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	// Find all metadata.json files
   216→	for path, entry := range entries {
   217→		if !strings.HasSuffix(path, "/"+metadataFile) {
   218→			continue
   219→		}
   220→
   221→		blob, err := s.repo.BlobObject(entry.Hash)
   222→		if err != nil {
   223→			continue
   224→		}
   225→		reader, err := blob.Reader()
   226→		if err != nil {
   227→			continue
   228→		}
   229→
   230→		var metadata Metadata
   231→		decodeErr := json.NewDecoder(reader).Decode(&metadata)
   232→		_ = reader.Close()
   233→		if decodeErr != nil {
   234→			continue
   235→		}
   236→
   237→		trails = append(trails, &metadata)
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	return trails, nil
   241→}
   242→
   243→// Update updates an existing trail's metadata. It reads the current metadata,
   244→// applies the provided update function, and writes it back.
   245→func (s *Store) Update(trailID ID, updateFn func(*Metadata)) error {
   246→	metadata, discussion, err := s.Read(trailID)
   247→	if err != nil {
   248→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read trail for update: %w", err)
   249→	}
   250→
   251→	updateFn(metadata)
   252→	metadata.UpdatedAt = time.Now()
   253→
   254→	return s.Write(metadata, discussion)
   255→}
   256→
   257→// Delete removes a trail from the entire/trails branch.
   258→func (s *Store) Delete(trailID ID) error {
   259→	ref, entries, err := s.getBranchEntries()
   260→	if err != nil {
   261→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get branch entries: %w", err)
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	basePath := trailID.Path() + "/"
   265→
   266→	// Remove entries for this trail
   267→	found := false
   268→	for path := range entries {
   269→		if strings.HasPrefix(path, basePath) {
   270→			delete(entries, path)
   271→			found = true
   272→		}
   273→	}
   274→	if !found {
   275→		return fmt.Errorf("trail %s not found", trailID)
   276→	}
   277→
   278→	// Build tree and commit
   279→	newTreeHash, err := checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)
   280→	if err != nil {
   281→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to build tree: %w", err)
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	authorName, authorEmail := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)
   285→	commitMsg := fmt.Sprintf("Delete trail: %s", trailID)
   286→	commitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, authorName, authorEmail)
   287→	if err != nil {
   288→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create commit: %w", err)
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.TrailsBranchName), commitHash)
   292→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
   293→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update branch reference: %w", err)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	return nil
   297→}
   298→
   299→// getBranchTree returns the tree for the entire/trails branch HEAD.
   300→func (s *Store) getBranchTree() (*object.Tree, error) {
   301→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.TrailsBranchName)
   302→	ref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
   303→	if err != nil {
   304→		// Try remote tracking branch
   305→		remoteRefName := plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", paths.TrailsBranchName)
   306→		ref, err = s.repo.Reference(remoteRefName, true)
   307→		if err != nil {
   308→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("trails branch not found: %w", err)
   309→		}
   310→	}
   311→
   312→	commit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   313→	if err != nil {
   314→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
   315→	}
   316→
   317→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   318→	if err != nil {
   319→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
   320→	}
   321→
   322→	return tree, nil
   323→}
   324→
   325→// getBranchEntries returns the current branch reference and a flat map of all tree entries.
   326→func (s *Store) getBranchEntries() (*plumbing.Reference, map[string]object.TreeEntry, error) {
   327→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.TrailsBranchName)
   328→	ref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
   329→	if err != nil {
   330→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("trails branch not found: %w", err)
   331→	}
   332→
   333→	commit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   334→	if err != nil {
   335→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
   336→	}
   337→
   338→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   339→	if err != nil {
   340→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
   341→	}
   342→
   343→	entries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)
   344→	if err := checkpoint.FlattenTree(s.repo, tree, "", entries); err != nil {
   345→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to flatten tree: %w", err)
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	return ref, entries, nil
   349→}
   350→
   351→// createCommit creates a commit on the trails branch.
   352→func (s *Store) createCommit(treeHash, parentHash plumbing.Hash, message, authorName, authorEmail string) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
   353→	now := time.Now()
   354→	sig := object.Signature{
   355→		Name:  authorName,
   356→		Email: authorEmail,
   357→		When:  now,
   358→	}
   359→
   360→	commit := &object.Commit{
   361→		TreeHash:  treeHash,
   362→		Author:    sig,
   363→		Committer: sig,
   364→		Message:   message,
   365→	}
   366→
   367→	if parentHash != plumbing.ZeroHash {
   368→		commit.ParentHashes = []plumbing.Hash{parentHash}
   369→	}
   370→
   371→	obj := s.repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
   372→	if err := commit.Encode(obj); err != nil {
   373→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to encode commit: %w", err)
   374→	}
   375→
   376→	hash, err := s.repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
   377→	if err != nil {
   378→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to store commit: %w", err)
   379→	}
   380→
   381→	return hash, nil
   382→}
   383→
```

```
    45→// runTrailShow shows the trail for the current branch, or falls through to list.

   104→
   105→func runTrailListAll(w io.Writer, statusFilter string, jsonOutput bool) error {
   106→	repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()
   107→	if err != nil {
   108→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   109→	}
   110→
   111→	store := trail.NewStore(repo)
   112→	trails, err := store.List()
   113→	if err != nil {
   114→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list trails: %w", err)
   115→	}
   116→
   117→	if trails == nil {
   118→		trails = []*trail.Metadata{}
   119→	}
   120→
   121→	// Apply status filter
   122→	if statusFilter != "" {
   123→		status := trail.Status(statusFilter)
   124→		if !status.IsValid() {
   125→			return fmt.Errorf("invalid status %q: valid values are %s", statusFilter, formatValidStatuses())
   126→		}
   127→		var filtered []*trail.Metadata
   128→		for _, t := range trails {
   129→			if t.Status == status {
   130→				filtered = append(filtered, t)
   131→			}
   132→		}
   133→		trails = filtered
   134→	}
   135→
   136→	// Sort by updated_at descending
   137→	sort.Slice(trails, func(i, j int) bool {
   138→		return trails[i].UpdatedAt.After(trails[j].UpdatedAt)
   139→	})
   140→
   141→	if jsonOutput {
   142→		enc := json.NewEncoder(w)
   143→		enc.SetIndent("", "  ")
   144→		if err := enc.Encode(trails); err != nil {
   145→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode JSON: %w", err)
   146→		}
   147→		return nil
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	if len(trails) == 0 {
   151→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "No trails found.")
   152→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
   153→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Commands:")
   154→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  entire trail create   Create a trail for the current branch")
   155→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  entire trail list     List all trails")
   156→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  entire trail update   Update trail metadata")
   157→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  entire trail enable   Enable automatic trail creation")
   158→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  entire trail disable  Disable automatic trail creation")
   159→		return nil
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	// Table output
   163→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "%-30s %-40s %-13s %-15s %s\n", "BRANCH", "TITLE", "STATUS", "AUTHOR", "UPDATED")
   164→	for _, t := range trails {
   165→		branch := truncate(t.Branch, 30)
   166→		title := truncate(t.Title, 40)
   167→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "%-30s %-40s %-13s %-15s %s\n",
   168→			branch, title, t.Status, truncate(t.Author, 15), timeAgo(t.UpdatedAt))
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	return nil
   172→}

   194→//nolint:cyclop // sequential steps for creating a trail — splitting would obscure the flow
   195→func runTrailCreate(w, errW io.Writer, title, description, base, branch, statusStr string) error {
   196→	repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()
   197→	if err != nil {
   198→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	// Determine branch
   202→	if branch == "" {
   203→		branch, err = GetCurrentBranch()
   204→		if err != nil {
   205→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to determine current branch: %w", err)
   206→		}
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	// Check if on default branch
   210→	if isDefault, _, defaultErr := IsOnDefaultBranch(); defaultErr == nil && isDefault {
   211→		fmt.Fprintln(errW, "Cannot create a trail on the default branch.")
   212→		fmt.Fprintln(errW, "Create or switch to a feature branch first.")
   213→		return NewSilentError(errors.New("cannot create trail on default branch"))
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	// Determine base branch
   217→	if base == "" {
   218→		base = strategy.GetDefaultBranchName(repo)
   219→		if base == "" {
   220→			base = defaultBaseBranch
   221→		}
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	// Check if trail already exists for this branch
   225→	store := trail.NewStore(repo)
   226→	existing, err := store.FindByBranch(branch)
   227→	if err == nil && existing != nil {
   228→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "Trail already exists for branch %q (ID: %s)\n", branch, existing.TrailID)
   229→		return nil
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	// Determine title
   233→	if title == "" {
   234→		defaultTitle := trail.HumanizeBranchName(branch)
   235→
   236→		// Interactive mode if flags not provided
   237→		if !hasFlag("title") {
   238→			var inputTitle string
   239→			form := NewAccessibleForm(
   240→				huh.NewGroup(
   241→					huh.NewInput().
   242→						Title("Trail title").
   243→						Placeholder(defaultTitle).
   244→						Value(&inputTitle),
   245→				),
   246→			)
   247→			if formErr := form.Run(); formErr != nil {
   248→				return fmt.Errorf("form cancelled: %w", formErr)
   249→			}
   250→			if inputTitle != "" {
   251→				title = inputTitle
   252→			} else {
   253→				title = defaultTitle
   254→			}
   255→		} else {
   256→			title = defaultTitle
   257→		}
   258→	}
   259→
   260→	// Determine status
   261→	var trailStatus trail.Status
   262→	if statusStr != "" {
   263→		trailStatus = trail.Status(statusStr)
   264→		if !trailStatus.IsValid() {
   265→			return fmt.Errorf("invalid status %q: valid values are %s", statusStr, formatValidStatuses())
   266→		}
   267→	} else {
   268→		trailStatus = trail.StatusDraft
   269→	}
   270→
   271→	// Generate trail ID
   272→	trailID, err := trail.GenerateID()
   273→	if err != nil {
   274→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate trail ID: %w", err)
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	// Get git author
   278→	authorName, _ := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
   279→
   280→	now := time.Now()
   281→	metadata := &trail.Metadata{
   282→		TrailID:     trailID,
   283→		Branch:      branch,
   284→		Base:        base,
   285→		Title:       title,
   286→		Description: description,
   287→		Status:      trailStatus,
   288→		Author:      authorName,
   289→		Assignees:   []string{},
   290→		Labels:      []string{},
   291→		CreatedAt:   now,
   292→		UpdatedAt:   now,
   293→	}
   294→
   295→	if err := store.Write(metadata, nil); err != nil {
   296→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create trail: %w", err)
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Created trail %q for branch %s (ID: %s)\n", title, branch, trailID)
   300→	return nil
   301→}

   303→func newTrailUpdateCmd() *cobra.Command {
   304→	var statusStr, title, description, branch string
   305→	var labelAdd, labelRemove []string
   306→
   307→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   308→		Use:   "update",
   309→		Short: "Update trail metadata",
   310→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   311→			return runTrailUpdate(cmd.OutOrStdout(), statusStr, title, description, branch, labelAdd, labelRemove)
   312→		},
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&statusStr, "status", "", "Update status")
   316→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&title, "title", "", "Update title")
   317→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&description, "description", "", "Update description")
   318→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&branch, "branch", "", "Branch to update trail for (defaults to current)")
   319→	cmd.Flags().StringSliceVar(&labelAdd, "add-label", nil, "Add label(s)")
   320→	cmd.Flags().StringSliceVar(&labelRemove, "remove-label", nil, "Remove label(s)")
   321→
   322→	return cmd
   323→}
   324→
   325→func runTrailUpdate(w io.Writer, statusStr, title, description, branch string, labelAdd, labelRemove []string) error {
   326→	repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()
   327→	if err != nil {
   328→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   329→	}
   330→
   331→	// Determine branch
   332→	if branch == "" {
   333→		branch, err = GetCurrentBranch()
   334→		if err != nil {
   335→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to determine current branch: %w", err)
   336→		}
   337→	}
   338→
   339→	store := trail.NewStore(repo)
   340→	metadata, err := store.FindByBranch(branch)
   341→	if err != nil {
   342→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to find trail: %w", err)
   343→	}
   344→	if metadata == nil {
   345→		return fmt.Errorf("no trail found for branch %q", branch)
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	// Validate status if provided
   349→	if statusStr != "" {
   350→		status := trail.Status(statusStr)
   351→		if !status.IsValid() {
   352→			return fmt.Errorf("invalid status %q: valid values are %s", statusStr, formatValidStatuses())
   353→		}
   354→	}
   355→
   356→	err = store.Update(metadata.TrailID, func(m *trail.Metadata) {
   357→		if statusStr != "" {
   358→			m.Status = trail.Status(statusStr)
   359→		}
   360→		if title != "" {
   361→			m.Title = title
   362→		}
   363→		if description != "" {
   364→			m.Description = description
   365→		}
   366→		for _, l := range labelAdd {
   367→			if !containsString(m.Labels, l) {
   368→				m.Labels = append(m.Labels, l)
   369→			}
   370→		}
   371→		for _, l := range labelRemove {
   372→			m.Labels = removeString(m.Labels, l)
   373→		}
   374→	})
   375→	if err != nil {
   376→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update trail: %w", err)
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Updated trail for branch %s\n", branch)
   380→	return nil
   381→}
   382→
   383→func newTrailEnableCmd() *cobra.Command {
   384→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   385→		Use:   "enable",
   386→		Short: "Enable automatic trail creation and pushing",
   387→		Long: `Enable trail auto-creation on session start and pushing the trails branch
   388→on git push. Trails are enabled by default.`,

   396→func runTrailEnable(w io.Writer) error {
   397→	s, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   398→	if err != nil {
   399→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   400→	}
   401→
   402→	if s.StrategyOptions != nil {
   403→		delete(s.StrategyOptions, "trails")
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(s); err != nil {
   407→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   408→	}
   409→
   410→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Trails enabled.")
   411→	return nil
   412→}
   413→
   414→func newTrailDisableCmd() *cobra.Command {
   415→	var useProjectSettings bool
   416→
   417→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   418→		Use:   "disable",
   419→		Short: "Disable automatic trail creation and pushing",
   420→		Long: `Disable trail auto-creation on session start and pushing the trails branch
   421→on git push. Existing trails are preserved.
   422→
   423→By default, writes to .entire/settings.local.json. Use --project to write to
   424→.entire/settings.json instead.`,
   425→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   426→			return runTrailDisable(cmd.OutOrStdout(), useProjectSettings)
   427→		},
   428→	}
   429→
   430→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&useProjectSettings, "project", false, "Write to .entire/settings.json instead of .entire/settings.local.json")
   431→	return cmd
   432→}
   433→
   434→func runTrailDisable(w io.Writer, useProjectSettings bool) error {
   435→	s, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   436→	if err != nil {
   437→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   438→	}
   439→
   440→	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
   441→		s.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]any)
   442→	}
   443→	s.StrategyOptions["trails"] = false
   444→
   445→	if useProjectSettings {
   446→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(s); err != nil {
   447→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   448→		}
   449→	} else {
   450→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(s); err != nil {
   451→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   452→		}
   453→	}
   454→
   455→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Trails disabled.")
   456→	return nil
   457→}
   458→
   459→// defaultBaseBranch is the fallback base branch name when it cannot be determined.
   460→const defaultBaseBranch = "main"
   461→
   462→// AutoCreateTrail creates a trail automatically for the current branch if one doesn't exist.
   463→// This is called during session start for non-main branches.
   464→func AutoCreateTrail(repo *git.Repository, branchName, baseBranch string) error {
   465→	store := trail.NewStore(repo)
   466→
   467→	existing, err := store.FindByBranch(branchName)
   468→	if err == nil && existing != nil {
   469→		return nil // Trail already exists
   470→	}
   471→
   472→	trailID, err := trail.GenerateID()
   473→	if err != nil {
   474→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate trail ID: %w", err)
   475→	}
   476→
   477→	authorName, _ := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
   478→	now := time.Now()
   479→
   480→	metadata := &trail.Metadata{
   481→		TrailID:   trailID,
   482→		Branch:    branchName,
   483→		Base:      baseBranch,
   484→		Title:     trail.HumanizeBranchName(branchName),
   485→		Status:    trail.StatusInProgress,
   486→		Author:    authorName,
   487→		Assignees: []string{},
   488→		Labels:    []string{},
   489→		CreatedAt: now,
   490→		UpdatedAt: now,
   491→	}
   492→
   493→	if err := store.Write(metadata, nil); err != nil {
   494→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create trail: %w", err)
   495→	}
   496→
   497→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created trail for branch: %s\n", branchName)
   498→	return nil
   499→}
   500→
   501→// hasFlag is a simple helper that checks os.Args for --flag presence.
   502→// Used to distinguish between "flag not provided" and "flag provided with empty value".
   503→func hasFlag(name string) bool {
   504→	for _, arg := range os.Args {
   505→		if arg == "--"+name || strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--"+name+"=") {
   506→			return true
   507→		}
   508→	}
   509→	return false
   510→}
   511→
   512→func truncate(s string, maxLen int) string {
   513→	if len(s) <= maxLen {
   514→		return s
   515→	}
   516→	if maxLen <= 3 {
   517→		return s[:maxLen]
   518→	}
   519→	return s[:maxLen-3] + "..."
   520→}
   521→
   522→func formatValidStatuses() string {
   523→	statuses := trail.ValidStatuses()
   524→	names := make([]string, len(statuses))
   525→	for i, s := range statuses {
   526→		names[i] = string(s)
   527→	}
   528→	return strings.Join(names, ", ")
   529→}
   530→
   531→func containsString(slice []string, s string) bool {
   532→	for _, v := range slice {
   533→		if v == s {
   534→			return true
   535→		}
   536→	}
   537→	return false
   538→}
   539→
   540→func removeString(slice []string, s string) []string {
   541→	var result []string
   542→	for _, v := range slice {
   543→		if v != s {
   544→			result = append(result, v)
   545→		}
   546→	}
   547→	return result
   548→}
   549→
```

```
     1→// lifecycle.go implements the generic lifecycle event dispatcher.
     2→// It routes normalized events from any agent to the appropriate framework actions.
     3→//
     4→// The dispatcher inverts the current flow from "agent handler calls framework functions"
     5→// to "framework dispatcher calls agent methods." Agents are passive data providers;
     6→// the dispatcher handles all orchestration: state transitions, strategy calls,
     7→// file change detection, metadata generation.
     8→package cli
     9→
    10→import (
    11→	"context"
    12→	"errors"
    13→	"fmt"
    14→	"log/slog"
    15→	"os"
    16→	"path/filepath"
    17→	"strings"
    18→	"time"
    19→
    20→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    21→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    22→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    23→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    24→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/settings"
    25→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    26→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
    27→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/validation"
    28→)
    29→
    30→// DispatchLifecycleEvent routes a normalized lifecycle event to the appropriate handler.
    31→// Returns nil if the event was handled successfully.
    32→func DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    33→	if ag == nil {
    34→		return errors.New("agent cannot be nil")
    35→	}
    36→	if event == nil {
    37→		return errors.New("event cannot be nil")
    38→	}
    39→
    40→	switch event.Type {
    41→	case agent.SessionStart:
    42→		return handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag, event)
    43→	case agent.TurnStart:
    44→		return handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag, event)
    45→	case agent.TurnEnd:
    46→		return handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag, event)
    47→	case agent.Compaction:
    48→		return handleLifecycleCompaction(ag, event)
    49→	case agent.SessionEnd:
    50→		return handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag, event)
    51→	case agent.SubagentStart:
    52→		return handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag, event)
    53→	case agent.SubagentEnd:
    54→		return handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag, event)
    55→	default:
    56→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown lifecycle event type: %d", event.Type)
    57→	}
    58→}
    59→
    60→// handleLifecycleSessionStart handles session start: shows banner, checks concurrent sessions,
    61→// fires state machine transition.
    62→func handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    63→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
    64→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-start",
    65→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
    66→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
    67→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
    68→	)
    69→
    70→	if event.SessionID == "" {
    71→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
    72→	}
    73→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(event.SessionID); err != nil {
    74→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	// Build informational message
    78→	message := "\n\nPowered by Entire:\n  This conversation will be linked to your next commit."
    79→
    80→	// Check for concurrent sessions and append count if any
    81→	strat := GetStrategy()
    82→	if concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker); ok {
    83→		if count, err := concurrentChecker.CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(event.SessionID); err == nil && count > 0 {
    84→			message += fmt.Sprintf("\n  %d other active conversation(s) in this workspace will also be included.\n  Use 'entire status' for more information.", count)
    85→		}
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	// Output informational message
    89→	if event.ResponseMessage != "" {
    90→		message = event.ResponseMessage
    91→	}
    92→	if err := outputHookResponse(message); err != nil {
    93→		return err
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	// Fire EventSessionStart for the current session (if state exists).
    97→	if state, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(event.SessionID); loadErr != nil {
    98→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state on start: %v\n", loadErr)
    99→	} else if state != nil {
   100→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   101→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   102→		}
   103→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); saveErr != nil {
   104→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state on start: %v\n", saveErr)
   105→		}
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	return nil
   109→}
   110→
   111→// handleLifecycleTurnStart handles turn start: captures pre-prompt state,
   112→// ensures strategy setup, initializes session.
   113→func handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   114→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   115→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-start",
   116→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   117→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   118→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   119→	)
   120→
   121→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   122→	if sessionID == "" {
   123→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
   124→	}
   125→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   126→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	// Capture pre-prompt state (including transcript position via TranscriptAnalyzer)
   130→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(ag, sessionID, event.SessionRef); err != nil {
   131→		return err
   132→	}
   133→
   134→	// Ensure strategy setup and initialize session
   135→	strat := GetStrategy()
   136→
   137→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   138→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   139→	}
   140→
   141→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
   142→		agentType := ag.Type()
   143→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(sessionID, agentType, event.SessionRef, event.Prompt); err != nil {
   144→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
   145→		}
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	// Auto-create trail for non-main branches
   149→	autoCreateTrailOnTurnStart()
   150→
   151→	return nil
   152→}
   153→
   154→// handleLifecycleTurnEnd handles turn end: validates transcript, extracts metadata,
   155→// detects file changes, saves step + checkpoint, transitions phase.
   156→//
   157→//nolint:maintidx // high complexity due to sequential orchestration of 8 steps (validation, extraction, file detection, filtering, token calc, step save, phase transition, cleanup) - splitting would obscure the flow
   158→func handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   159→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   160→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-end",
   161→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   162→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   163→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   164→	)
   165→
   166→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   167→	if sessionID == "" {
   168→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	transcriptRef := event.SessionRef
   172→	if transcriptRef == "" {
   173→		return errors.New("transcript file not specified")
   174→	}
   175→	if !fileExists(transcriptRef) {
   176→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found: %s", transcriptRef)
   177→	}
   178→
   179→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   180→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   181→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   182→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	// Create session metadata directory
   186→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   187→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   188→	if err != nil {
   189→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir
   190→	}
   191→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   192→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	// If agent implements TranscriptPreparer, wait for transcript to be ready
   196→	if preparer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptPreparer); ok {
   197→		if err := preparer.PrepareTranscript(transcriptRef); err != nil {
   198→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to prepare transcript: %v\n", err)
   199→		}
   200→	}
   201→
   202→	// Copy transcript to session directory
   203→	transcriptData, err := ag.ReadTranscript(transcriptRef)
   204→	if err != nil {
   205→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   206→	}
   207→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   208→	if err := os.WriteFile(logFile, transcriptData, 0o600); err != nil {
   209→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   210→	}
   211→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   212→
   213→	// Copy export JSON if it exists alongside the transcript (written by OpenCode plugin).
   214→	// This is used by `opencode import` during resume/rewind to restore the session
   215→	// into OpenCode's SQLite database with the original session ID.
   216→	exportSrc := strings.TrimSuffix(transcriptRef, filepath.Ext(transcriptRef)) + ".export.json"
   217→	if exportData, readErr := os.ReadFile(exportSrc); readErr == nil { //nolint:gosec // Path derived from agent hook
   218→		exportDest := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ExportDataFileName)
   219→		if writeErr := os.WriteFile(exportDest, exportData, 0o600); writeErr != nil {
   220→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to write export data: %v\n", writeErr)
   221→		}
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on TurnStart)
   225→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   226→	if err != nil {
   227→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   228→	}
   229→
   230→	// Determine transcript offset
   231→	transcriptOffset := resolveTranscriptOffset(preState, sessionID)
   232→
   233→	// Extract metadata via agent interface (prompts, summary, modified files)
   234→	var allPrompts []string
   235→	var summary string
   236→	var modifiedFiles []string
   237→	var newTranscriptPosition int
   238→
   239→	// Compute subagents directory for agents that support subagent extraction.
   240→	// Subagent transcripts live in <transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/
   241→	subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptRef), event.SessionID, "subagents")
   242→
   243→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   244→		// Extract prompts
   245→		if prompts, promptErr := analyzer.ExtractPrompts(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); promptErr != nil {
   246→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract prompts: %v\n", promptErr)
   247→		} else {
   248→			allPrompts = prompts
   249→		}
   250→
   251→		// Extract summary
   252→		if s, sumErr := analyzer.ExtractSummary(transcriptRef); sumErr != nil {
   253→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract summary: %v\n", sumErr)
   254→		} else {
   255→			summary = s
   256→		}
   257→
   258→		// Extract modified files - prefer SubagentAwareExtractor if available to include subagent files
   259→		if subagentExtractor, subOk := ag.(agent.SubagentAwareExtractor); subOk {
   260→			if files, fileErr := subagentExtractor.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset, subagentsDir); fileErr != nil {
   261→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files (with subagents): %v\n", fileErr)
   262→			} else {
   263→				modifiedFiles = files
   264→			}
   265→			// Get position from basic analyzer
   266→			if _, pos, posErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); posErr == nil {
   267→				newTranscriptPosition = pos
   268→			}
   269→		} else {
   270→			// Fall back to basic extraction (main transcript only)
   271→			if files, pos, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); fileErr != nil {
   272→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files: %v\n", fileErr)
   273→			} else {
   274→				modifiedFiles = files
   275→				newTranscriptPosition = pos
   276→			}
   277→		}
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	// Write prompts file
   281→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   282→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   283→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   284→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   285→	}
   286→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   287→
   288→	// Write summary file
   289→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   290→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   291→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   292→	}
   293→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   294→
   295→	// Generate commit message from last prompt
   296→	lastPrompt := ""
   297→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   298→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   299→	}
   300→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   301→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   302→
   303→	// Get repo root for path normalization
   304→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   305→	if err != nil {
   306→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   307→	}
   308→
   309→	var preUntrackedFiles []string
   310→	if preState != nil {
   311→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   312→		preUntrackedFiles = preState.PreUntrackedFiles()
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	// Detect file changes via git status
   316→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preUntrackedFiles)
   317→	if err != nil {
   318→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   319→	}
   320→
   321→	// Filter and normalize all paths
   322→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   323→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   324→	if changes != nil {
   325→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   326→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	// Filter transcript-extracted files to exclude files already committed to HEAD.
   330→	// When an agent commits files mid-turn, those files are condensed by PostCommit
   331→	// and should not be re-added to FilesTouched by SaveStep. A file is "committed"
   332→	// if it exists in HEAD with the same content as the working tree.
   333→	relModifiedFiles = filterToUncommittedFiles(relModifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   334→
   335→	// Check if there are any changes
   336→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   337→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   338→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   339→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   340→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   341→		if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   342→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   343→		}
   344→		return nil
   345→	}
   346→
   347→	// Log file changes
   348→	logFileChanges(relModifiedFiles, relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles)
   349→
   350→	// Create context file
   351→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   352→	if err := createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, allPrompts, summary); err != nil {
   353→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   354→	}
   355→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   356→
   357→	// Get git author
   358→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   359→	if err != nil {
   360→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   361→	}
   362→
   363→	// Get strategy and agent type
   364→	strat := GetStrategy()
   365→	agentType := ag.Type()
   366→
   367→	// Get transcript position/identifier from pre-prompt state
   368→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   369→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   370→	if preState != nil {
   371→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   372→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.TranscriptOffset
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	// Calculate token usage - prefer SubagentAwareExtractor to include subagent tokens
   376→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   377→	if subagentExtractor, ok := ag.(agent.SubagentAwareExtractor); ok {
   378→		usage, tokenErr := subagentExtractor.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   379→		if tokenErr != nil {
   380→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage (with subagents): %v\n", tokenErr)
   381→		} else {
   382→			tokenUsage = usage
   383→		}
   384→	} else if calculator, ok := ag.(agent.TokenCalculator); ok {
   385→		// Fall back to basic token calculation (main transcript only)
   386→		usage, tokenErr := calculator.CalculateTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart)
   387→		if tokenErr != nil {
   388→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", tokenErr)
   389→		} else {
   390→			tokenUsage = usage
   391→		}
   392→	}
   393→
   394→	// Build fully-populated step context and delegate to strategy
   395→	ctx := strategy.StepContext{
   396→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   397→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   398→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   399→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   400→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   401→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   402→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   403→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptRef,
   404→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   405→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   406→		AgentType:                agentType,
   407→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   408→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   409→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   410→	}
   411→
   412→	if err := strat.SaveStep(ctx); err != nil {
   413→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save step: %w", err)
   414→	}
   415→
   416→	// Update session state transcript position for auto-commit strategy
   417→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit && newTranscriptPosition > 0 {
   418→		updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID, newTranscriptPosition)
   419→	}
   420→
   421→	// Transition session phase and cleanup
   422→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   423→	if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   424→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   425→	}
   426→
   427→	return nil
   428→}
   429→
   430→// handleLifecycleCompaction handles context compaction: saves current progress
   431→// but stays in ACTIVE phase (unlike TurnEnd which transitions to IDLE).
   432→// Also resets the transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated.
   433→func handleLifecycleCompaction(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   434→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   435→	logging.Info(logCtx, "compaction",
   436→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   437→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   438→	)
   439→
   440→	// Fire EventCompaction to trigger ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched (stays in ACTIVE)
   441→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   442→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   443→	if loadErr != nil {
   444→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for compaction: %v\n", loadErr)
   445→	}
   446→	if sessionState != nil {
   447→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(sessionState, session.EventCompaction, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   448→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: compaction transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   449→		}
   450→
   451→		// Reset transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated/reorganized
   452→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0
   453→
   454→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); saveErr != nil {
   455→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save session state after compaction: %v\n", saveErr)
   456→		}
   457→	}
   458→
   459→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Context compaction: transcript offset reset\n")
   460→	return nil
   461→}
   462→
   463→// handleLifecycleSessionEnd handles session end: marks the session as ended.
   464→func handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   465→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   466→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   467→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   468→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   469→	)
   470→
   471→	if event.SessionID == "" {
   472→		return nil // No session to update
   473→	}
   474→
   475→	if err := markSessionEnded(event.SessionID); err != nil {
   476→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   477→	}
   478→	return nil
   479→}
   480→
   481→// handleLifecycleSubagentStart handles subagent start: captures pre-task state.
   482→func handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   483→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   484→	logging.Info(logCtx, "subagent-start",
   485→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   486→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   487→		slog.String("tool_use_id", event.ToolUseID),
   488→	)
   489→
   490→	// Log context
   491→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Subagent started\n")
   492→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", event.SessionID)
   493→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", event.ToolUseID)
   494→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Transcript: %s\n", event.SessionRef)
   495→
   496→	// Capture pre-task state
   497→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(event.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   498→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   499→	}
   500→
   501→	return nil
   502→}
   503→
   504→// handleLifecycleSubagentEnd handles subagent end: detects changes, saves task checkpoint.
   505→func handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   506→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   507→	logging.Info(logCtx, "subagent-end",
   508→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   509→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   510→		slog.String("tool_use_id", event.ToolUseID),
   511→		slog.String("subagent_id", event.SubagentID),
   512→	)
   513→
   514→	// Extract subagent type and description from tool input
   515→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(event.ToolInput)
   516→
   517→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   518→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(event.SessionRef)
   519→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   520→	if event.SubagentID != "" {
   521→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, event.SubagentID)
   522→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   523→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   524→		}
   525→	}
   526→
   527→	// Log context
   528→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Subagent completed\n")
   529→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", event.SessionID)
   530→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", event.ToolUseID)
   531→	if event.SubagentID != "" {
   532→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Agent ID: %s\n", event.SubagentID)
   533→	}
   534→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   535→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Subagent Transcript: %s\n", subagentTranscriptPath)
   536→	}
   537→
   538→	// Extract modified files from subagent transcript
   539→	var modifiedFiles []string
   540→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   541→		transcriptToScan := event.SessionRef
   542→		if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   543→			transcriptToScan = subagentTranscriptPath
   544→		}
   545→		if files, _, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptToScan, 0); fileErr != nil {
   546→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files from subagent: %v\n", fileErr)
   547→		} else {
   548→			modifiedFiles = files
   549→		}
   550→	}
   551→
   552→	// Load pre-task state and detect file changes
   553→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID)
   554→	if err != nil {
   555→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   556→	}
   557→	var preUntrackedFiles []string
   558→	if preState != nil {
   559→		preUntrackedFiles = preState.PreUntrackedFiles()
   560→	}
   561→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preUntrackedFiles)
   562→	if err != nil {
   563→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   564→	}
   565→
   566→	// Get repo root and normalize paths
   567→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   568→	if err != nil {
   569→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   570→	}
   571→
   572→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   573→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   574→	if changes != nil {
   575→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   576→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   577→	}
   578→
   579→	// If no changes, skip
   580→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   581→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   582→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   583→		return nil
   584→	}
   585→
   586→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort)
   587→	var checkpointUUID string
   588→	// Use the existing CLI-level checkpoint UUID finder
   589→	mainLines, _, _ := parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(event.SessionRef) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort
   590→	if mainLines != nil {
   591→		checkpointUUID, _ = FindCheckpointUUID(mainLines, event.ToolUseID)
   592→	}
   593→
   594→	// Get git author
   595→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   596→	if err != nil {
   597→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   598→	}
   599→
   600→	// Build task checkpoint context
   601→	strat := GetStrategy()
   602→	agentType := ag.Type()
   603→
   604→	ctx := strategy.TaskStepContext{
   605→		SessionID:              event.SessionID,
   606→		ToolUseID:              event.ToolUseID,
   607→		AgentID:                event.SubagentID,
   608→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   609→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   610→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   611→		TranscriptPath:         event.SessionRef,
   612→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   613→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   614→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   615→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   616→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   617→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   618→		AgentType:              agentType,
   619→	}
   620→
   621→	if err := strat.SaveTaskStep(ctx); err != nil {
   622→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task step: %w", err)
   623→	}
   624→
   625→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   626→	return nil
   627→}
   628→
   629→// --- Helper functions ---
   630→
   631→// resolveTranscriptOffset determines the transcript offset to use for parsing.
   632→// Prefers pre-prompt state, falls back to session state.
   633→func resolveTranscriptOffset(preState *PrePromptState, sessionID string) int {
   634→	if preState != nil && preState.TranscriptOffset > 0 {
   635→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\n", preState.TranscriptOffset)
   636→		return preState.TranscriptOffset
   637→	}
   638→
   639→	// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   640→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   641→	if loadErr != nil {
   642→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   643→		return 0
   644→	}
   645→	if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   646→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\n", sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   647→		return sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   648→	}
   649→
   650→	return 0
   651→}
   652→
   653→// updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition updates the session state with the new transcript position
   654→// for the auto-commit strategy.
   655→func updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID string, newPosition int) {
   656→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   657→	if loadErr != nil {
   658→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   659→		return
   660→	}
   661→	if sessionState == nil {
   662→		sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   663→			SessionID: sessionID,
   664→		}
   665→	}
   666→	sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = newPosition
   667→	sessionState.StepCount++
   668→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   669→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   670→	} else {
   671→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   672→			newPosition, sessionState.StepCount)
   673→	}
   674→}
   675→
   676→// createContextFile creates a context.md file for the session checkpoint.
   677→// This is a unified version that works for all agents.
   678→func createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID string, prompts []string, summary string) error {
   679→	var sb strings.Builder
   680→
   681→	sb.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   682→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Session ID: %s\n", sessionID)
   683→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Commit Message: %s\n\n", commitMessage)
   684→
   685→	if len(prompts) > 0 {
   686→		sb.WriteString("## Prompts\n\n")
   687→		for i, p := range prompts {
   688→			fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "### Prompt %d\n\n%s\n\n", i+1, p)
   689→		}
   690→	}
   691→
   692→	if summary != "" {
   693→		sb.WriteString("## Summary\n\n")
   694→		sb.WriteString(summary)
   695→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   696→	}
   697→
   698→	if err := os.WriteFile(contextFile, []byte(sb.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   699→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write context file: %w", err)
   700→	}
   701→	return nil
   702→}
   703→
   704→// parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID is a thin wrapper around transcript parsing for checkpoint UUID lookup.
   705→// Returns parsed transcript lines for use with FindCheckpointUUID.
   706→func parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(transcriptPath string) ([]transcriptLine, int, error) {
   707→	lines, total, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   708→	if err != nil {
   709→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("parsing transcript for checkpoint UUID: %w", err)
   710→	}
   711→	return lines, total, nil
   712→}
   713→
   714→// transitionSessionTurnEnd transitions the session phase to IDLE and dispatches turn-end actions.
   715→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   716→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   717→	if loadErr != nil {
   718→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   719→		return
   720→	}
   721→	if turnState == nil {
   722→		return
   723→	}
   724→	if err := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); err != nil {
   725→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end transition failed: %v\n", err)
   726→	}
   727→
   728→	// Always dispatch to strategy for turn-end handling. The strategy reads
   729→	// work items from state (e.g. TurnCheckpointIDs), not the action list.
   730→	strat := GetStrategy()
   731→	if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   732→		if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState); err != nil {
   733→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   734→		}
   735→	}
   736→
   737→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   738→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   739→	}
   740→}
   741→
   742→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   743→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   744→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   745→	if err != nil {
   746→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   747→	}
   748→	if state == nil {
   749→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   750→	}
   751→
   752→	if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   753→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session stop transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   754→	}
   755→
   756→	now := time.Now()
   757→	state.EndedAt = &now
   758→
   759→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   760→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   761→	}
   762→	return nil
   763→}
   764→
   765→// autoCreateTrailOnTurnStart creates a trail if on a non-main branch without one.
   766→// Non-blocking: logs errors but doesn't fail the session.
   767→func autoCreateTrailOnTurnStart() {
   768→	if settings.IsTrailsDisabled() {
   769→		return
   770→	}
   771→
   772→	isDefault, branchName, err := IsOnDefaultBranch()
   773→	if err != nil || isDefault || branchName == "" {
   774→		return
   775→	}
   776→
   777→	repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()
   778→	if err != nil {
   779→		return
   780→	}
   781→
   782→	baseBranch := strategy.GetDefaultBranchName(repo)
   783→	if baseBranch == "" {
   784→		baseBranch = defaultBaseBranch
   785→	}
   786→
   787→	if err := AutoCreateTrail(repo, branchName, baseBranch); err != nil {
   788→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to auto-create trail: %v\n", err)
   789→	}
   790→}
   791→
   792→// logFileChanges logs the files modified, created, and deleted during a session.
   793→func logFileChanges(modified, newFiles, deleted []string) {
   794→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(modified))
   795→	for _, file := range modified {
   796→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   797→	}
   798→	if len(newFiles) > 0 {
   799→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(newFiles))
   800→		for _, file := range newFiles {
   801→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   802→		}
   803→	}
   804→	if len(deleted) > 0 {
   805→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(deleted))
   806→		for _, file := range deleted {
   807→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   808→		}
   809→	}
   810→}
   811→
```

```
    12→const (
    13→	// logsOnlyScanLimit is the maximum number of commits to scan for logs-only points.
    14→	logsOnlyScanLimit = 50
    15→
    16→	// maxFirstPromptRunes is the maximum rune length for FirstPrompt stored in session state.
    17→	maxFirstPromptRunes = 100
    18→)
    19→
    20→// truncatePromptForStorage collapses whitespace and truncates a user prompt
    21→// for storage in FirstPrompt fields.
    22→func truncatePromptForStorage(prompt string) string {
    23→	return stringutil.TruncateRunes(stringutil.CollapseWhitespace(prompt), maxFirstPromptRunes, "...")
    24→}
    25→
    26→// SessionState is an alias for session.State.
    27→// Previously this was a separate struct with manual conversion functions.
    28→type SessionState = session.State
    29→
    30→// PromptAttribution is an alias for session.PromptAttribution.
    31→type PromptAttribution = session.PromptAttribution
    32→
    33→// CheckpointInfo represents checkpoint metadata stored on the sessions branch.
    34→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpoint_id[:2]>/<checkpoint_id[2:]>/
    35→type CheckpointInfo struct {
    36→	CheckpointID     id.CheckpointID `json:"checkpoint_id"` // 12-hex-char from Entire-Checkpoint trailer, used as directory path
    37→	SessionID        string          `json:"session_id"`
    38→	CreatedAt        time.Time       `json:"created_at"`
    39→	CheckpointsCount int             `json:"checkpoints_count"`
    40→	FilesTouched     []string        `json:"files_touched"`
    41→	Agent            agent.AgentType `json:"agent,omitempty"` // Human-readable agent name (e.g., "Claude Code")
    42→	IsTask           bool            `json:"is_task,omitempty"`
    43→	ToolUseID        string          `json:"tool_use_id,omitempty"`
    44→	SessionCount     int             `json:"session_count,omitempty"` // Number of sessions (1 if omitted)
    45→	SessionIDs       []string        `json:"session_ids,omitempty"`   // All session IDs in this checkpoint
    46→}
    47→
    48→// CondenseResult contains the result of a session condensation operation.
    49→type CondenseResult struct {
    50→	CheckpointID         id.CheckpointID // 12-hex-char from Entire-Checkpoint trailer, used as directory path
    51→	SessionID            string
    52→	CheckpointsCount     int
    53→	FilesTouched         []string
    54→	TotalTranscriptLines int // Total lines in transcript after this condensation
    55→}
    56→
    57→// ExtractedSessionData contains data extracted from a shadow branch.

    61→	Prompts             []string // All user prompts from this portion

    64→	TokenUsage          *agent.TokenUsage // Token usage calculated from transcript (since CheckpointTranscriptStart)
```
