  1031→	// For PrepareCommitMsg context, we verify staged files overlap with session's files
  1032→	// using content-aware matching to detect reverted files.
  1033→	// For PostCommit context, getStagedFiles() is empty (files already committed),
  1034→	// so we return true and let the caller do the overlap check via filesOverlapWithContent.
  1035→
  1036→	hasTranscriptGrowth := transcriptLines > state.CheckpointTranscriptStart
  1037→	hasUncommittedFiles := len(state.FilesTouched) > 0
  1038→
  1039→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: transcript check",
  1040→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1041→		slog.Int("transcript_lines", transcriptLines),
  1042→		slog.Int("checkpoint_transcript_start", state.CheckpointTranscriptStart),
  1043→		slog.Bool("has_transcript_growth", hasTranscriptGrowth),
  1044→		slog.Bool("has_uncommitted_files", hasUncommittedFiles),
  1045→	)
  1046→
  1047→	if !hasTranscriptGrowth && !hasUncommittedFiles {
  1048→		return false, nil // No new content and no carry-forward files
  1049→	}
  1050→
  1051→	// Check if staged files overlap with session's files with content-aware matching.
  1052→	// This is primarily for PrepareCommitMsg; in PostCommit, stagedFiles is empty.
  1053→	stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
  1054→	if len(stagedFiles) > 0 {
  1055→		result := stagedFilesOverlapWithContent(repo, tree, stagedFiles, state.FilesTouched)
  1056→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: staged files overlap check",
  1057→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1058→			slog.Int("staged_files", len(stagedFiles)),
  1059→			slog.Bool("result", result),
  1060→		)
  1061→		return result, nil
  1062→	}
  1063→
  1064→	// No staged files - either PostCommit context or edge case.
  1065→	// Return transcript growth status. For PostCommit with hasTranscriptFile=true,
  1066→	// if there's no transcript growth, the session hasn't done new work since last checkpoint.
  1067→	// (Carry-forward creates a shadow branch WITHOUT transcript, handled in the block above.)
  1068→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: no staged files, returning transcript growth",
  1069→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1070→		slog.Bool("has_transcript_growth", hasTranscriptGrowth),
  1071→		slog.Bool("has_uncommitted_files", hasUncommittedFiles),
  1072→	)
  1073→	return hasTranscriptGrowth, nil
  1074→}
  1075→
  1076→// sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript checks if a session has new content
  1077→// by examining the live transcript file. This is used when no shadow branch exists
  1078→// (i.e., no Stop has happened yet) but the agent may have done work.
  1079→//
  1080→// Returns true if:
  1081→//  1. The transcript has grown since the last condensation, AND
  1082→//  2. The new transcript portion contains file modifications, AND
  1083→//  3. At least one modified file overlaps with the currently staged files
  1084→//
  1085→// The overlap check ensures we don't add checkpoint trailers to commits that are
  1086→// unrelated to the agent's recent changes.
  1087→//
  1088→// This handles the scenario where the agent commits mid-session before Stop.
  1089→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
  1090→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1091→
  1092→	modifiedFiles, ok := s.extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state)
  1093→	if !ok || len(modifiedFiles) == 0 {
  1094→		return false, nil
  1095→	}
  1096→
  1097→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: found file modifications",
  1098→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1099→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(modifiedFiles)),
  1100→	)
  1101→
  1102→	// Check if any modified files overlap with currently staged files
  1103→	// This ensures we only add checkpoint trailers to commits that include
  1104→	// files the agent actually modified
  1105→	stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
  1106→
  1107→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: comparing staged vs modified",
  1108→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1109→		slog.Int("staged_files", len(stagedFiles)),
  1110→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(modifiedFiles)),
  1111→	)
  1112→
  1113→	if !hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, modifiedFiles) {
  1114→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: no overlap between staged and modified files",
  1115→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1116→		)
  1117→		return false, nil // No overlap - staged files are unrelated to agent's work
  1118→	}
  1119→
  1120→	return true, nil
  1121→}
  1122→
  1123→// extractFilesFromLiveTranscript extracts modified file paths from the live transcript.
  1124→// Returns empty slice if extraction fails (fail-open behavior for hooks).
  1125→// Extracts files from the transcript starting at CheckpointTranscriptStart, which gives
  1126→// files touched since the last condensation — used for carry-forward computation.
  1127→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractFilesFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState) []string {
  1128→	return s.extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
  1129→}
  1130→
  1131→// extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript extracts modified files from the live
  1132→// transcript that are NEW since the last condensation. Returns the normalized file list
  1133→// and whether the extraction succeeded. Used by sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript
  1134→// to detect agent work.
  1135→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState) ([]string, bool) {
  1136→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1137→
  1138→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" || state.AgentType == "" {
  1139→		return nil, false
  1140→	}
  1141→
  1142→	ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(state.AgentType)
  1143→	if err != nil {
  1144→		return nil, false
  1145→	}
  1146→
  1147→	analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer)
  1148→	if !ok {
  1149→		return nil, false
  1150→	}
  1151→
  1152→	// Check if transcript has grown since last condensation
  1153→	currentPos, err := analyzer.GetTranscriptPosition(state.TranscriptPath)
  1154→	if err != nil {
  1155→		return nil, false
  1156→	}
  1157→	if currentPos <= state.CheckpointTranscriptStart {
  1158→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: no new content",
  1159→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1160→			slog.Int("current_pos", currentPos),
  1161→			slog.Int("start_offset", state.CheckpointTranscriptStart),
  1162→		)
  1163→		return nil, true // No new content, but extraction succeeded
  1164→	}
  1165→
  1166→	return s.extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart), true
  1167→}
  1168→
  1169→// extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript extracts modified files from the live transcript
  1170→// (including subagent transcripts) starting from the given offset, and normalizes them
  1171→// to repo-relative paths. Returns the normalized file list.
  1172→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState, offset int) []string {
  1173→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1174→
  1175→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" || state.AgentType == "" {
  1176→		return nil
  1177→	}
  1178→
  1179→	ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(state.AgentType)
  1180→	if err != nil {
  1181→		return nil
  1182→	}
  1183→
  1184→	analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer)
  1185→	if !ok {
  1186→		return nil
  1187→	}
  1188→
  1189→	var modifiedFiles []string
  1190→
  1191→	// For Claude Code, use ExtractAllModifiedFiles which parses the main transcript
  1192→	// AND subagent transcripts in a single pass, avoiding redundant parsing.
  1193→	if state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode {
  1194→		subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(state.TranscriptPath), state.SessionID, "subagents")
  1195→		allFiles, extractErr := claudecode.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(state.TranscriptPath, offset, subagentsDir)
  1196→		if extractErr != nil {
  1197→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript: extraction failed",
  1198→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1199→				slog.String("error", extractErr.Error()),
  1200→			)
  1201→		} else {
  1202→			modifiedFiles = allFiles
  1203→		}
  1204→	} else {
  1205→		files, _, err := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(state.TranscriptPath, offset)
  1206→		if err != nil {
  1207→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript: main transcript extraction failed",
  1208→				slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
  1209→				slog.Any("error", err),
  1210→			)
  1211→		} else {
  1212→			modifiedFiles = files
  1213→		}
  1214→	}
  1215→
  1216→	if len(modifiedFiles) == 0 {
  1217→		return nil
  1218→	}
  1219→
  1220→	// Normalize to repo-relative paths.
  1221→	// Transcript tool_use entries contain absolute paths (e.g., /Users/alex/project/src/main.go)
  1222→	// but getStagedFiles/committedFiles use repo-relative paths (e.g., src/main.go).
  1223→	basePath := state.WorktreePath
  1224→	if basePath == "" {
  1225→		if wp, wpErr := GetWorktreePath(); wpErr == nil {
  1226→			basePath = wp
  1227→		}
  1228→	}
  1229→	if basePath != "" {
  1230→		normalized := make([]string, 0, len(modifiedFiles))
  1231→		for _, f := range modifiedFiles {
  1232→			if rel := paths.ToRelativePath(f, basePath); rel != "" {
  1233→				normalized = append(normalized, rel)
  1234→			} else {
  1235→				normalized = append(normalized, f)
  1236→			}
  1237→		}
  1238→		modifiedFiles = normalized
  1239→	}
  1240→
  1241→	return modifiedFiles
  1242→}
  1243→
  1244→// addTrailerForAgentCommit handles the fast path when an agent is committing
  1245→// (ACTIVE session + no TTY). Generates a checkpoint ID and adds the trailer
  1246→// directly, bypassing content detection and interactive prompts.
  1247→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) addTrailerForAgentCommit(logCtx context.Context, commitMsgFile string, state *SessionState, source string) error {
  1248→	cpID, err := id.Generate()
  1249→	if err != nil {
  1250→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
  1251→	}
  1252→
  1253→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
  1254→	if err != nil {
  1255→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
  1256→	}
  1257→
  1258→	message := string(content)
  1259→
  1260→	// Don't add if trailer already exists
  1261→	if _, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
  1262→		return nil
  1263→	}
  1264→
  1265→	message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, cpID)
  1266→
  1267→	logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: agent commit trailer added",
  1268→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
  1269→		slog.String("source", source),
  1270→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
  1271→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1272→	)
  1273→
  1274→	if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
  1275→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
  1276→	}
  1277→	return nil
  1278→}
  1279→
  1280→// addCheckpointTrailer adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer to a commit message.
  1281→// Handles proper trailer formatting (blank line before trailers if needed).
  1282→func addCheckpointTrailer(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) string {
  1283→	trailer := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
  1284→
  1285→	// If message already ends with trailers (lines starting with key:), just append
  1286→	// Otherwise, add a blank line first
  1287→	lines := strings.Split(strings.TrimRight(message, "\n"), "\n")
  1288→
  1289→	// Check if the message already ends with a trailer paragraph.
  1290→	// Git trailers must be in a separate paragraph (preceded by a blank line).
  1291→	// A single-paragraph message (e.g., just a subject line) cannot have trailers,
  1292→	// even if the subject contains ": " (like conventional commits: "docs: Add foo").
  1293→	//
  1294→	// Scan from the bottom: find the last paragraph of non-comment content,
  1295→	// then check if it looks like trailers AND has a blank line above it.
  1296→	hasTrailers := false
  1297→	i := len(lines) - 1
  1298→
  1299→	// Skip trailing comment lines
  1300→	for i >= 0 && strings.HasPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(lines[i]), "#") {
  1301→		i--
  1302→	}
  1303→
  1304→	// Check if the last non-comment line looks like a trailer
  1305→	if i >= 0 {
  1306→		line := strings.TrimSpace(lines[i])
  1307→		if line != "" && strings.Contains(line, ": ") {
  1308→			// Found a trailer-like line. Now scan upward past the trailer block
  1309→			// to verify there's a blank line (paragraph separator) above it.
  1310→			for i > 0 {
  1311→				i--
  1312→				above := strings.TrimSpace(lines[i])
  1313→				if strings.HasPrefix(above, "#") {
  1314→					continue
  1315→				}
  1316→				if above == "" {
  1317→					// Blank line found above trailer block — real trailers
  1318→					hasTrailers = true
  1319→					break
  1320→				}
  1321→				if !strings.Contains(above, ": ") {
  1322→					// Non-trailer, non-blank line — this is message body, not trailers
  1323→					break
  1324→				}
  1325→				// Another trailer-like line, keep scanning upward
  1326→			}
  1327→		}
  1328→	}
  1329→
  1330→	if hasTrailers {
  1331→		// Append trailer directly
  1332→		return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n" + trailer + "\n"
  1333→	}
  1334→
  1335→	// Add blank line before trailer
  1336→	return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n"
  1337→}
  1338→
  1339→// addCheckpointTrailerWithComment adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer with an explanatory comment.
  1340→// The trailer is placed above the git comment block but below the user's message area,
  1341→// with a comment explaining that the user can remove it if they don't want to link the commit
  1342→// to the agent session. If prompt is non-empty, it's shown as context.
  1343→func addCheckpointTrailerWithComment(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, agentName, prompt string) string {
  1344→	trailer := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
  1345→	commentLines := []string{
  1346→		"# Remove the Entire-Checkpoint trailer above if you don't want to link this commit to " + agentName + " session context.",
  1347→	}
  1348→	if prompt != "" {
  1349→		commentLines = append(commentLines, "# Last Prompt: "+prompt)
  1350→	}
  1351→	commentLines = append(commentLines, "# The trailer will be added to your next commit based on this branch.")
  1352→	comment := strings.Join(commentLines, "\n")
  1353→
  1354→	lines := strings.Split(message, "\n")
  1355→
  1356→	// Find where the git comment block starts (first # line)
  1357→	commentStart := -1
  1358→	for i, line := range lines {
  1359→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") {
  1360→			commentStart = i
  1361→			break
  1362→		}
  1363→	}
  1364→
  1365→	if commentStart == -1 {
  1366→		// No git comments, append trailer at the end
  1367→		return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n"
  1368→	}
  1369→
  1370→	// Split into user content and git comments
  1371→	userContent := strings.Join(lines[:commentStart], "\n")
  1372→	gitComments := strings.Join(lines[commentStart:], "\n")
  1373→
  1374→	// Build result: user content, blank line, trailer, comment, blank line, git comments
  1375→	userContent = strings.TrimRight(userContent, "\n")
  1376→	if userContent == "" {
  1377→		// No user content yet - leave space for them to type, then trailer
  1378→		// Two newlines: first for user's message line, second for blank separator
  1379→		return "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n\n" + gitComments
  1380→	}
  1381→	return userContent + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n\n" + gitComments
  1382→}
  1383→
  1384→// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session or updates an existing one.
  1385→// This implements the optional SessionInitializer interface.
  1386→// Called during UserPromptSubmit to allow git hooks to detect active sessions.
  1387→//
  1388→// If the session already exists and HEAD has moved (e.g., user committed), updates
  1389→// BaseCommit to the new HEAD so future checkpoints go to the correct shadow branch.
  1390→//
  1391→// If there's an existing shadow branch with commits from a different session ID,
  1392→// returns a SessionIDConflictError to prevent orphaning existing session work.
  1393→//
  1394→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
  1395→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
  1396→// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as FirstPrompt for display).
  1397→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) InitializeSession(sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) error {
  1398→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1399→	if err != nil {
  1400→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
  1401→	}
  1402→
  1403→	// Check if session already exists
  1404→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1405→	if err != nil {
  1406→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check session state: %w", err)
  1407→	}
  1408→
  1409→	if state != nil && state.BaseCommit != "" {
  1410→		// Session is fully initialized — apply phase transition for TurnStart.
  1411→		if transErr := TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventTurnStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
  1412→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: turn start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
  1413→		}
  1414→
  1415→		// Generate a new TurnID for each turn (correlates carry-forward checkpoints)
  1416→		turnID, err := id.Generate()
  1417→		if err != nil {
  1418→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate turn ID: %w", err)
  1419→		}
  1420→		state.TurnID = turnID.String()
  1421→
  1422→		// Backfill AgentType if empty or set to the generic default "Agent"
  1423→		if !isSpecificAgentType(state.AgentType) && agentType != "" {
  1424→			state.AgentType = agentType
  1425→		}
  1426→
  1427→		// Backfill FirstPrompt if empty (for sessions created before the first_prompt field was added)
  1428→		if state.FirstPrompt == "" && userPrompt != "" {
  1429→			state.FirstPrompt = truncatePromptForStorage(userPrompt)
  1430→		}
  1431→
  1432→		// Update transcript path if provided (may change on session resume)
  1433→		if transcriptPath != "" && state.TranscriptPath != transcriptPath {
  1434→			state.TranscriptPath = transcriptPath
  1435→		}
  1436→
  1437→		// Clear checkpoint IDs on every new prompt.
  1438→		// LastCheckpointID is set during PostCommit, cleared at new prompt.
  1439→		// TurnCheckpointIDs tracks mid-turn checkpoints for stop-time finalization.
  1440→		state.LastCheckpointID = ""
  1441→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1442→
  1443→		// Calculate attribution at prompt start (BEFORE agent makes any changes)
  1444→		// This captures user edits since the last checkpoint (or base commit for first prompt).
  1445→		// IMPORTANT: Always calculate attribution, even for the first checkpoint, to capture
  1446→		// user edits made before the first prompt. The inner CalculatePromptAttribution handles
  1447→		// nil lastCheckpointTree by falling back to baseTree.
  1448→		promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(repo, state)
  1449→		state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
  1450→
  1451→		// Check if HEAD has moved (user pulled/rebased or committed)
  1452→		// migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded handles renaming the shadow branch and updating state.BaseCommit
  1453→		if _, err := s.migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded(repo, state); err != nil {
  1454→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to check/migrate shadow branch: %w", err)
  1455→		}
  1456→
  1457→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
  1458→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to update session state: %w", err)
  1459→		}
  1460→		return nil
  1461→	}
  1462→	// If state exists but BaseCommit is empty, it's a partial state from concurrent warning
  1463→	// Continue below to properly initialize it
  1464→
  1465→	// Initialize new session
  1466→	state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, sessionID, agentType, transcriptPath, userPrompt)
  1467→	if err != nil {
  1468→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize session: %w", err)
  1469→	}
  1470→
  1471→	// Apply phase transition: new session starts as ACTIVE.
  1472→	if transErr := TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventTurnStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
  1473→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: turn start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
  1474→	}
  1475→
  1476→	// Calculate attribution for pre-prompt edits
  1477→	// This captures any user edits made before the first prompt
  1478→	promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(repo, state)
  1479→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
  1480→	if err = s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
  1481→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save attribution: %w", err)
  1482→	}
  1483→
  1484→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Initialized shadow session: %s\n", sessionID)
  1485→	return nil
  1486→}
  1487→
  1488→// calculatePromptAttributionAtStart calculates attribution at prompt start (before agent runs).
  1489→// This captures user changes since the last checkpoint - no filtering needed since
  1490→// the agent hasn't made any changes yet.
  1491→//
  1492→// IMPORTANT: This reads from the worktree (not staging area) to match what WriteTemporary
  1493→// captures in checkpoints. If we read staged content but checkpoints capture worktree content,
  1494→// unstaged changes would be in the checkpoint but not counted in PromptAttribution, causing
  1495→// them to be incorrectly attributed to the agent later.
  1496→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(
  1497→	repo *git.Repository,
  1498→	state *SessionState,
  1499→) PromptAttribution {
  1500→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
  1501→	nextCheckpointNum := state.StepCount + 1
  1502→	result := PromptAttribution{CheckpointNumber: nextCheckpointNum}
  1503→
  1504→	// Get last checkpoint tree from shadow branch (if it exists)
  1505→	// For the first checkpoint, no shadow branch exists yet - this is fine,
  1506→	// CalculatePromptAttribution will use baseTree as the reference instead.
  1507→	var lastCheckpointTree *object.Tree
  1508→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1509→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
  1510→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1511→	if err != nil {
  1512→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: no shadow branch yet (first checkpoint)",
  1513→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName))
  1514→		// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1515→	} else {
  1516→		shadowCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
  1517→		if err != nil {
  1518→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: failed to get shadow commit",
  1519→				slog.String("shadow_ref", ref.Hash().String()),
  1520→				slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1521→			// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1522→		} else {
  1523→			lastCheckpointTree, err = shadowCommit.Tree()
  1524→			if err != nil {
  1525→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: failed to get shadow tree",
  1526→					slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1527→				// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1528→			}
  1529→		}
  1530→	}
  1531→
  1532→	// Get base tree for agent lines calculation
  1533→	var baseTree *object.Tree
  1534→	if baseCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(state.BaseCommit)); err == nil {
  1535→		if tree, treeErr := baseCommit.Tree(); treeErr == nil {
  1536→			baseTree = tree
  1537→		} else {
  1538→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: base tree unavailable",
  1539→				slog.String("error", treeErr.Error()))
  1540→		}
  1541→	} else {
  1542→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: base commit unavailable",
  1543→			slog.String("base_commit", state.BaseCommit),
  1544→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1545→	}
  1546→
  1547→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1548→	if err != nil {
  1549→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution skipped: failed to get worktree",
  1550→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1551→		return result
  1552→	}
  1553→
  1554→	// Get worktree status to find ALL changed files
  1555→	status, err := worktree.Status()
  1556→	if err != nil {
  1557→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution skipped: failed to get worktree status",
  1558→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1559→		return result
  1560→	}
  1561→
  1562→	worktreeRoot := worktree.Filesystem.Root()
  1563→
  1564→	// Build map of changed files with their worktree content
  1565→	// IMPORTANT: We read from worktree (not staging area) to match what WriteTemporary
  1566→	// captures in checkpoints. This ensures attribution is consistent.
  1567→	changedFiles := make(map[string]string)
  1568→	for filePath, fileStatus := range status {
  1569→		// Skip unmodified files
  1570→		if fileStatus.Worktree == git.Unmodified && fileStatus.Staging == git.Unmodified {
  1571→			continue
  1572→		}
  1573→		// Skip .entire metadata directory (session data, not user code)
  1574→		if strings.HasPrefix(filePath, paths.EntireMetadataDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(filePath, ".entire/") {
  1575→			continue
  1576→		}
  1577→
  1578→		// Always read from worktree to match checkpoint behavior
  1579→		fullPath := filepath.Join(worktreeRoot, filePath)
  1580→		var content string
  1581→		if data, err := os.ReadFile(fullPath); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // filePath is from git worktree status
  1582→			// Use git's binary detection algorithm (matches getFileContent behavior).
  1583→			// Binary files are excluded from line-based attribution calculations.
  1584→			isBinary, binErr := binary.IsBinary(bytes.NewReader(data))
  1585→			if binErr == nil && !isBinary {
  1586→				content = string(data)
  1587→			}
  1588→		}
  1589→		// else: file deleted, unreadable, or binary - content remains empty string
  1590→
  1591→		changedFiles[filePath] = content
  1592→	}
  1593→
  1594→	// Use CalculatePromptAttribution from manual_commit_attribution.go
  1595→	result = CalculatePromptAttribution(baseTree, lastCheckpointTree, changedFiles, nextCheckpointNum)
  1596→
  1597→	return result
  1598→}
  1599→
  1600→// getStagedFiles returns a list of files staged for commit.
  1601→func getStagedFiles(repo *git.Repository) []string {
  1602→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1603→	if err != nil {
  1604→		return nil
  1605→	}
  1606→
  1607→	status, err := worktree.Status()
  1608→	if err != nil {
  1609→		return nil
  1610→	}
  1611→
  1612→	var staged []string
  1613→	for path, fileStatus := range status {
  1614→		// Check if file is staged (in index)
  1615→		if fileStatus.Staging != git.Unmodified && fileStatus.Staging != git.Untracked {
  1616→			staged = append(staged, path)
  1617→		}
  1618→	}
  1619→	return staged
  1620→}
  1621→
  1622→// getLastPrompt retrieves the most recent user prompt from a session's shadow branch.
  1623→// Returns empty string if no prompt can be retrieved.
  1624→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getLastPrompt(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) string {
  1625→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1626→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
  1627→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1628→	if err != nil {
  1629→		return ""
  1630→	}
  1631→
  1632→	// Extract session data to get prompts for commit message generation
  1633→	// Pass agent type to handle different transcript formats (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini)
  1634→	// Pass 0 for checkpointTranscriptStart since we're extracting all prompts, not calculating token usage
  1635→	sessionData, err := s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, nil, state.AgentType, "", 0)
  1636→	if err != nil || len(sessionData.Prompts) == 0 {
  1637→		return ""
  1638→	}
  1639→
  1640→	// Return the last prompt (most recent work before commit)
  1641→	return sessionData.Prompts[len(sessionData.Prompts)-1]
  1642→}
  1643→
  1644→// HandleTurnEnd dispatches strategy-specific actions emitted when an agent turn ends.
  1645→// The primary job is to finalize all checkpoints from this turn with the full transcript.
  1646→//
  1647→// During a turn, PostCommit writes provisional transcript data (whatever was available
  1648→// at commit time). HandleTurnEnd replaces that with the complete session transcript
  1649→// (from prompt to stop event), ensuring every checkpoint has the full context.
  1650→//
  1651→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
  1652→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) HandleTurnEnd(state *SessionState) error {
  1653→	// Finalize all checkpoints from this turn with the full transcript.
  1654→	//
  1655→	// IMPORTANT: This is best-effort - errors are logged but don't fail the hook.
  1656→	// Failing here would prevent session cleanup and could leave state inconsistent.
  1657→	// The provisional transcript from PostCommit is already persisted, so the
  1658→	// checkpoint isn't lost - it just won't have the complete transcript.
  1659→	errCount := s.finalizeAllTurnCheckpoints(state)
  1660→	if errCount > 0 {
  1661→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1662→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "HandleTurnEnd completed with errors (best-effort)",
  1663→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1664→			slog.Int("error_count", errCount),
  1665→		)
  1666→	}
  1667→	return nil
  1668→}
  1669→
  1670→// finalizeAllTurnCheckpoints replaces the provisional transcript in each checkpoint
  1671→// created during this turn with the full session transcript.
  1672→//
  1673→// This is called at turn end (stop hook). During the turn, PostCommit wrote whatever
  1674→// transcript was available at commit time. Now we have the complete transcript and
  1675→// replace it so every checkpoint has the full prompt-to-stop context.
  1676→//
  1677→// Returns the number of errors encountered (best-effort: continues processing on error).
  1678→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) finalizeAllTurnCheckpoints(state *SessionState) int {
  1679→	if len(state.TurnCheckpointIDs) == 0 {
  1680→		return 0 // No mid-turn commits to finalize
  1681→	}
  1682→
  1683→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1684→
  1685→	logging.Info(logCtx, "finalizing turn checkpoints with full transcript",
  1686→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1687→		slog.Int("checkpoint_count", len(state.TurnCheckpointIDs)),
  1688→	)
  1689→
  1690→	errCount := 0
  1691→
  1692→	// Read full transcript from live transcript file
  1693→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
  1694→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: no transcript path, skipping",
  1695→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1696→		)
  1697→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1698→		return 1 // Count as error - all checkpoints will be skipped
  1699→	}
  1700→
  1701→	fullTranscript, err := os.ReadFile(state.TranscriptPath)
  1702→	if err != nil || len(fullTranscript) == 0 {
  1703→		msg := "finalize: empty transcript, skipping"
  1704→		if err != nil {
  1705→			msg = "finalize: failed to read transcript, skipping"
  1706→		}
  1707→		logging.Warn(logCtx, msg,
  1708→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1709→			slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
  1710→			slog.Any("error", err),
  1711→		)
  1712→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1713→		return 1 // Count as error - all checkpoints will be skipped
  1714→	}
  1715→
  1716→	// Extract prompts and context from the full transcript
  1717→	prompts := extractUserPrompts(state.AgentType, string(fullTranscript))
  1718→	contextBytes := generateContextFromPrompts(prompts)
  1719→
  1720→	// Redact secrets before writing — matches WriteCommitted behavior.
  1721→	// The live transcript on disk contains raw content; redaction must happen
  1722→	// before anything is persisted to the metadata branch.
  1723→	fullTranscript, err = redact.JSONLBytes(fullTranscript)
  1724→	if err != nil {
  1725→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: transcript redaction failed, skipping",
  1726→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1727→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1728→		)
  1729→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1730→		return 1 // Count as error - all checkpoints will be skipped
  1731→	}
  1732→	for i, p := range prompts {
  1733→		prompts[i] = redact.String(p)
  1734→	}
  1735→	contextBytes = redact.Bytes(contextBytes)
  1736→
  1737→	// Open repository and create checkpoint store
  1738→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1739→	if err != nil {
  1740→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: failed to open repository",
  1741→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1742→		)
  1743→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1744→		return 1 // Count as error - all checkpoints will be skipped
  1745→	}
  1746→	store := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
  1747→
  1748→	// Update each checkpoint with the full transcript
  1749→	for _, cpIDStr := range state.TurnCheckpointIDs {
  1750→		cpID, parseErr := id.NewCheckpointID(cpIDStr)
  1751→		if parseErr != nil {
  1752→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: invalid checkpoint ID, skipping",
  1753→				slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpIDStr),
  1754→				slog.String("error", parseErr.Error()),
  1755→			)
  1756→			errCount++
  1757→			continue
  1758→		}
  1759→
  1760→		updateErr := store.UpdateCommitted(context.Background(), checkpoint.UpdateCommittedOptions{
  1761→			CheckpointID: cpID,
  1762→			SessionID:    state.SessionID,
  1763→			Transcript:   fullTranscript,
  1764→			Prompts:      prompts,
  1765→			Context:      contextBytes,
  1766→			Agent:        state.AgentType,
  1767→		})
  1768→		if updateErr != nil {
  1769→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: failed to update checkpoint",
  1770→				slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpIDStr),
  1771→				slog.String("error", updateErr.Error()),
  1772→			)
  1773→			errCount++
  1774→			continue
  1775→		}
  1776→
  1777→		logging.Info(logCtx, "finalize: checkpoint updated with full transcript",
  1778→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpIDStr),
  1779→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1780→		)
  1781→	}
  1782→
  1783→	// Clear turn checkpoint IDs. Do NOT update CheckpointTranscriptStart here — it was
  1784→	// already set correctly by PostCommit: condenseAndUpdateState sets it to the total
  1785→	// transcript lines when condensing, and carryForwardToNewShadowBranch resets it to 0
  1786→	// when carry-forward is active. Overwriting here would break carry-forward by making
  1787→	// sessionHasNewContent think the transcript is fully consumed (no growth).
  1788→	state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1789→
  1790→	return errCount
  1791→}
  1792→
  1793→// filesChangedInCommit returns the set of files changed in a commit by diffing against its parent.
  1794→func filesChangedInCommit(commit *object.Commit) map[string]struct{} {
  1795→	result := make(map[string]struct{})
  1796→
  1797→	commitTree, err := commit.Tree()
  1798→	if err != nil {
  1799→		return result
  1800→	}
  1801→
  1802→	var parentTree *object.Tree
  1803→	if commit.NumParents() > 0 {
  1804→		parent, err := commit.Parent(0)
  1805→		if err != nil {
  1806→			return result
  1807→		}
  1808→		parentTree, err = parent.Tree()
  1809→		if err != nil {
  1810→			return result
  1811→		}
  1812→	}
  1813→
  1814→	if parentTree == nil {
  1815→		// Initial commit — all files are new
  1816→		if iterErr := commitTree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
  1817→			result[f.Name] = struct{}{}
  1818→			return nil
  1819→		}); iterErr != nil {
  1820→			return result
  1821→		}
  1822→		return result
  1823→	}
  1824→
  1825→	changes, err := parentTree.Diff(commitTree)
  1826→	if err != nil {
  1827→		return result
  1828→	}
  1829→	for _, change := range changes {
  1830→		name := change.To.Name
  1831→		if name == "" {
  1832→			name = change.From.Name
  1833→		}
  1834→		result[name] = struct{}{}
  1835→	}
  1836→	return result
  1837→}
  1838→
  1839→// subtractFiles returns files that are NOT in the exclude set.
  1840→func subtractFiles(files []string, exclude map[string]struct{}) []string {
  1841→	var remaining []string
  1842→	for _, f := range files {
  1843→		if _, excluded := exclude[f]; !excluded {
  1844→			remaining = append(remaining, f)
  1845→		}
  1846→	}
  1847→	return remaining
  1848→}
  1849→
  1850→// carryForwardToNewShadowBranch creates a new shadow branch at the current HEAD
  1851→// containing the remaining uncommitted files and all session metadata.
  1852→// This enables the next commit to get its own unique checkpoint.
  1853→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) carryForwardToNewShadowBranch(
  1854→	logCtx context.Context,
  1855→	repo *git.Repository,
  1856→	state *SessionState,
  1857→	remainingFiles []string,
  1858→) {
  1859→	store := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
  1860→
  1861→	// Don't include metadata directory in carry-forward. The carry-forward branch
  1862→	// only needs to preserve file content for comparison - not the transcript.
  1863→	// Including the transcript would cause sessionHasNewContent to always return true
  1864→	// because CheckpointTranscriptStart is reset to 0 for carry-forward.
  1865→	result, err := store.WriteTemporary(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteTemporaryOptions{
  1866→		SessionID:         state.SessionID,
  1867→		BaseCommit:        state.BaseCommit,
  1868→		WorktreeID:        state.WorktreeID,
  1869→		ModifiedFiles:     remainingFiles,
  1870→		MetadataDir:       "",
  1871→		MetadataDirAbs:    "",
  1872→		CommitMessage:     "carry forward: uncommitted session files",
  1873→		IsFirstCheckpoint: false,
  1874→	})
  1875→	if err != nil {
  1876→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward failed",
  1877→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1878→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1879→		)
  1880→		return
  1881→	}
  1882→	if result.Skipped {
  1883→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward skipped (no changes)",
  1884→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1885→		)
  1886→		return
  1887→	}
  1888→
  1889→	// Update state for the carry-forward checkpoint.
  1890→	// CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0 is intentional: each checkpoint is self-contained with
  1891→	// the full transcript. This trades storage efficiency for simplicity:
  1892→	// - Pro: Each checkpoint is independently readable without needing to stitch together
  1893→	//   multiple checkpoints to understand the session history
  1894→	// - Con: For long sessions with multiple partial commits, each checkpoint includes
  1895→	//   the full transcript, which could be large
  1896→	// An alternative would be incremental checkpoints (only new content since last condensation),
  1897→	// but this would complicate checkpoint retrieval and require careful tracking of dependencies.
  1898→	state.StepCount = 1
  1899→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0
  1900→	state.LastCheckpointID = ""
  1901→	// NOTE: TurnCheckpointIDs is intentionally NOT cleared here. Those checkpoint
  1902→	// IDs from earlier in the turn still need finalization with the full transcript
  1903→	// when HandleTurnEnd runs at stop time.
  1904→
  1905→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-commit: carried forward remaining files",
  1906→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1907→		slog.Int("remaining_files", len(remainingFiles)),
  1908→	)
  1909→}
  1910→
