    27→
    28→// isNotFoundError checks if an error represents a "not found" condition in go-git.
    29→// This includes entry not found, file not found, directory not found, and object not found.
    30→func isNotFoundError(err error) bool {
    31→	return errors.Is(err, object.ErrEntryNotFound) ||
    32→		errors.Is(err, object.ErrFileNotFound) ||
    33→		errors.Is(err, object.ErrDirectoryNotFound) ||
    34→		errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrObjectNotFound) ||
    35→		errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrReferenceNotFound)
    36→}
    37→
    38→// commitOrHead attempts to create a commit. If the commit would be empty (files already
    39→// committed), it returns HEAD hash instead. This handles the case where files were
    40→// modified during a session but already committed by the user before the hook runs.
    41→func commitOrHead(repo *git.Repository, worktree *git.Worktree, msg string, author *object.Signature) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
    42→	commitHash, err := worktree.Commit(msg, &git.CommitOptions{Author: author})
    43→	if errors.Is(err, git.ErrEmptyCommit) {
    44→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No changes to commit (files already committed)\n")
    45→		head, err := repo.Head()
    46→		if err != nil {
    47→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
    48→		}
    49→		return head.Hash(), nil
    50→	}
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to commit: %w", err)
    53→	}
    54→	return commitHash, nil
    55→}
    56→
    57→// AutoCommitStrategy implements the auto-commit strategy:
    58→// - Code changes are committed to the active branch (like commit strategy)
    59→// - Session logs are committed to a shadow branch (like manual-commit strategy)
    60→// - Code commits can reference the shadow branch via trailers
    61→type AutoCommitStrategy struct {
    62→	// checkpointStore manages checkpoint data on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
    63→	checkpointStore *checkpoint.GitStore
    64→	// checkpointStoreOnce ensures thread-safe lazy initialization
    65→	checkpointStoreOnce sync.Once
    66→	// checkpointStoreErr captures any error during initialization
    67→	checkpointStoreErr error
    68→}
    69→
    70→// getCheckpointStore returns the checkpoint store, initializing it lazily if needed.
    71→// Thread-safe via sync.Once.
    72→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) getCheckpointStore() (*checkpoint.GitStore, error) {
    73→	s.checkpointStoreOnce.Do(func() {
    74→		repo, err := OpenRepository()
    75→		if err != nil {
    76→			s.checkpointStoreErr = fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
    77→			return
    78→		}
    79→		s.checkpointStore = checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
    80→	})
    81→	return s.checkpointStore, s.checkpointStoreErr
    82→}
    83→
    84→// NewAutoCommitStrategy creates a new AutoCommitStrategy instance.
    85→//
    86→
    87→func NewAutoCommitStrategy() Strategy {
    88→	return &AutoCommitStrategy{}
    89→}
    90→
    91→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) Name() string {
    92→	return StrategyNameAutoCommit
    93→}
    94→
    95→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) Description() string {
    96→	return "Auto-commits code to active branch with metadata on entire/checkpoints/v1"
    97→}
    98→
    99→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) ValidateRepository() error {
   100→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   101→	if err != nil {
   102→		return fmt.Errorf("not a git repository: %w", err)
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	_, err = repo.Worktree()
   106→	if err != nil {
   107→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to access worktree: %w", err)
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	return nil
   111→}
   112→
   113→// PrePush is called by the git pre-push hook before pushing to a remote.
   114→// It pushes the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch alongside the user's push.
   115→// Configuration options (stored in .entire/settings.json under strategy_options.push_sessions):
   116→//   - "auto": always push automatically
   117→//   - "prompt" (default): ask user with option to enable auto
   118→//   - "false"/"off"/"no": never push
   119→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) PrePush(remote string) error {
   120→	if err := pushSessionsBranchCommon(remote, paths.MetadataBranchName); err != nil {
   121→		return err
   122→	}
   123→	return PushTrailsBranch(remote)
   124→}
   125→
   126→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) SaveStep(ctx StepContext) error {
   127→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   128→	if err != nil {
   129→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   130→	}
   131→
   132→	// Generate checkpoint ID for this commit
   133→	cpID, err := id.Generate()
   134→	if err != nil {
   135→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   136→	}
   137→	// Step 1: Commit code changes to active branch with checkpoint ID trailer
   138→	// We do code first to avoid orphaned metadata if this step fails.
   139→	// If metadata commit fails after this, the code commit exists but GetRewindPoints
   140→	// already handles missing metadata gracefully (skips commits without metadata).
   141→	codeResult, err := s.commitCodeToActive(repo, ctx, cpID)
   142→	if err != nil {
   143→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit code to active branch: %w", err)
   144→	}
   145→
   146→	// If no code commit was created (no changes), skip metadata creation
   147→	// This prevents orphaned metadata commits that don't correspond to any code commit
   148→	if !codeResult.Created {
   149→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   150→		logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint skipped (no changes)",
   151→			slog.String("strategy", "auto-commit"),
   152→			slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   153→		)
   154→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipped checkpoint (no changes since last commit)\n")
   155→		return nil
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	// Step 2: Commit metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch using sharded path
   159→	// Path is <checkpointID[:2]>/<checkpointID[2:]>/ for direct lookup
   160→	_, err = s.commitMetadataToMetadataBranch(repo, ctx, cpID)
   161→	if err != nil {
   162→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch: %w", err)
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	// Log checkpoint creation
   166→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   167→	logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint saved",
   168→		slog.String("strategy", "auto-commit"),
   169→		slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   170→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
   171→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(ctx.ModifiedFiles)),
   172→		slog.Int("new_files", len(ctx.NewFiles)),
   173→		slog.Int("deleted_files", len(ctx.DeletedFiles)),
   174→	)
   175→
   176→	return nil
   177→}
   178→
   179→// commitCodeResult contains the result of committing code to the active branch.
   180→type commitCodeResult struct {
   181→	CommitHash plumbing.Hash
   182→	Created    bool // True if a new commit was created, false if skipped (no changes)
   183→}
   184→
   185→// commitCodeToActive commits code changes to the active branch.
   186→// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer for metadata lookup that survives amend/rebase.
   187→// Returns the result containing commit hash and whether a commit was created.
   188→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) commitCodeToActive(repo *git.Repository, ctx StepContext, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (commitCodeResult, error) {
   189→	// Check if there are any code changes to commit
   190→	if len(ctx.ModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(ctx.NewFiles) == 0 && len(ctx.DeletedFiles) == 0 {
   191→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No code changes to commit to active branch\n")
   192→		// Return current HEAD hash but mark as not created
   193→		head, err := repo.Head()
   194→		if err != nil {
   195→			return commitCodeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   196→		}
   197→		return commitCodeResult{CommitHash: head.Hash(), Created: false}, nil
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   201→	if err != nil {
   202→		return commitCodeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree: %w", err)
   203→	}
   204→
   205→	// Get HEAD hash before commit to detect if commitOrHead actually creates a new commit
   206→	// (commitOrHead returns HEAD hash without error when git.ErrEmptyCommit occurs)
   207→	headBefore, err := repo.Head()
   208→	if err != nil {
   209→		return commitCodeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   210→	}
   211→
   212→	// Stage code changes
   213→	StageFiles(worktree, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles, StageForSession)
   214→
   215→	// Add checkpoint ID trailer to commit message
   216→	commitMsg := ctx.CommitMessage + "\n\n" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
   217→
   218→	author := &object.Signature{
   219→		Name:  ctx.AuthorName,
   220→		Email: ctx.AuthorEmail,
   221→		When:  time.Now(),
   222→	}
   223→	commitHash, err := commitOrHead(repo, worktree, commitMsg, author)
   224→	if err != nil {
   225→		return commitCodeResult{}, err
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	// Check if a new commit was actually created by comparing with HEAD before
   229→	created := commitHash != headBefore.Hash()
   230→	if created {
   231→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed code changes to active branch (%s)\n", commitHash.String()[:7])
   232→	}
   233→	return commitCodeResult{CommitHash: commitHash, Created: created}, nil
   234→}
   235→
   236→// commitMetadataToMetadataBranch commits session metadata to the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   237→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpointID[:2]>/<checkpointID[2:]>/
   238→// This allows direct lookup from the checkpoint ID trailer on the code commit.
   239→// Uses checkpoint.WriteCommitted for git operations.
   240→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) commitMetadataToMetadataBranch(repo *git.Repository, ctx StepContext, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
   241→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   242→	if err != nil {
   243→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   244→	}
   245→
   246→	// Extract session ID from metadata dir
   247→	sessionID := filepath.Base(ctx.MetadataDir)
   248→
   249→	// Get current branch name
   250→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   251→
   252→	// Combine all file changes into FilesTouched (same as manual-commit)
   253→	filesTouched := mergeFilesTouched(nil, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles)
   254→
   255→	// Load TurnID from session state (correlates checkpoints from the same turn)
   256→	var turnID string
   257→	if state, loadErr := LoadSessionState(sessionID); loadErr == nil && state != nil {
   258→		turnID = state.TurnID
   259→	}
   260→
   261→	// Write committed checkpoint using the checkpoint store
   262→	// Pass TranscriptPath so writeTranscript generates content_hash.txt
   263→	transcriptPath := filepath.Join(ctx.MetadataDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   264→	err = store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteCommittedOptions{
   265→		CheckpointID:                checkpointID,
   266→		SessionID:                   sessionID,
   267→		Strategy:                    StrategyNameAutoCommit, // Use new strategy name
   268→		Branch:                      branchName,
   269→		MetadataDir:                 ctx.MetadataDirAbs, // Copy all files from metadata dir
   270→		TranscriptPath:              transcriptPath,     // For content hash generation
   271→		AuthorName:                  ctx.AuthorName,
   272→		AuthorEmail:                 ctx.AuthorEmail,
   273→		Agent:                       ctx.AgentType,
   274→		TurnID:                      turnID,
   275→		TranscriptIdentifierAtStart: ctx.StepTranscriptIdentifier,
   276→		CheckpointTranscriptStart:   ctx.StepTranscriptStart,
   277→		TokenUsage:                  ctx.TokenUsage,
   278→		CheckpointsCount:            1,            // Each auto-commit checkpoint = 1
   279→		FilesTouched:                filesTouched, // Track modified files (same as manual-commit)
   280→	})
   281→	if err != nil {
   282→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to write committed checkpoint: %w", err)
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed session metadata to %s (%s)\n", paths.MetadataBranchName, checkpointID)
   286→	return plumbing.ZeroHash, nil // Commit hash not needed by callers
   287→}
   288→
   289→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetRewindPoints(limit int) ([]RewindPoint, error) {
   290→	// For auto-commit strategy, rewind points are found by looking for Entire-Checkpoint trailers
   291→	// in the current branch's commit history. The checkpoint ID provides direct lookup
   292→	// to metadata on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   293→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   294→	if err != nil {
   295→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	head, err := repo.Head()
   299→	if err != nil {
   300→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   301→	}
   302→
   303→	// Get metadata branch tree for lookups
   304→	metadataTree, err := GetMetadataBranchTree(repo)
   305→	if err != nil {
   306→		// No metadata branch yet is fine
   307→		return []RewindPoint{}, nil //nolint:nilerr // Expected when no metadata exists
   308→	}
   309→
   310→	// Get the main branch commit hash to determine branch-only commits
   311→	mainBranchHash := GetMainBranchHash(repo)
   312→
   313→	// Walk current branch history looking for commits with checkpoint trailers
   314→	iter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{
   315→		From:  head.Hash(),
   316→		Order: git.LogOrderCommitterTime,
   317→	})
   318→	if err != nil {
   319→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit log: %w", err)
   320→	}
   321→
   322→	var points []RewindPoint
   323→	count := 0
   324→
   325→	err = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   326→		if count >= logsOnlyScanLimit || len(points) >= limit {
   327→			return errStop
   328→		}
   329→		count++
   330→
   331→		// Check for Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   332→		cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(c.Message)
   333→		if !found {
   334→			return nil
   335→		}
   336→
   337→		// Look up metadata from sharded path
   338→		checkpointPath := cpID.Path()
   339→		metadata, err := ReadCheckpointMetadata(metadataTree, checkpointPath)
   340→		if err != nil {
   341→			// Checkpoint exists in commit but no metadata found - skip this commit
   342→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: skip commits without metadata
   343→		}
   344→
   345→		message := strings.Split(c.Message, "\n")[0]
   346→
   347→		// Determine if this is a full rewind or logs-only
   348→		// Full rewind is allowed if commit is only on this branch (not reachable from main)
   349→		isLogsOnly := false
   350→		if mainBranchHash != plumbing.ZeroHash {
   351→			if IsAncestorOf(repo, c.Hash, mainBranchHash) {
   352→				isLogsOnly = true
   353→			}
   354→		}
   355→
   356→		// Build metadata path - for task checkpoints, include the task path
   357→		metadataDir := checkpointPath
   358→		if metadata.IsTask && metadata.ToolUseID != "" {
   359→			metadataDir = checkpointPath + "/tasks/" + metadata.ToolUseID
   360→		}
   361→
   362→		// Read session prompt from metadata tree
   363→		sessionPrompt := ReadSessionPromptFromTree(metadataTree, checkpointPath)
   364→
   365→		points = append(points, RewindPoint{
   366→			ID:               c.Hash.String(),
   367→			Message:          message,
   368→			MetadataDir:      metadataDir,
   369→			Date:             c.Author.When,
   370→			IsLogsOnly:       isLogsOnly,
   371→			CheckpointID:     cpID,
   372→			IsTaskCheckpoint: metadata.IsTask,
   373→			ToolUseID:        metadata.ToolUseID,
   374→			Agent:            metadata.Agent,
   375→			SessionID:        metadata.SessionID,
   376→			SessionPrompt:    sessionPrompt,
   377→		})
   378→
   379→		return nil
   380→	})
   381→
   382→	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, errStop) {
   383→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to iterate commits: %w", err)
   384→	}
   385→
   386→	return points, nil
   387→}
   388→
   389→// findTaskMetadataPathForCommit looks up the task metadata path for a task checkpoint commit
   390→// by searching the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch commit history for the checkpoint directory.
   391→// Returns ("", nil) if metadata is not found - this is expected for commits without metadata.
   392→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) findTaskMetadataPathForCommit(repo *git.Repository, commitSHA, toolUseID string) (string, error) {
   393→	// Get the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   394→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   395→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   396→	if err != nil {
   397→		if isNotFoundError(err) {
   398→			return "", nil // No metadata branch yet
   399→		}
   400→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata branch: %w", err)
   401→	}
   402→
   403→	// Search commit history for a commit referencing this code commit SHA and tool use ID
   404→	shortSHA := commitSHA
   405→	if len(shortSHA) > 7 {
   406→		shortSHA = shortSHA[:7]
   407→	}
   408→
   409→	iter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: ref.Hash()})
   410→	if err != nil {
   411→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit log: %w", err)
   412→	}
   413→
   414→	var foundTaskPath string
   415→	err = iter.ForEach(func(commit *object.Commit) error {
   416→		// Check if commit message contains "Commit: <sha>" and the tool use ID
   417→		if strings.Contains(commit.Message, "Commit: "+shortSHA) &&
   418→			strings.Contains(commit.Message, toolUseID) {
   419→			// Parse task metadata trailer
   420→			if taskPath, found := trailers.ParseTaskMetadata(commit.Message); found {
   421→				foundTaskPath = taskPath
   422→				return errStop // Found it
   423→			}
   424→		}
   425→		return nil
   426→	})
   427→	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, errStop) {
   428→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to iterate commits: %w", err)
   429→	}
   430→
   431→	return foundTaskPath, nil
   432→}
   433→
   434→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) Rewind(point RewindPoint) error {
   435→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(point.ID)
   436→	shortID, err := HardResetWithProtection(commitHash)
   437→	if err != nil {
   438→		return err
   439→	}
   440→
   441→	fmt.Println()
   442→	fmt.Printf("Reset to commit %s\n", shortID)
   443→	fmt.Println()
   444→
   445→	return nil
   446→}
   447→
   448→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) CanRewind() (bool, string, error) {
   449→	return checkCanRewind()
   450→}
   451→
   452→// PreviewRewind returns what will happen if rewinding to the given point.
   453→// For auto-commit strategy, this returns nil since git reset doesn't delete untracked files.
   454→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) PreviewRewind(_ RewindPoint) (*RewindPreview, error) {
   455→	// Auto-commit uses git reset --hard which doesn't affect untracked files
   456→	// Return empty preview to indicate no untracked files will be deleted
   457→	return &RewindPreview{}, nil
   458→}
   459→
   460→// EnsureSetup ensures the strategy's required setup is in place.
   461→// For auto-commit strategy:
   462→// - Ensure .entire/.gitignore has all required entries
   463→// - Create orphan entire/checkpoints/v1 branch if it doesn't exist
   464→// - Install git hooks if missing (self-healing for third-party overwrites)
   465→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) EnsureSetup() error {
   466→	if err := EnsureEntireGitignore(); err != nil {
   467→		return err
   468→	}
   469→
   470→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   471→	if err != nil {
   472→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   473→	}
   474→
   475→	// Ensure the entire/checkpoints/v1 orphan branch exists
   476→	if err := EnsureMetadataBranch(repo); err != nil {
   477→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure metadata branch: %w", err)
   478→	}
   479→
   480→	// Install generic hooks if missing (they delegate to strategy at runtime)
   481→	if !IsGitHookInstalled() {
   482→		if _, err := InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   483→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   484→		}
   485→	}
   486→
   487→	return nil
   488→}
   489→
   490→// GetSessionInfo returns session information for linking commits.
   491→// For auto-commit strategy, we don't track active sessions - metadata is stored on
   492→// entire/checkpoints/v1 branch when SaveStep is called. Active branch commits
   493→// are kept clean (no trailers), so this returns ErrNoSession.
   494→// Use ListSessions() or GetSession() to retrieve session info from the metadata branch.
   495→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetSessionInfo() (*SessionInfo, error) {
   496→	// Dual strategy doesn't track active sessions like shadow does.
   497→	// Session metadata is stored on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch and can be
   498→	// retrieved via ListSessions() or GetSession().
   499→	return nil, ErrNoSession
   500→}
   501→
   502→// SaveTaskStep creates a checkpoint commit for a completed task.
   503→// For auto-commit strategy:
   504→// 1. Commit code changes to active branch (no trailers - clean history)
   505→// 2. Commit task metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch with checkpoint format
   506→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) SaveTaskStep(ctx TaskStepContext) error {
   507→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   508→	if err != nil {
   509→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   510→	}
   511→
   512→	// Ensure entire/checkpoints/v1 branch exists
   513→	if err := EnsureMetadataBranch(repo); err != nil {
   514→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure metadata branch: %w", err)
   515→	}
   516→
   517→	// Generate checkpoint ID for this task checkpoint
   518→	cpID, err := id.Generate()
   519→	if err != nil {
   520→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   521→	}
   522→
   523→	// Step 1: Commit code changes to active branch with checkpoint ID trailer
   524→	// We do code first to avoid orphaned metadata if this step fails.
   525→	_, err = s.commitTaskCodeToActive(repo, ctx, cpID)
   526→	if err != nil {
   527→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit task code to active branch: %w", err)
   528→	}
   529→
   530→	// Step 2: Commit task metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch at sharded path
   531→	_, err = s.commitTaskMetadataToMetadataBranch(repo, ctx, cpID)
   532→	if err != nil {
   533→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit task metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch: %w", err)
   534→	}
   535→
   536→	// Log task checkpoint creation
   537→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   538→	attrs := []any{
   539→		slog.String("strategy", "auto-commit"),
   540→		slog.String("checkpoint_type", "task"),
   541→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
   542→		slog.String("checkpoint_uuid", ctx.CheckpointUUID),
   543→		slog.String("tool_use_id", ctx.ToolUseID),
   544→		slog.String("subagent_type", ctx.SubagentType),
   545→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(ctx.ModifiedFiles)),
   546→		slog.Int("new_files", len(ctx.NewFiles)),
   547→		slog.Int("deleted_files", len(ctx.DeletedFiles)),
   548→	}
   549→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   550→		attrs = append(attrs,
   551→			slog.Bool("is_incremental", true),
   552→			slog.String("incremental_type", ctx.IncrementalType),
   553→			slog.Int("incremental_sequence", ctx.IncrementalSequence),
   554→		)
   555→	}
   556→	logging.Info(logCtx, "task checkpoint saved", attrs...)
   557→
   558→	return nil
   559→}
   560→
   561→// commitTaskCodeToActive commits task code changes to the active branch.
   562→// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer for metadata lookup that survives amend/rebase.
   563→// Skips commit creation if there are no file changes.
   564→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) commitTaskCodeToActive(repo *git.Repository, ctx TaskStepContext, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
   565→	hasFileChanges := len(ctx.ModifiedFiles) > 0 || len(ctx.NewFiles) > 0 || len(ctx.DeletedFiles) > 0
   566→
   567→	// If no file changes, skip code commit
   568→	if !hasFileChanges {
   569→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No code changes to commit for task checkpoint\n")
   570→		// Return current HEAD hash so metadata can still be stored
   571→		head, err := repo.Head()
   572→		if err != nil {
   573→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   574→		}
   575→		return head.Hash(), nil
   576→	}
   577→
   578→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   579→	if err != nil {
   580→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree: %w", err)
   581→	}
   582→
   583→	// Stage code changes
   584→	StageFiles(worktree, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles, StageForTask)
   585→
   586→	// Build commit message with checkpoint trailer
   587→	shortToolUseID := ctx.ToolUseID
   588→	if len(shortToolUseID) > id.ShortIDLength {
   589→		shortToolUseID = shortToolUseID[:id.ShortIDLength]
   590→	}
   591→
   592→	var subject string
   593→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   594→		subject = FormatIncrementalSubject(
   595→			ctx.IncrementalType,
   596→			ctx.SubagentType,
   597→			ctx.TaskDescription,
   598→			ctx.TodoContent,
   599→			ctx.IncrementalSequence,
   600→			shortToolUseID,
   601→		)
   602→	} else {
   603→		subject = FormatSubagentEndMessage(ctx.SubagentType, ctx.TaskDescription, shortToolUseID)
   604→	}
   605→
   606→	// Add checkpoint ID trailer to commit message
   607→	commitMsg := subject + "\n\n" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
   608→
   609→	author := &object.Signature{
   610→		Name:  ctx.AuthorName,
   611→		Email: ctx.AuthorEmail,
   612→		When:  time.Now(),
   613→	}
   614→
   615→	commitHash, err := commitOrHead(repo, worktree, commitMsg, author)
   616→	if err != nil {
   617→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
   618→	}
   619→
   620→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   621→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed incremental checkpoint #%d to active branch (%s)\n", ctx.IncrementalSequence, commitHash.String()[:7])
   622→	} else {
   623→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed task checkpoint to active branch (%s)\n", commitHash.String()[:7])
   624→	}
   625→	return commitHash, nil
   626→}
   627→
   628→// commitTaskMetadataToMetadataBranch commits task metadata to the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   629→// Uses sharded path: <checkpointID[:2]>/<checkpointID[2:]>/tasks/<tool-use-id>/
   630→// Returns the metadata commit hash.
   631→// When IsIncremental is true, only writes the incremental checkpoint file, skipping transcripts.
   632→// Uses checkpoint.WriteCommitted for git operations.
   633→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) commitTaskMetadataToMetadataBranch(repo *git.Repository, ctx TaskStepContext, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
   634→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   635→	if err != nil {
   636→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   637→	}
   638→
   639→	// Format commit subject line for better git log readability
   640→	shortToolUseID := ctx.ToolUseID
   641→	if len(shortToolUseID) > id.ShortIDLength {
   642→		shortToolUseID = shortToolUseID[:id.ShortIDLength]
   643→	}
   644→
   645→	var messageSubject string
   646→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   647→		messageSubject = FormatIncrementalSubject(
   648→			ctx.IncrementalType,
   649→			ctx.SubagentType,
   650→			ctx.TaskDescription,
   651→			ctx.TodoContent,
   652→			ctx.IncrementalSequence,
   653→			shortToolUseID,
   654→		)
   655→	} else {
   656→		messageSubject = FormatSubagentEndMessage(ctx.SubagentType, ctx.TaskDescription, shortToolUseID)
   657→	}
   658→
   659→	// Get current branch name
   660→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   661→
   662→	// Write committed checkpoint using the checkpoint store
   663→	err = store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteCommittedOptions{
   664→		CheckpointID:           checkpointID,
   665→		SessionID:              ctx.SessionID,
   666→		Strategy:               StrategyNameAutoCommit,
   667→		Branch:                 branchName,
   668→		IsTask:                 true,
   669→		ToolUseID:              ctx.ToolUseID,
   670→		AgentID:                ctx.AgentID,
   671→		CheckpointUUID:         ctx.CheckpointUUID,
   672→		TranscriptPath:         ctx.TranscriptPath,
   673→		SubagentTranscriptPath: ctx.SubagentTranscriptPath,
   674→		IsIncremental:          ctx.IsIncremental,
   675→		IncrementalSequence:    ctx.IncrementalSequence,
   676→		IncrementalType:        ctx.IncrementalType,
   677→		IncrementalData:        ctx.IncrementalData,
   678→		CommitSubject:          messageSubject,
   679→		AuthorName:             ctx.AuthorName,
   680→		AuthorEmail:            ctx.AuthorEmail,
   681→		Agent:                  ctx.AgentType,
   682→	})
   683→	if err != nil {
   684→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to write task checkpoint: %w", err)
   685→	}
   686→
   687→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   688→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed incremental checkpoint metadata to %s (%s)\n", paths.MetadataBranchName, checkpointID)
   689→	} else {
   690→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed task metadata to %s (%s)\n", paths.MetadataBranchName, checkpointID)
   691→	}
   692→	return plumbing.ZeroHash, nil // Commit hash not needed by callers
   693→}
   694→
   695→// GetTaskCheckpoint returns the task checkpoint for a given rewind point.
   696→// For auto-commit strategy, checkpoints are stored on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch in checkpoint directories.
   697→// Returns ErrNotTaskCheckpoint if the point is not a task checkpoint.
   698→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetTaskCheckpoint(point RewindPoint) (*TaskCheckpoint, error) {
   699→	if !point.IsTaskCheckpoint {
   700→		return nil, ErrNotTaskCheckpoint
   701→	}
   702→
   703→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   704→	if err != nil {
   705→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   706→	}
   707→
   708→	// Get the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   709→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   710→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   711→	if err != nil {
   712→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("metadata branch %s not found: %w", paths.MetadataBranchName, err)
   713→	}
   714→
   715→	metadataCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   716→	if err != nil {
   717→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata branch commit: %w", err)
   718→	}
   719→
   720→	tree, err := metadataCommit.Tree()
   721→	if err != nil {
   722→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata tree: %w", err)
   723→	}
   724→
   725→	// Find checkpoint using the metadata path from rewind point
   726→	// MetadataDir for auto-commit task checkpoints is: cond-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS-XXXXXXXX/tasks/<tool-use-id>
   727→	checkpointPath := point.MetadataDir + "/checkpoint.json"
   728→	file, err := tree.File(checkpointPath)
   729→	if err != nil {
   730→		// Try finding via commit SHA lookup
   731→		taskCheckpointPath, findErr := s.findTaskCheckpointPath(repo, point.ID, point.ToolUseID)
   732→		if findErr != nil {
   733→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find checkpoint at %s: %w", checkpointPath, err)
   734→		}
   735→		file, err = tree.File(taskCheckpointPath)
   736→		if err != nil {
   737→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find checkpoint at %s: %w", taskCheckpointPath, err)
   738→		}
   739→	}
   740→
   741→	content, err := file.Contents()
   742→	if err != nil {
   743→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
   744→	}
   745→
   746→	var checkpoint TaskCheckpoint
   747→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &checkpoint); err != nil {
   748→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse checkpoint: %w", err)
   749→	}
   750→
   751→	return &checkpoint, nil
   752→}
   753→
   754→// GetTaskCheckpointTranscript returns the session transcript for a task checkpoint.
   755→// For auto-commit strategy, transcripts are stored on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch in checkpoint directories.
   756→// Returns ErrNotTaskCheckpoint if the point is not a task checkpoint.
   757→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetTaskCheckpointTranscript(point RewindPoint) ([]byte, error) {
   758→	if !point.IsTaskCheckpoint {
   759→		return nil, ErrNotTaskCheckpoint
   760→	}
   761→
   762→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   763→	if err != nil {
   764→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   765→	}
   766→
   767→	// Get the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   768→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   769→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   770→	if err != nil {
   771→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("metadata branch %s not found: %w", paths.MetadataBranchName, err)
   772→	}
   773→
   774→	metadataCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   775→	if err != nil {
   776→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata branch commit: %w", err)
   777→	}
   778→
   779→	tree, err := metadataCommit.Tree()
   780→	if err != nil {
   781→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata tree: %w", err)
   782→	}
   783→
   784→	// MetadataDir for auto-commit task checkpoints is: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/tasks/<tool-use-id>
   785→	// Extract the checkpoint path by removing "/tasks/<tool-use-id>"
   786→	metadataDir := point.MetadataDir
   787→	if idx := strings.Index(metadataDir, "/tasks/"); idx > 0 {
   788→		checkpointPath := metadataDir[:idx]
   789→
   790→		// Use the first session's transcript path from sessions array
   791→		transcriptPath := ""
   792→		summaryFile, summaryErr := tree.File(checkpointPath + "/" + paths.MetadataFileName)
   793→		if summaryErr == nil {
   794→			summaryContent, contentErr := summaryFile.Contents()
   795→			if contentErr == nil {
   796→				var summary checkpoint.CheckpointSummary
   797→				if json.Unmarshal([]byte(summaryContent), &summary) == nil && len(summary.Sessions) > 0 {
   798→					// Use first session's transcript path (task checkpoints have only one session)
   799→					// SessionFilePaths now contains absolute paths with leading "/"
   800→					// Strip the leading "/" for tree.File() which expects paths without leading slash
   801→					if summary.Sessions[0].Transcript != "" {
   802→						transcriptPath = strings.TrimPrefix(summary.Sessions[0].Transcript, "/")
   803→					}
   804→				}
   805→			}
   806→		}
   807→
   808→		// Fall back to old format if sessions map not available
   809→		if transcriptPath == "" {
   810→			transcriptPath = checkpointPath + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName
   811→		}
   812→
   813→		file, err := tree.File(transcriptPath)
   814→		if err != nil {
   815→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find transcript at %s: %w", transcriptPath, err)
   816→		}
   817→		content, err := file.Contents()
   818→		if err != nil {
   819→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   820→		}
   821→		return []byte(content), nil
   822→	}
   823→
   824→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid metadata path format: %s", metadataDir)
   825→}
   826→
   827→// findTaskCheckpointPath finds the full path to a task checkpoint on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   828→// Searches checkpoint directories for the task checkpoint matching the commit SHA and tool use ID.
   829→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) findTaskCheckpointPath(repo *git.Repository, commitSHA, toolUseID string) (string, error) {
   830→	// Use findTaskMetadataPathForCommit which searches commit history
   831→	taskPath, err := s.findTaskMetadataPathForCommit(repo, commitSHA, toolUseID)
   832→	if err != nil {
   833→		return "", err
   834→	}
   835→	if taskPath == "" {
   836→		return "", errors.New("task checkpoint not found")
   837→	}
   838→	// taskPath is like: cond-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS-XXXXXXXX/tasks/<tool-use-id>/checkpoints/001-<tool-use-id>.json
   839→	// We need: cond-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS-XXXXXXXX/tasks/<tool-use-id>/checkpoint.json
   840→	if idx := strings.Index(taskPath, "/checkpoints/"); idx > 0 {
   841→		return taskPath[:idx] + "/checkpoint.json", nil
   842→	}
   843→	return taskPath + "/checkpoint.json", nil
   844→}
   845→
   846→// GetMetadataRef returns a reference to the metadata for the given checkpoint.
   847→// For auto-commit strategy, returns the checkpoint path on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   848→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetMetadataRef(checkpoint Checkpoint) string {
   849→	if checkpoint.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   850→		return ""
   851→	}
   852→	return paths.MetadataBranchName + ":" + checkpoint.CheckpointID.Path()
   853→}
   854→
   855→// GetSessionMetadataRef returns a reference to the most recent metadata for a session.
   856→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetSessionMetadataRef(sessionID string) string {
   857→	session, err := GetSession(sessionID)
   858→	if err != nil || len(session.Checkpoints) == 0 {
   859→		return ""
   860→	}
   861→	// Checkpoints are ordered with most recent first
   862→	return s.GetMetadataRef(session.Checkpoints[0])
   863→}
   864→
   865→// GetSessionContext returns the context.md content for a session.
   866→// For auto-commit strategy, reads from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch using the checkpoint store.
   867→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetSessionContext(sessionID string) string {
   868→	session, err := GetSession(sessionID)
   869→	if err != nil || len(session.Checkpoints) == 0 {
   870→		return ""
   871→	}
   872→
   873→	// Get the most recent checkpoint
   874→	cp := session.Checkpoints[0]
   875→	if cp.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   876→		return ""
   877→	}
   878→
   879→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   880→	if err != nil {
   881→		return ""
   882→	}
   883→
   884→	content, err := store.ReadSessionContentByID(context.Background(), cp.CheckpointID, sessionID)
   885→	if err != nil || content == nil {
   886→		return ""
   887→	}
   888→
   889→	return content.Context
   890→}
   891→
   892→// GetCheckpointLog returns the session transcript for a specific checkpoint.
   893→// For auto-commit strategy, looks up checkpoint by ID on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch using the checkpoint store.
   894→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetCheckpointLog(cp Checkpoint) ([]byte, error) {
   895→	if cp.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   896→		return nil, ErrNoMetadata
   897→	}
   898→
   899→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   900→	if err != nil {
   901→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   902→	}
   903→
   904→	content, err := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), cp.CheckpointID)
   905→	if err != nil {
   906→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
   907→	}
   908→	if content == nil {
   909→		return nil, ErrNoMetadata
   910→	}
   911→
   912→	return content.Transcript, nil
   913→}
   914→
   915→// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session.
   916→// This is called during UserPromptSubmit hook to set up tracking for the session.
   917→// For auto-commit strategy, this creates a SessionState file in .git/entire-sessions/
   918→// to track CheckpointTranscriptStart (transcript offset) across checkpoints.
   919→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   920→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   921→// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as FirstPrompt for display).
   922→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) InitializeSession(sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) error {
   923→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   924→	if err != nil {
   925→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   926→	}
   927→
   928→	// Get current HEAD commit to track as base
   929→	head, err := repo.Head()
   930→	if err != nil {
   931→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   932→	}
   933→
   934→	baseCommit := head.Hash().String()
   935→
   936→	// Check if session state already exists (e.g., session resuming)
   937→	existing, err := LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   938→	if err != nil {
   939→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check existing session state: %w", err)
   940→	}
   941→	if existing != nil {
   942→		// Session already initialized — update last interaction time on every prompt submit
   943→		now := time.Now()
   944→		existing.LastInteractionTime = &now
   945→
   946→		// Generate a new TurnID for each turn (correlates carry-forward checkpoints)
   947→		turnID, err := id.Generate()
   948→		if err != nil {
   949→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate turn ID: %w", err)
   950→		}
   951→		existing.TurnID = turnID.String()
   952→		existing.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
   953→
   954→		// Backfill FirstPrompt if empty (for sessions
   955→		// created before the first_prompt field was added, or resumed sessions)
   956→		if existing.FirstPrompt == "" && userPrompt != "" {
   957→			existing.FirstPrompt = truncatePromptForStorage(userPrompt)
   958→		}
   959→
   960→		if err := SaveSessionState(existing); err != nil {
   961→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to update session state: %w", err)
   962→		}
   963→		return nil
   964→	}
   965→
   966→	// Generate TurnID for the first turn
   967→	turnID, err := id.Generate()
   968→	if err != nil {
   969→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate turn ID: %w", err)
   970→	}
   971→
   972→	// Create new session state
   973→	now := time.Now()
   974→	state := &SessionState{
   975→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   976→		CLIVersion:          buildinfo.Version,
   977→		BaseCommit:          baseCommit,
   978→		StartedAt:           now,
   979→		LastInteractionTime: &now,
   980→		TurnID:              turnID.String(),
   981→		StepCount:           0,
   982→		// CheckpointTranscriptStart defaults to 0 (start from beginning of transcript)
   983→		FilesTouched:   []string{},
   984→		AgentType:      agentType,
   985→		TranscriptPath: transcriptPath,
   986→		FirstPrompt:    truncatePromptForStorage(userPrompt),
   987→	}
   988→
   989→	if err := SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   990→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   991→	}
   992→
   993→	return nil
   994→}
   995→
   996→// ListOrphanedItems returns orphaned items created by the auto-commit strategy.
   997→// For auto-commit, checkpoints are orphaned when no commit has an Entire-Checkpoint
   998→// trailer referencing them (e.g., after rebasing or squashing).
   999→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) ListOrphanedItems() ([]CleanupItem, error) {
  1000→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1001→	if err != nil {
  1002→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
  1003→	}
  1004→
  1005→	// Get checkpoint store (lazily initialized)
  1006→	cpStore, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
  1007→	if err != nil {
  1008→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
  1009→	}
  1010→
  1011→	// Get all checkpoints from entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
  1012→	checkpoints, err := cpStore.ListCommitted(context.Background())
  1013→	if err != nil {
  1014→		return []CleanupItem{}, nil //nolint:nilerr // No checkpoints is not an error for cleanup
  1015→	}
  1016→
  1017→	if len(checkpoints) == 0 {
  1018→		return []CleanupItem{}, nil
  1019→	}
  1020→
  1021→	// Filter to only auto-commit checkpoints (identified by strategy in metadata)
  1022→	autoCommitCheckpoints := make(map[string]bool)
  1023→	for _, cp := range checkpoints {
  1024→		summary, readErr := cpStore.ReadCommitted(context.Background(), cp.CheckpointID)
  1025→		if readErr != nil || summary == nil {
  1026→			continue
  1027→		}
  1028→		// Only consider checkpoints created by this strategy
  1029→		if summary.Strategy == StrategyNameAutoCommit {
  1030→			autoCommitCheckpoints[cp.CheckpointID.String()] = true
  1031→		}
  1032→	}
  1033→
  1034→	if len(autoCommitCheckpoints) == 0 {
  1035→		return []CleanupItem{}, nil
  1036→	}
  1037→
  1038→	// Find checkpoint IDs referenced in commits
  1039→	referencedCheckpoints := s.findReferencedCheckpoints(repo)
  1040→
  1041→	// Find orphaned checkpoints
  1042→	var items []CleanupItem
  1043→	for checkpointID := range autoCommitCheckpoints {
  1044→		if !referencedCheckpoints[checkpointID] {
  1045→			items = append(items, CleanupItem{
  1046→				Type:   CleanupTypeCheckpoint,
  1047→				ID:     checkpointID,
  1048→				Reason: "no commit references this checkpoint",
  1049→			})
  1050→		}
  1051→	}
  1052→
  1053→	return items, nil
  1054→}
  1055→
  1056→// findReferencedCheckpoints scans commits for Entire-Checkpoint trailers.
  1057→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) findReferencedCheckpoints(repo *git.Repository) map[string]bool {
  1058→	referenced := make(map[string]bool)
  1059→
  1060→	refs, err := repo.References()
  1061→	if err != nil {
  1062→		return referenced
  1063→	}
  1064→
  1065→	visited := make(map[plumbing.Hash]bool)
  1066→
  1067→	_ = refs.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error { //nolint:errcheck // Best effort
  1068→		if !ref.Name().IsBranch() {
  1069→			return nil
  1070→		}
  1071→		// Skip entire/* branches
  1072→		branchName := strings.TrimPrefix(ref.Name().String(), "refs/heads/")
  1073→		if strings.HasPrefix(branchName, "entire/") {
  1074→			return nil
  1075→		}
  1076→
  1077→		iter, iterErr := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: ref.Hash()})
  1078→		if iterErr != nil {
  1079→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Best effort
  1080→		}
  1081→
  1082→		count := 0
  1083→		_ = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error { //nolint:errcheck // Best effort
  1084→			count++
  1085→			if count > 1000 {
  1086→				return errors.New("limit reached")
  1087→			}
  1088→			if visited[c.Hash] {
  1089→				return nil
  1090→			}
  1091→			visited[c.Hash] = true
  1092→
  1093→			if cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(c.Message); found {
  1094→				referenced[cpID.String()] = true
  1095→			}
  1096→			return nil
  1097→		})
  1098→		return nil
  1099→	})
  1100→
  1101→	return referenced
  1102→}
  1103→
  1104→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Standard pattern for strategy registration
  1105→func init() {
  1106→	// Register auto-commit as the primary strategy name
  1107→	Register(StrategyNameAutoCommit, NewAutoCommitStrategy)
  1108→}
  1109→
```

  "old_string": "// Read reads a trail by its ID from the entire/trails branch.\nfunc (s *Store) Read(trailID ID) (*Metadata, *Discussion, error) {\n\ttree, err := s.getBranchTree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tbasePath := trailID.Path() + \"/\"\n\n\t// Read metadata\n\tmetadataEntry, err := tree.FindEntry(basePath + metadataFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"trail %s not found: %w\", trailID, err)\n\t}\n\tmetadataBlob, err := s.repo.BlobObject(metadataEntry.Hash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read metadata blob: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tmetadataReader, err := metadataBlob.Reader()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open metadata reader: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer metadataReader.Close()\n\n\tvar metadata Metadata\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(metadataReader).Decode(&metadata); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to decode metadata: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Read discussion (optional, may not exist yet)\n\tvar discussion Discussion\n\tdiscussionEntry, err := tree.FindEntry(basePath + discussionFile)\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\tdiscussionBlob, blobErr := s.repo.BlobObject(discussionEntry.Hash)\n\t\tif blobErr == nil {\n\t\t\tdiscussionReader, readerErr := discussionBlob.Reader()\n\t\t\tif readerErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\t//nolint:errcheck,gosec // best-effort decode of optional discussion\n\t\t\t\tjson.NewDecoder(discussionReader).Decode(&discussion)\n\t\t\t\t_ = discussionReader.Close()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn &metadata, &discussion, nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// Read reads a trail by its ID from the entire/trails branch.\nfunc (s *Store) Read(trailID ID) (*Metadata, *Discussion, *Checkpoints, error) {\n\ttree, err := s.getBranchTree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tbasePath := trailID.Path() + \"/\"\n\n\t// Read metadata\n\tmetadataEntry, err := tree.FindEntry(basePath + metadataFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"trail %s not found: %w\", trailID, err)\n\t}\n\tmetadataBlob, err := s.repo.BlobObject(metadataEntry.Hash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read metadata blob: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tmetadataReader, err := metadataBlob.Reader()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open metadata reader: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer metadataReader.Close()\n\n\tvar metadata Metadata\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(metadataReader).Decode(&metadata); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to decode metadata: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Read discussion (optional, may not exist yet)\n\tvar discussion Discussion\n\tdiscussionEntry, err := tree.FindEntry(basePath + discussionFile)\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\tdiscussionBlob, blobErr := s.repo.BlobObject(discussionEntry.Hash)\n\t\tif blobErr == nil {\n\t\t\tdiscussionReader, readerErr := discussionBlob.Reader()\n\t\t\tif readerErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\t//nolint:errcheck,gosec // best-effort decode of optional discussion\n\t\t\t\tjson.NewDecoder(discussionReader).Decode(&discussion)\n\t\t\t\t_ = discussionReader.Close()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Read checkpoints (optional, may not exist yet)\n\tvar checkpoints Checkpoints\n\tcheckpointsEntry, err := tree.FindEntry(basePath + checkpointsFile)\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\tcheckpointsBlob, blobErr := s.repo.BlobObject(checkpointsEntry.Hash)\n\t\tif blobErr == nil {\n\t\t\tcheckpointsReader, readerErr := checkpointsBlob.Reader()\n\t\t\tif readerErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\t//nolint:errcheck,gosec // best-effort decode of optional checkpoints\n\t\t\t\tjson.NewDecoder(checkpointsReader).Decode(&checkpoints)\n\t\t\t\t_ = checkpointsReader.Close()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn &metadata, &discussion, &checkpoints, nil\n}"
}

  "new_string": "// AddCheckpoint prepends a checkpoint reference to a trail's checkpoints list (newest first).\nfunc (s *Store) AddCheckpoint(trailID ID, ref CheckpointRef) error {\n\tmetadata, discussion, checkpoints, err := s.Read(trailID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read trail for checkpoint update: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif checkpoints == nil {\n\t\tcheckpoints = &Checkpoints{Checkpoints: []CheckpointRef{}}\n\t}\n\n\t// Prepend new ref (newest first)\n\tcheckpoints.Checkpoints = append([]CheckpointRef{ref}, checkpoints.Checkpoints...)\n\n\treturn s.Write(metadata, discussion, checkpoints)\n}\n\n// Delete removes a trail from the entire/trails branch."
}
```

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01CiJLTvPsz9brb6B2i76LCy
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/trail_cmd.go",
  "old_string": "// AutoCreateTrail creates a trail automatically for the current branch if one doesn't exist.\n// This is called during session start for non-main branches.\nfunc AutoCreateTrail(repo *git.Repository, branchName, baseBranch string) error {\n\tstore := trail.NewStore(repo)\n\n\texisting, err := store.FindByBranch(branchName)\n\tif err == nil && existing != nil {\n\t\treturn nil // Trail already exists\n\t}\n\n\ttrailID, err := trail.GenerateID()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to generate trail ID: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tauthorName, _ := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\n\tmetadata := &trail.Metadata{\n\t\tTrailID:   trailID,\n\t\tBranch:    branchName,\n\t\tBase:      baseBranch,\n\t\tTitle:     trail.HumanizeBranchName(branchName),\n\t\tStatus:    trail.StatusInProgress,\n\t\tAuthor:    authorName,\n\t\tAssignees: []string{},\n\t\tLabels:    []string{},\n\t\tCreatedAt: now,\n\t\tUpdatedAt: now,\n\t}\n\n\tif err := store.Write(metadata, nil); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create trail: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[entire] Created trail for branch: %s\\n\", branchName)\n\treturn nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// AutoCreateTrail creates a trail automatically for the current branch if one doesn't exist.\n// This is called during session start for non-main branches.\n// If prompt is non-empty, its first line is used as the trail title instead of the branch name.\nfunc AutoCreateTrail(repo *git.Repository, branchName, baseBranch, prompt string) error {\n\tstore := trail.NewStore(repo)\n\n\texisting, err := store.FindByBranch(branchName)\n\tif err == nil && existing != nil {\n\t\treturn nil // Trail already exists\n\t}\n\n\ttrailID, err := trail.GenerateID()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to generate trail ID: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tauthorName, _ := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\n\ttitle := titleFromPrompt(prompt)\n\tif title == \"\" {\n\t\ttitle = trail.HumanizeBranchName(branchName)\n\t}\n\n\tmetadata := &trail.Metadata{\n\t\tTrailID:   trailID,\n\t\tBranch:    branchName,\n\t\tBase:      baseBranch,\n\t\tTitle:     title,\n\t\tStatus:    trail.StatusInProgress,\n\t\tAuthor:    authorName,\n\t\tAssignees: []string{},\n\t\tLabels:    []string{},\n\t\tCreatedAt: now,\n\t\tUpdatedAt: now,\n\t}\n\n\tif err := store.Write(metadata, nil, nil); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create trail: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[entire] Created trail for branch: %s\\n\", branchName)\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// titleFromPrompt extracts a trail title from the user's prompt.\n// Uses the first line, trimmed and truncated to 80 characters.\n// Returns empty string if prompt is empty.\nfunc titleFromPrompt(prompt string) string {\n\tif prompt == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\tline, _, _ := strings.Cut(prompt, \"\\n\")\n\ttitle := strings.TrimSpace(line)\n\tif len(title) > 80 {\n\t\ttitle = title[:77] + \"...\"\n\t}\n\treturn title\n}"
}
```

  "new_string": "\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"post-commit: carried forward remaining files\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", state.SessionID),\n\t\tslog.Int(\"remaining_files\", len(remainingFiles)),\n\t)\n}\n\n// appendCheckpointToTrail links a checkpoint to the trail for the current branch.\n// Best-effort: silently returns on any error (trails are non-critical metadata).\nfunc appendCheckpointToTrail(repo *git.Repository, cpID id.CheckpointID, commitSHA plumbing.Hash, prompts []string) {\n\tbranchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)\n\tif branchName == \"\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdefaultBranch := GetDefaultBranchName(repo)\n\tif branchName == defaultBranch {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tstore := trail.NewStore(repo)\n\texisting, err := store.FindByBranch(branchName)\n\tif err != nil || existing == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tvar summary string\n\tif len(prompts) > 0 {\n\t\tsummary = truncateForSummary(prompts[len(prompts)-1], 200)\n\t}\n\n\t_ = store.AddCheckpoint(existing.TrailID, trail.CheckpointRef{\n\t\tCheckpointID: cpID.String(),\n\t\tCommitSHA:    commitSHA.String(),\n\t\tCreatedAt:    time.Now().UTC(),\n\t\tSummary:      summary,\n\t})\n}\n\n// truncateForSummary truncates a string to maxLen, adding \"...\" if truncated.\n// Takes the first line only to keep summaries concise.\nfunc truncateForSummary(s string, maxLen int) string {\n\tline, _, _ := strings.Cut(s, \"\\n\")\n\tline = strings.TrimSpace(line)\n\tif len(line) <= maxLen {\n\t\treturn line\n\t}\n\tif maxLen <= 3 {\n\t\treturn line[:maxLen]\n\t}\n\treturn line[:maxLen-3] + \"...\"\n}"
}

   126→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) SaveStep(ctx StepContext) error {
   127→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   128→	if err != nil {
   129→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   130→	}
   131→
   132→	// Generate checkpoint ID for this commit
   133→	cpID, err := id.Generate()
   134→	if err != nil {
   135→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   136→	}
   137→	// Step 1: Commit code changes to active branch with checkpoint ID trailer
   138→	// We do code first to avoid orphaned metadata if this step fails.
   139→	// If metadata commit fails after this, the code commit exists but GetRewindPoints
   140→	// already handles missing metadata gracefully (skips commits without metadata).
   141→	codeResult, err := s.commitCodeToActive(repo, ctx, cpID)
   142→	if err != nil {
   143→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit code to active branch: %w", err)
   144→	}
   145→
   146→	// If no code commit was created (no changes), skip metadata creation
   147→	// This prevents orphaned metadata commits that don't correspond to any code commit
   148→	if !codeResult.Created {
   149→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   150→		logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint skipped (no changes)",
   151→			slog.String("strategy", "auto-commit"),
   152→			slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   153→		)
   154→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipped checkpoint (no changes since last commit)\n")
   155→		return nil
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	// Step 2: Commit metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch using sharded path
   159→	// Path is <checkpointID[:2]>/<checkpointID[2:]>/ for direct lookup
   160→	_, err = s.commitMetadataToMetadataBranch(repo, ctx, cpID)
   161→	if err != nil {
   162→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch: %w", err)
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	// Log checkpoint creation
   166→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   167→	logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint saved",
   168→		slog.String("strategy", "auto-commit"),
   169→		slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   170→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
   171→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(ctx.ModifiedFiles)),
   172→		slog.Int("new_files", len(ctx.NewFiles)),
   173→		slog.Int("deleted_files", len(ctx.DeletedFiles)),
   174→	)
   175→
   176→	return nil
   177→}

  "old_string": "// autoCreateTrailOnTurnStart creates a trail if on a non-main branch without one.\n// Non-blocking: logs errors but doesn't fail the session.\nfunc autoCreateTrailOnTurnStart() {\n\tif settings.IsTrailsDisabled() {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tisDefault, branchName, err := IsOnDefaultBranch()\n\tif err != nil || isDefault || branchName == \"\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\trepo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tbaseBranch := strategy.GetDefaultBranchName(repo)\n\tif baseBranch == \"\" {\n\t\tbaseBranch = defaultBaseBranch\n\t}\n\n\tif err := AutoCreateTrail(repo, branchName, baseBranch); err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to auto-create trail: %v\\n\", err)\n\t}\n}",
  "new_string": "// autoCreateTrailOnTurnStart creates a trail if on a non-main branch without one.\n// Non-blocking: logs errors but doesn't fail the session.\n// prompt is the user's prompt text, used for the trail title.\nfunc autoCreateTrailOnTurnStart(prompt string) {\n\tif settings.IsTrailsDisabled() {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tisDefault, branchName, err := IsOnDefaultBranch()\n\tif err != nil || isDefault || branchName == \"\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\trepo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tbaseBranch := strategy.GetDefaultBranchName(repo)\n\tif baseBranch == \"\" {\n\t\tbaseBranch = defaultBaseBranch\n\t}\n\n\tif err := AutoCreateTrail(repo, branchName, baseBranch, prompt); err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to auto-create trail: %v\\n\", err)\n\t}\n}"
}

    14→// initTestRepo creates a test git repository with an initial commit.
    15→func initTestRepo(t *testing.T) *git.Repository {
    16→	t.Helper()
    17→
    18→	dir := t.TempDir()
    19→
    20→	ctx := context.Background()
    21→	cmds := [][]string{
    22→		{"git", "init", dir},
    23→		{"git", "-C", dir, "config", "user.name", "Test"},
    24→		{"git", "-C", dir, "config", "user.email", "test@test.com"},
    25→	}
    26→	for _, args := range cmds {
    27→		cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, args[0], args[1:]...)
    28→		if out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
    29→			t.Fatalf("command %v failed: %v\n%s", args, err, out)
    30→		}
    31→	}
    32→
    33→	// Create a file and commit
    34→	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "README.md"), []byte("# Test"), 0o644); err != nil {
    35→		t.Fatalf("failed to write file: %v", err)
    36→	}
    37→	commitCmds := [][]string{
    38→		{"git", "-C", dir, "add", "."},
    39→		{"git", "-C", dir, "commit", "-m", "Initial commit"},
    40→	}
    41→	for _, args := range commitCmds {
    42→		cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, args[0], args[1:]...)
    43→		if out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
    44→			t.Fatalf("command %v failed: %v\n%s", args, err, out)
    45→		}
    46→	}
    47→
    48→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
    49→	if err != nil {
    50→		t.Fatalf("failed to open repo: %v", err)
    51→	}
    52→	return repo
    53→}
    54→
    55→func TestStore_EnsureBranch(t *testing.T) {
    56→	t.Parallel()
    57→	repo := initTestRepo(t)
    58→	store := NewStore(repo)
    59→
    60→	// First call should create the branch
    61→	if err := store.EnsureBranch(); err != nil {
    62→		t.Fatalf("EnsureBranch() error = %v", err)
    63→	}
    64→
    65→	// Second call should be idempotent
    66→	if err := store.EnsureBranch(); err != nil {
    67→		t.Fatalf("EnsureBranch() second call error = %v", err)
    68→	}
    69→}
    70→
    71→func TestStore_WriteAndRead(t *testing.T) {
    72→	t.Parallel()
    73→	repo := initTestRepo(t)
    74→	store := NewStore(repo)
    75→
    76→	trailID, err := GenerateID()
    77→	if err != nil {
    78→		t.Fatalf("GenerateID() error = %v", err)
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	now := time.Now().Truncate(time.Second)
    82→	metadata := &Metadata{
    83→		TrailID:     trailID,
    84→		Branch:      "feature/test",
    85→		Base:        "main",
    86→		Title:       "Test trail",
    87→		Description: "A test trail",
    88→		Status:      StatusDraft,
    89→		Author:      "tester",
    90→		Assignees:   []string{},
    91→		Labels:      []string{"test"},
    92→		CreatedAt:   now,
    93→		UpdatedAt:   now,
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	discussion := &Discussion{Comments: []Comment{}}
    97→
    98→	if err := store.Write(metadata, discussion); err != nil {
    99→		t.Fatalf("Write() error = %v", err)
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	// Read it back
   103→	gotMeta, gotDisc, err := store.Read(trailID)
   104→	if err != nil {
   105→		t.Fatalf("Read() error = %v", err)
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	if gotMeta.TrailID != trailID {
   109→		t.Errorf("Read() trail_id = %s, want %s", gotMeta.TrailID, trailID)
   110→	}
   111→	if gotMeta.Branch != "feature/test" {
   112→		t.Errorf("Read() branch = %q, want %q", gotMeta.Branch, "feature/test")
   113→	}
   114→	if gotMeta.Title != "Test trail" {
   115→		t.Errorf("Read() title = %q, want %q", gotMeta.Title, "Test trail")
   116→	}
   117→	if gotMeta.Status != StatusDraft {
   118→		t.Errorf("Read() status = %q, want %q", gotMeta.Status, StatusDraft)
   119→	}
   120→	if len(gotMeta.Labels) != 1 || gotMeta.Labels[0] != "test" {

   122→	}

   128→func TestStore_FindByBranch(t *testing.T) {
   129→	t.Parallel()
   130→	repo := initTestRepo(t)
   131→	store := NewStore(repo)

   138→		if err != nil {
   139→			t.Fatalf("GenerateID() error = %v", err)
   140→		}
   141→		meta := &Metadata{
   142→			TrailID:   id,
   143→			Branch:    branch,
   144→			Base:      "main",
   145→			Title:     HumanizeBranchName(branch),
   146→			Status:    StatusDraft,
   147→			Author:    "test",
   148→			Assignees: []string{},
   149→			Labels:    []string{},
   150→			CreatedAt: now,
   151→			UpdatedAt: now,
   152→		}
   153→		if err := store.Write(meta, nil); err != nil {
   154→			t.Fatalf("Write() error = %v", err)
   155→		}
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	// Find by branch
   159→	found, err := store.FindByBranch("feature/a")
   160→	if err != nil {
   161→		t.Fatalf("FindByBranch() error = %v", err)
   162→	}
   163→	if found == nil {
   164→		t.Fatal("FindByBranch() returned nil, expected trail")
   165→	}
   166→	if found.Branch != "feature/a" {
   167→		t.Errorf("FindByBranch() branch = %q, want %q", found.Branch, "feature/a")
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	// Not found
   171→	notFound, err := store.FindByBranch("feature/c")
   172→	if err != nil {
   173→		t.Fatalf("FindByBranch() error = %v", err)
   174→	}
   175→	if notFound != nil {
   176→		t.Error("FindByBranch() should return nil for non-existent branch")
   177→	}
   178→}
   179→
   180→func TestStore_List(t *testing.T) {
   181→	t.Parallel()
   182→	repo := initTestRepo(t)
   183→	store := NewStore(repo)
   184→
   185→	// List when no trails exist (branch doesn't exist yet)
   186→	trails, err := store.List()
   187→	if err != nil {
   188→		t.Fatalf("List() error = %v", err)
   189→	}
   190→	if trails != nil {
   191→		t.Errorf("List() = %v, want nil for empty store", trails)
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	// Create a trail
   195→	now := time.Now()
   196→	id, err := GenerateID()
   197→	if err != nil {
   198→		t.Fatalf("GenerateID() error = %v", err)
   199→	}
   200→	meta := &Metadata{
   201→		TrailID:   id,
   202→		Branch:    "feature/test",
   203→		Base:      "main",
   204→		Title:     "Test",
   205→		Status:    StatusDraft,
   206→		Author:    "test",
   207→		Assignees: []string{},
   208→		Labels:    []string{},
   209→		CreatedAt: now,
   210→		UpdatedAt: now,
   211→	}
   212→	if err := store.Write(meta, nil); err != nil {
   213→		t.Fatalf("Write() error = %v", err)
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	trails, err = store.List()
   217→	if err != nil {
   218→		t.Fatalf("List() error = %v", err)
   219→	}
   220→	if len(trails) != 1 {
   221→		t.Fatalf("List() returned %d trails, want 1", len(trails))
   222→	}
   223→	if trails[0].TrailID != id {
   224→		t.Errorf("List()[0].TrailID = %s, want %s", trails[0].TrailID, id)
   225→	}
   226→}
   227→
   228→func TestStore_Update(t *testing.T) {
   229→	t.Parallel()
   230→	repo := initTestRepo(t)
   231→	store := NewStore(repo)
   232→
   233→	now := time.Now()
   234→	id, err := GenerateID()
   235→	if err != nil {
   236→		t.Fatalf("GenerateID() error = %v", err)
   237→	}
   238→	meta := &Metadata{
   239→		TrailID:   id,
   240→		Branch:    "feature/test",
   241→		Base:      "main",
   242→		Title:     "Original",
   243→		Status:    StatusDraft,
   244→		Author:    "test",
   245→		Assignees: []string{},
   246→		Labels:    []string{},
   247→		CreatedAt: now,
   248→		UpdatedAt: now,
   249→	}
   250→	if err := store.Write(meta, nil); err != nil {
   251→		t.Fatalf("Write() error = %v", err)
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	// Update
   255→	if err := store.Update(id, func(m *Metadata) {
   256→		m.Title = "Updated"
   257→		m.Status = StatusInProgress
   258→		m.Labels = []string{"urgent"}
   259→	}); err != nil {
   260→		t.Fatalf("Update() error = %v", err)
   261→	}
   262→
   263→	// Verify
   264→	updated, _, err := store.Read(id)
   265→	if err != nil {
   266→		t.Fatalf("Read() error = %v", err)
   267→	}
   268→	if updated.Title != "Updated" {
   269→		t.Errorf("Read() title = %q, want %q", updated.Title, "Updated")
   270→	}
   271→	if updated.Status != StatusInProgress {
   272→		t.Errorf("Read() status = %q, want %q", updated.Status, StatusInProgress)
   273→	}
   274→	if len(updated.Labels) != 1 || updated.Labels[0] != "urgent" {
   275→		t.Errorf("Read() labels = %v, want [urgent]", updated.Labels)
   276→	}
   277→	if !updated.UpdatedAt.After(now) {
   278→		t.Error("Read() updated_at should be after original")
   279→	}
   280→}
   281→
   282→func TestStore_Delete(t *testing.T) {
   283→	t.Parallel()
   284→	repo := initTestRepo(t)
   285→	store := NewStore(repo)
   286→
   287→	now := time.Now()
   288→	id, err := GenerateID()
   289→	if err != nil {
   290→		t.Fatalf("GenerateID() error = %v", err)
   291→	}
   292→	meta := &Metadata{
   293→		TrailID:   id,
   294→		Branch:    "feature/test",
   295→		Base:      "main",
   296→		Title:     "To delete",
   297→		Status:    StatusDraft,
   298→		Author:    "test",
   299→		Assignees: []string{},
   300→		Labels:    []string{},
   301→		CreatedAt: now,
   302→		UpdatedAt: now,
   303→	}
   304→	if err := store.Write(meta, nil); err != nil {
   305→		t.Fatalf("Write() error = %v", err)
   306→	}
   307→
   308→	// Delete
   309→	if err := store.Delete(id); err != nil {
   310→		t.Fatalf("Delete() error = %v", err)
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	// Verify it's gone
   314→	_, _, err = store.Read(id)
   315→	if err == nil {
   316→		t.Error("Read() should fail after delete")
   317→	}
   318→}
   319→
   320→func TestStore_ReadNonExistent(t *testing.T) {
   321→	t.Parallel()
   322→	repo := initTestRepo(t)
   323→	store := NewStore(repo)
   324→
   325→	if err := store.EnsureBranch(); err != nil {
   326→		t.Fatalf("EnsureBranch() error = %v", err)
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	_, _, err := store.Read(ID("abcdef123456"))
   330→	if err == nil {
   331→		t.Error("Read() should fail for non-existent trail")
   332→	}
   333→}
   334→

## Summary

### Part A: `checkpoints.json` for trails

**`cmd/entire/cli/trail/trail.go`** - Added `CheckpointRef` and `Checkpoints` types to represent checkpoint references linked to trails.

**`cmd/entire/cli/trail/store.go`**:
- Added `checkpointsFile` constant
- Updated `Write()` to accept a `*Checkpoints` parameter (writes empty `{"checkpoints":[]}` when nil)
- Updated `Read()` to return `*Checkpoints` as 4th return value (reads best-effort)
- Updated `Update()` to preserve existing checkpoints through read/write cycle
- Added `AddCheckpoint()` method that prepends a new ref (newest first)

**`cmd/entire/cli/trail/store_test.go`** - Updated all `Write()` and `Read()` calls to match new signatures.

**`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_types.go`** - Added `Prompts []string` field to `CondenseResult`.

**`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go`** - Populated `Prompts` from `sessionData.Prompts` in the `CondenseResult`.

**`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`**:
- Added `appendCheckpointToTrail()` helper (finds trail by branch, prepends checkpoint ref)
- Added `truncateForSummary()` helper
- Called `appendCheckpointToTrail()` after successful `CondenseSession()` in `condenseAndUpdateState()`

**`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/auto_commit.go`** - Called `appendCheckpointToTrail()` after successful metadata commit in `SaveStep()`.

### Part B: Semantic trail titles

**`cmd/entire/cli/lifecycle.go`** - `autoCreateTrailOnTurnStart()` now accepts and passes `event.Prompt` through to `AutoCreateTrail()`.

**`cmd/entire/cli/trail_cmd.go`**:
- `AutoCreateTrail()` now accepts a `prompt string` parameter
- Added `titleFromPrompt()` helper (first line of prompt, max 80 chars)
- Trail title uses prompt-derived title when available, falls back to `HumanizeBranchName()`
- Updated all `store.Write()` calls to pass `nil` for checkpoints

**`CLAUDE.md`** - Documented `checkpoints.json` format, checkpoint linking behavior, and prompt-based title generation.
