```
   736→		// Record checkpoint ID for ACTIVE sessions so HandleTurnEnd can finalize
   737→		// with full transcript. IDLE/ENDED sessions already have complete transcripts.
   738→		// NOTE: This check runs AFTER TransitionAndLog updated the phase. It relies on
   739→		// ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE (phase stays ACTIVE). If that state machine
   740→		// transition ever changed, this guard would silently stop recording IDs.
   741→		if handler.condensed && state.Phase.IsActive() {
   742→			state.TurnCheckpointIDs = append(state.TurnCheckpointIDs, checkpointID.String())
   743→		}
   744→
   745→		// Carry forward remaining uncommitted files so the next commit gets its
   746→		// own checkpoint ID. This applies to ALL phases — if a user splits their
   747→		// commit across two `git commit` invocations, each gets a 1:1 checkpoint.
   748→		// Uses content-aware comparison: if user did `git add -p` and committed
   749→		// partial changes, the file still has remaining agent changes to carry forward.
   750→		if handler.condensed {
   751→			remainingFiles := filesWithRemainingAgentChanges(repo, shadowBranchName, commit, filesTouchedBefore, committedFileSet)
   752→			state.FilesTouched = remainingFiles
   753→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward decision (content-aware)",
   754→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   755→				slog.Int("files_touched_before", len(filesTouchedBefore)),
   756→				slog.Int("committed_files", len(committedFileSet)),
   757→				slog.Int("remaining_files", len(remainingFiles)),
   758→				slog.Any("remaining", remainingFiles),
   759→				slog.Any("committed_files", committedFileSet),
   760→			)
   761→			if len(remainingFiles) > 0 {
   762→				s.carryForwardToNewShadowBranch(logCtx, repo, state, remainingFiles)
   763→			}
   764→		}
   765→
   766→		// Save the updated state
   767→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   768→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
   769→		}
   770→
   771→		// Only preserve shadow branch for active sessions that were NOT condensed.
   772→		// Condensed sessions already have their data on entire/checkpoints/v1.
   773→		if state.Phase.IsActive() && !handler.condensed {
   774→			uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] = true
   775→		}
   776→	}
   777→
   778→	// Clean up shadow branches — only delete when ALL sessions on the branch are non-active
   779→	// or were condensed during this PostCommit.
   780→	for shadowBranchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {
   781→		if uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] {
   782→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: preserving shadow branch (active session exists)",
   783→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   784→			)
   785→			continue
   786→		}
   787→		if err := deleteShadowBranch(repo, shadowBranchName); err != nil {
   788→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to clean up %s: %v\n", shadowBranchName, err)
   789→		} else {
   790→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Cleaned up shadow branch: %s\n", shadowBranchName)
   791→			logging.Info(logCtx, "shadow branch deleted",
   792→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   793→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   794→			)
   795→		}
   796→	}
   797→
   798→	return nil
   799→}
   800→
   801→// condenseAndUpdateState runs condensation for a session and updates state afterward.
   802→// Returns true if condensation succeeded.
   803→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) condenseAndUpdateState(
   804→	logCtx context.Context,
   805→	repo *git.Repository,
   806→	checkpointID id.CheckpointID,
   807→	state *SessionState,
   808→	head *plumbing.Reference,
   809→	shadowBranchName string,
   810→	shadowBranchesToDelete map[string]struct{},
   811→	committedFiles map[string]struct{},
   812→) bool {
   813→	result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state, committedFiles)
   814→	if err != nil {
   815→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: condensation failed for session %s: %v\n",
   816→			state.SessionID, err)
   817→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: condensation failed",
   818→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   819→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   820→		)
   821→		return false
   822→	}
   823→
   824→	// Link checkpoint to trail (best-effort)
   825→	appendCheckpointToTrail(repo, result.CheckpointID, head.Hash(), result.Prompts)
   826→
   827→	// Track this shadow branch for cleanup
   828→	shadowBranchesToDelete[shadowBranchName] = struct{}{}
   829→
   830→	// Update session state for the new base commit
   831→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   832→	state.BaseCommit = newHead
   833→	state.AttributionBaseCommit = newHead
   834→	state.StepCount = 0
   835→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   836→
   837→	// Clear attribution tracking — condensation already used these values
   838→	state.PromptAttributions = nil
   839→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   840→	state.FilesTouched = nil
   841→
   842→	// Save checkpoint ID so subsequent commits can reuse it (e.g., amend restores trailer)
   843→	state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   844→
   845→	shortID := state.SessionID
   846→	if len(shortID) > 8 {
   847→		shortID = shortID[:8]
   848→	}
   849→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Condensed session %s: %s (%d checkpoints)\n",
   850→		shortID, result.CheckpointID, result.CheckpointsCount)
   851→
   852→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session condensed",
   853→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   854→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   855→		slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   856→		slog.Int("transcript_lines", result.TotalTranscriptLines),
   857→	)
   858→
   859→	return true
   860→}
   861→
   862→// updateBaseCommitIfChanged updates BaseCommit to newHead if it changed.
   863→// Only updates ACTIVE sessions. IDLE/ENDED sessions should NOT have their
   864→// BaseCommit updated, as this would cause them to be incorrectly associated
   865→// with a new shadow branch and potentially condensed on future commits.
   866→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx context.Context, state *SessionState, newHead string) {
   867→	// Only update ACTIVE sessions. IDLE/ENDED sessions are kept around for
   868→	// LastCheckpointID reuse and should not be advanced to HEAD.
   869→	if !state.Phase.IsActive() {
   870→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: updateBaseCommitIfChanged skipped non-active session",
   871→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   872→			slog.String("phase", string(state.Phase)),
   873→		)
   874→		return
   875→	}
   876→	if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   877→		state.BaseCommit = newHead
   878→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: updated BaseCommit",
   879→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   880→			slog.String("new_head", truncateHash(newHead)),
   881→		)
   882→	}
   883→}
   884→
   885→// postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly updates BaseCommit for all sessions on the current
   886→// worktree when a commit has no Entire-Checkpoint trailer. This prevents BaseCommit
   887→// from going stale, which would cause future PrepareCommitMsg calls to skip the
   888→// session (BaseCommit != currentHeadHash filter).
   889→//
   890→// Unlike the full PostCommit flow, this does NOT fire EventGitCommit or trigger
   891→// condensation — it only keeps BaseCommit in sync with HEAD.
   892→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly(logCtx context.Context, head *plumbing.Reference) {
   893→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   894→	if err != nil {
   895→		return // Silent failure — hooks must be resilient
   896→	}
   897→
   898→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   899→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   900→		return
   901→	}
   902→
   903→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   904→	for _, state := range sessions {
   905→		// Only update active sessions. Idle/ended sessions are kept around for
   906→		// LastCheckpointID reuse and should not be advanced to HEAD.
   907→		if !state.Phase.IsActive() {
   908→			continue
   909→		}
   910→		if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   911→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit (no trailer): updating BaseCommit",
   912→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   913→				slog.String("old_base", truncateHash(state.BaseCommit)),
   914→				slog.String("new_head", truncateHash(newHead)),
   915→			)
   916→			state.BaseCommit = newHead
   917→			if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   918→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
   919→			}
   920→		}
   921→	}
   922→}
   923→
   924→// truncateHash safely truncates a git hash to 7 chars for logging.
   925→func truncateHash(h string) string {
   926→	if len(h) > 7 {
   927→		return h[:7]
   928→	}
   929→	return h
   930→}
   931→
   932→// filterSessionsWithNewContent returns sessions that have new transcript content
   933→// beyond what was already condensed.
   934→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo *git.Repository, sessions []*SessionState) []*SessionState {
   935→	var result []*SessionState
   936→
   937→	for _, state := range sessions {
   938→		hasNew, err := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
   939→		if err != nil {
   940→			// On error, include the session (fail open for hooks)
   941→			result = append(result, state)
   942→			continue
   943→		}
   944→		if hasNew {
   945→			result = append(result, state)
   946→		}
   947→	}
   948→
   949→	return result
   950→}
   951→
   952→// sessionHasNewContent checks if a session has new transcript content
   953→// beyond what was already condensed.
   954→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContent(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
   955→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "manual-commit")
   956→
   957→	// Get shadow branch
   958→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   959→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   960→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   961→	if err != nil {
   962→		// No shadow branch means no Stop has happened since the last condensation.
   963→		// However, the agent may have done work (including commits) without a Stop.
   964→		// Check the live transcript to detect this scenario.
   965→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: no shadow branch, checking live transcript",
   966→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   967→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   968→		)
   969→		return s.sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo, state)
   970→	}
   971→
   972→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   973→	if err != nil {
   974→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   975→	}
   976→
   977→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   978→	if err != nil {
   979→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   980→	}
   981→
   982→	// Look for transcript file
   983→	metadataDir := paths.EntireMetadataDir + "/" + state.SessionID
   984→	var transcriptLines int
   985→	var hasTranscriptFile bool
   986→
   987→	if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   988→		hasTranscriptFile = true
   989→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   990→			transcriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, content)
   991→		}
   992→	} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   993→		hasTranscriptFile = true
   994→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   995→			transcriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, content)
   996→		}
   997→	}
   998→
   999→	// If shadow branch exists but has no transcript (e.g., carry-forward from mid-session commit),
  1000→	// check if the session has FilesTouched. Carry-forward sets FilesTouched with remaining files.
  1001→	if !hasTranscriptFile {
  1002→		if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 {
  1003→			// Shadow branch has files from carry-forward - check if staged files overlap
  1004→			// AND have matching content (content-aware check).
  1005→			stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
  1006→			if len(stagedFiles) > 0 {
  1007→				// PrepareCommitMsg context: check staged files overlap with content
  1008→				result := stagedFilesOverlapWithContent(repo, tree, stagedFiles, state.FilesTouched)
  1009→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: no transcript, carry-forward with staged files",
  1010→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1011→					slog.Int("files_touched", len(state.FilesTouched)),
  1012→					slog.Int("staged_files", len(stagedFiles)),
  1013→					slog.Bool("result", result),
  1014→				)
  1015→				return result, nil
  1016→			}
  1017→			// PostCommit context: no staged files, but we have carry-forward files.
  1018→			// Return true and let the caller do the overlap check with committed files.
  1019→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: no transcript, carry-forward without staged files (post-commit context)",
  1020→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1021→				slog.Int("files_touched", len(state.FilesTouched)),
  1022→			)
  1023→			return true, nil
  1024→		}
  1025→		// No transcript and no FilesTouched - fall back to live transcript check
  1026→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: no transcript and no files touched, checking live transcript",
  1027→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1028→		)
  1029→		return s.sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo, state)
  1030→	}
  1031→
  1032→	// Check if there's new content to condense. Two cases:
  1033→	// 1. Transcript has grown since last condensation (new prompts/responses)
  1034→	// 2. FilesTouched has files not yet committed (carry-forward scenario)
  1035→	//
  1036→	// For PrepareCommitMsg context, we verify staged files overlap with session's files
  1037→	// using content-aware matching to detect reverted files.
  1038→	// For PostCommit context, getStagedFiles() is empty (files already committed),
  1039→	// so we return true and let the caller do the overlap check via filesOverlapWithContent.
  1040→
  1041→	hasTranscriptGrowth := transcriptLines > state.CheckpointTranscriptStart
  1042→	hasUncommittedFiles := len(state.FilesTouched) > 0
  1043→
  1044→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: transcript check",
  1045→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1046→		slog.Int("transcript_lines", transcriptLines),
  1047→		slog.Int("checkpoint_transcript_start", state.CheckpointTranscriptStart),
  1048→		slog.Bool("has_transcript_growth", hasTranscriptGrowth),
  1049→		slog.Bool("has_uncommitted_files", hasUncommittedFiles),
  1050→	)
  1051→
  1052→	if !hasTranscriptGrowth && !hasUncommittedFiles {
  1053→		return false, nil // No new content and no carry-forward files
  1054→	}
  1055→
  1056→	// Check if staged files overlap with session's files with content-aware matching.
  1057→	// This is primarily for PrepareCommitMsg; in PostCommit, stagedFiles is empty.
  1058→	stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
  1059→	if len(stagedFiles) > 0 {
  1060→		result := stagedFilesOverlapWithContent(repo, tree, stagedFiles, state.FilesTouched)
  1061→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: staged files overlap check",
  1062→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1063→			slog.Int("staged_files", len(stagedFiles)),
  1064→			slog.Bool("result", result),
  1065→		)
  1066→		return result, nil
  1067→	}
  1068→
  1069→	// No staged files - either PostCommit context or edge case.
  1070→	// Return transcript growth status. For PostCommit with hasTranscriptFile=true,
  1071→	// if there's no transcript growth, the session hasn't done new work since last checkpoint.
  1072→	// (Carry-forward creates a shadow branch WITHOUT transcript, handled in the block above.)
  1073→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: no staged files, returning transcript growth",
  1074→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1075→		slog.Bool("has_transcript_growth", hasTranscriptGrowth),
  1076→		slog.Bool("has_uncommitted_files", hasUncommittedFiles),
  1077→	)
  1078→	return hasTranscriptGrowth, nil
  1079→}
  1080→
  1081→// sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript checks if a session has new content
  1082→// by examining the live transcript file. This is used when no shadow branch exists
  1083→// (i.e., no Stop has happened yet) but the agent may have done work.
  1084→//
  1085→// Returns true if:
  1086→//  1. The transcript has grown since the last condensation, AND
  1087→//  2. The new transcript portion contains file modifications, AND
  1088→//  3. At least one modified file overlaps with the currently staged files
  1089→//
  1090→// The overlap check ensures we don't add checkpoint trailers to commits that are
  1091→// unrelated to the agent's recent changes.
  1092→//
  1093→// This handles the scenario where the agent commits mid-session before Stop.
  1094→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
  1095→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1096→
  1097→	modifiedFiles, ok := s.extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state)
  1098→	if !ok || len(modifiedFiles) == 0 {
  1099→		return false, nil
  1100→	}
  1101→
  1102→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: found file modifications",
  1103→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1104→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(modifiedFiles)),
  1105→	)
  1106→
  1107→	// Check if any modified files overlap with currently staged files
  1108→	// This ensures we only add checkpoint trailers to commits that include
  1109→	// files the agent actually modified
  1110→	stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
  1111→
  1112→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: comparing staged vs modified",
  1113→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1114→		slog.Int("staged_files", len(stagedFiles)),
  1115→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(modifiedFiles)),
  1116→	)
  1117→
  1118→	if !hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, modifiedFiles) {
  1119→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: no overlap between staged and modified files",
  1120→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1121→		)
  1122→		return false, nil // No overlap - staged files are unrelated to agent's work
  1123→	}
  1124→
  1125→	return true, nil
  1126→}
  1127→
  1128→// extractFilesFromLiveTranscript extracts modified file paths from the live transcript.
  1129→// Returns empty slice if extraction fails (fail-open behavior for hooks).
  1130→// Extracts files from the transcript starting at CheckpointTranscriptStart, which gives
  1131→// files touched since the last condensation — used for carry-forward computation.
  1132→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractFilesFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState) []string {
  1133→	return s.extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
  1134→}
  1135→
  1136→// extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript extracts modified files from the live
  1137→// transcript that are NEW since the last condensation. Returns the normalized file list
  1138→// and whether the extraction succeeded. Used by sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript
  1139→// to detect agent work.
  1140→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractNewModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState) ([]string, bool) {
  1141→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1142→
  1143→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" || state.AgentType == "" {
  1144→		return nil, false
  1145→	}
  1146→
  1147→	ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(state.AgentType)
  1148→	if err != nil {
  1149→		return nil, false
  1150→	}
  1151→
  1152→	analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer)
  1153→	if !ok {
  1154→		return nil, false
  1155→	}
  1156→
  1157→	// Check if transcript has grown since last condensation
  1158→	currentPos, err := analyzer.GetTranscriptPosition(state.TranscriptPath)
  1159→	if err != nil {
  1160→		return nil, false
  1161→	}
  1162→	if currentPos <= state.CheckpointTranscriptStart {
  1163→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "live transcript check: no new content",
  1164→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1165→			slog.Int("current_pos", currentPos),
  1166→			slog.Int("start_offset", state.CheckpointTranscriptStart),
  1167→		)
  1168→		return nil, true // No new content, but extraction succeeded
  1169→	}
  1170→
  1171→	return s.extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart), true
  1172→}
  1173→
  1174→// extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript extracts modified files from the live transcript
  1175→// (including subagent transcripts) starting from the given offset, and normalizes them
  1176→// to repo-relative paths. Returns the normalized file list.
  1177→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState, offset int) []string {
  1178→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1179→
  1180→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" || state.AgentType == "" {
  1181→		return nil
  1182→	}
  1183→
  1184→	ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(state.AgentType)
  1185→	if err != nil {
  1186→		return nil
  1187→	}
  1188→
  1189→	analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer)
  1190→	if !ok {
  1191→		return nil
  1192→	}
  1193→
  1194→	var modifiedFiles []string
  1195→
  1196→	// For Claude Code, use ExtractAllModifiedFiles which parses the main transcript
  1197→	// AND subagent transcripts in a single pass, avoiding redundant parsing.
  1198→	if state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode {
  1199→		subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(state.TranscriptPath), state.SessionID, "subagents")
  1200→		allFiles, extractErr := claudecode.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(state.TranscriptPath, offset, subagentsDir)
  1201→		if extractErr != nil {
  1202→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript: extraction failed",
  1203→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1204→				slog.String("error", extractErr.Error()),
  1205→			)
  1206→		} else {
  1207→			modifiedFiles = allFiles
  1208→		}
  1209→	} else {
  1210→		files, _, err := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(state.TranscriptPath, offset)
  1211→		if err != nil {
  1212→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript: main transcript extraction failed",
  1213→				slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
  1214→				slog.Any("error", err),
  1215→			)
  1216→		} else {
  1217→			modifiedFiles = files
  1218→		}
  1219→	}
  1220→
  1221→	if len(modifiedFiles) == 0 {
  1222→		return nil
  1223→	}
  1224→
  1225→	// Normalize to repo-relative paths.
  1226→	// Transcript tool_use entries contain absolute paths (e.g., /Users/alex/project/src/main.go)
  1227→	// but getStagedFiles/committedFiles use repo-relative paths (e.g., src/main.go).
  1228→	basePath := state.WorktreePath
  1229→	if basePath == "" {
  1230→		if wp, wpErr := GetWorktreePath(); wpErr == nil {
  1231→			basePath = wp
  1232→		}
  1233→	}
  1234→	if basePath != "" {
  1235→		normalized := make([]string, 0, len(modifiedFiles))
  1236→		for _, f := range modifiedFiles {
  1237→			if rel := paths.ToRelativePath(f, basePath); rel != "" {
  1238→				normalized = append(normalized, rel)
  1239→			} else {
  1240→				normalized = append(normalized, f)
  1241→			}
  1242→		}
  1243→		modifiedFiles = normalized
  1244→	}
  1245→
  1246→	return modifiedFiles
  1247→}
  1248→
  1249→// addTrailerForAgentCommit handles the fast path when an agent is committing
  1250→// (ACTIVE session + no TTY). Generates a checkpoint ID and adds the trailer
  1251→// directly, bypassing content detection and interactive prompts.
  1252→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) addTrailerForAgentCommit(logCtx context.Context, commitMsgFile string, state *SessionState, source string) error {
  1253→	cpID, err := id.Generate()
  1254→	if err != nil {
  1255→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
  1256→	}
  1257→
  1258→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
  1259→	if err != nil {
  1260→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
  1261→	}
  1262→
  1263→	message := string(content)
  1264→
  1265→	// Don't add if trailer already exists
  1266→	if _, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
  1267→		return nil
  1268→	}
  1269→
  1270→	message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, cpID)
  1271→
  1272→	logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: agent commit trailer added",
  1273→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
  1274→		slog.String("source", source),
  1275→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
  1276→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1277→	)
  1278→
  1279→	if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
  1280→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
  1281→	}
  1282→	return nil
  1283→}
  1284→
  1285→// addCheckpointTrailer adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer to a commit message.
  1286→// Handles proper trailer formatting (blank line before trailers if needed).
  1287→func addCheckpointTrailer(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) string {
  1288→	trailer := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
  1289→
  1290→	// If message already ends with trailers (lines starting with key:), just append
  1291→	// Otherwise, add a blank line first
  1292→	lines := strings.Split(strings.TrimRight(message, "\n"), "\n")
  1293→
  1294→	// Check if the message already ends with a trailer paragraph.
  1295→	// Git trailers must be in a separate paragraph (preceded by a blank line).
  1296→	// A single-paragraph message (e.g., just a subject line) cannot have trailers,
  1297→	// even if the subject contains ": " (like conventional commits: "docs: Add foo").
  1298→	//
  1299→	// Scan from the bottom: find the last paragraph of non-comment content,
  1300→	// then check if it looks like trailers AND has a blank line above it.
  1301→	hasTrailers := false
  1302→	i := len(lines) - 1
  1303→
  1304→	// Skip trailing comment lines
  1305→	for i >= 0 && strings.HasPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(lines[i]), "#") {
  1306→		i--
  1307→	}
  1308→
  1309→	// Check if the last non-comment line looks like a trailer
  1310→	if i >= 0 {
  1311→		line := strings.TrimSpace(lines[i])
  1312→		if line != "" && strings.Contains(line, ": ") {
  1313→			// Found a trailer-like line. Now scan upward past the trailer block
  1314→			// to verify there's a blank line (paragraph separator) above it.
  1315→			for i > 0 {
  1316→				i--
  1317→				above := strings.TrimSpace(lines[i])
  1318→				if strings.HasPrefix(above, "#") {
  1319→					continue
  1320→				}
  1321→				if above == "" {
  1322→					// Blank line found above trailer block — real trailers
  1323→					hasTrailers = true
  1324→					break
  1325→				}
  1326→				if !strings.Contains(above, ": ") {
  1327→					// Non-trailer, non-blank line — this is message body, not trailers
  1328→					break
  1329→				}
  1330→				// Another trailer-like line, keep scanning upward
  1331→			}
  1332→		}
  1333→	}
  1334→
  1335→	if hasTrailers {
  1336→		// Append trailer directly
  1337→		return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n" + trailer + "\n"
  1338→	}
  1339→
  1340→	// Add blank line before trailer
  1341→	return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n"
  1342→}
  1343→
  1344→// addCheckpointTrailerWithComment adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer with an explanatory comment.
  1345→// The trailer is placed above the git comment block but below the user's message area,
  1346→// with a comment explaining that the user can remove it if they don't want to link the commit
  1347→// to the agent session. If prompt is non-empty, it's shown as context.
  1348→func addCheckpointTrailerWithComment(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, agentName, prompt string) string {
  1349→	trailer := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
  1350→	commentLines := []string{
  1351→		"# Remove the Entire-Checkpoint trailer above if you don't want to link this commit to " + agentName + " session context.",
  1352→	}
  1353→	if prompt != "" {
  1354→		commentLines = append(commentLines, "# Last Prompt: "+prompt)
  1355→	}
  1356→	commentLines = append(commentLines, "# The trailer will be added to your next commit based on this branch.")
  1357→	comment := strings.Join(commentLines, "\n")
  1358→
  1359→	lines := strings.Split(message, "\n")
  1360→
  1361→	// Find where the git comment block starts (first # line)
  1362→	commentStart := -1
  1363→	for i, line := range lines {
  1364→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") {
  1365→			commentStart = i
  1366→			break
  1367→		}
  1368→	}
  1369→
  1370→	if commentStart == -1 {
  1371→		// No git comments, append trailer at the end
  1372→		return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n"
  1373→	}
  1374→
  1375→	// Split into user content and git comments
  1376→	userContent := strings.Join(lines[:commentStart], "\n")
  1377→	gitComments := strings.Join(lines[commentStart:], "\n")
  1378→
  1379→	// Build result: user content, blank line, trailer, comment, blank line, git comments
  1380→	userContent = strings.TrimRight(userContent, "\n")
  1381→	if userContent == "" {
  1382→		// No user content yet - leave space for them to type, then trailer
  1383→		// Two newlines: first for user's message line, second for blank separator
  1384→		return "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n\n" + gitComments
  1385→	}
  1386→	return userContent + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n\n" + gitComments
  1387→}
  1388→
  1389→// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session or updates an existing one.
  1390→// This implements the optional SessionInitializer interface.
  1391→// Called during UserPromptSubmit to allow git hooks to detect active sessions.
  1392→//
  1393→// If the session already exists and HEAD has moved (e.g., user committed), updates
  1394→// BaseCommit to the new HEAD so future checkpoints go to the correct shadow branch.
  1395→//
  1396→// If there's an existing shadow branch with commits from a different session ID,
  1397→// returns a SessionIDConflictError to prevent orphaning existing session work.
  1398→//
  1399→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
  1400→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
  1401→// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as FirstPrompt for display).
  1402→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) InitializeSession(sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) error {
  1403→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1404→	if err != nil {
  1405→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
  1406→	}
  1407→
  1408→	// Check if session already exists
  1409→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1410→	if err != nil {
  1411→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check session state: %w", err)
  1412→	}
  1413→
  1414→	if state != nil && state.BaseCommit != "" {
  1415→		// Session is fully initialized — apply phase transition for TurnStart.
  1416→		if transErr := TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventTurnStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
  1417→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: turn start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
  1418→		}
  1419→
  1420→		// Generate a new TurnID for each turn (correlates carry-forward checkpoints)
  1421→		turnID, err := id.Generate()
  1422→		if err != nil {
  1423→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate turn ID: %w", err)
  1424→		}
  1425→		state.TurnID = turnID.String()
  1426→
  1427→		// Backfill AgentType if empty or set to the generic default "Agent"
  1428→		if !isSpecificAgentType(state.AgentType) && agentType != "" {
  1429→			state.AgentType = agentType
  1430→		}
  1431→
  1432→		// Backfill FirstPrompt if empty (for sessions created before the first_prompt field was added)
  1433→		if state.FirstPrompt == "" && userPrompt != "" {
  1434→			state.FirstPrompt = truncatePromptForStorage(userPrompt)
  1435→		}
  1436→
  1437→		// Update transcript path if provided (may change on session resume)
  1438→		if transcriptPath != "" && state.TranscriptPath != transcriptPath {
  1439→			state.TranscriptPath = transcriptPath
  1440→		}
  1441→
  1442→		// Clear checkpoint IDs on every new prompt.
  1443→		// LastCheckpointID is set during PostCommit, cleared at new prompt.
  1444→		// TurnCheckpointIDs tracks mid-turn checkpoints for stop-time finalization.
  1445→		state.LastCheckpointID = ""
  1446→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1447→
  1448→		// Calculate attribution at prompt start (BEFORE agent makes any changes)
  1449→		// This captures user edits since the last checkpoint (or base commit for first prompt).
  1450→		// IMPORTANT: Always calculate attribution, even for the first checkpoint, to capture
  1451→		// user edits made before the first prompt. The inner CalculatePromptAttribution handles
  1452→		// nil lastCheckpointTree by falling back to baseTree.
  1453→		promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(repo, state)
  1454→		state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
  1455→
  1456→		// Check if HEAD has moved (user pulled/rebased or committed)
  1457→		// migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded handles renaming the shadow branch and updating state.BaseCommit
  1458→		if _, err := s.migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded(repo, state); err != nil {
  1459→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to check/migrate shadow branch: %w", err)
  1460→		}
  1461→
  1462→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
  1463→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to update session state: %w", err)
  1464→		}
  1465→		return nil
  1466→	}
  1467→	// If state exists but BaseCommit is empty, it's a partial state from concurrent warning
  1468→	// Continue below to properly initialize it
  1469→
  1470→	// Initialize new session
  1471→	state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, sessionID, agentType, transcriptPath, userPrompt)
  1472→	if err != nil {
  1473→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize session: %w", err)
  1474→	}
  1475→
  1476→	// Apply phase transition: new session starts as ACTIVE.
  1477→	if transErr := TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventTurnStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
  1478→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: turn start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
  1479→	}
  1480→
  1481→	// Calculate attribution for pre-prompt edits
  1482→	// This captures any user edits made before the first prompt
  1483→	promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(repo, state)
  1484→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
  1485→	if err = s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
  1486→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save attribution: %w", err)
  1487→	}
  1488→
  1489→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Initialized shadow session: %s\n", sessionID)
  1490→	return nil
  1491→}
  1492→
  1493→// calculatePromptAttributionAtStart calculates attribution at prompt start (before agent runs).
  1494→// This captures user changes since the last checkpoint - no filtering needed since
  1495→// the agent hasn't made any changes yet.
  1496→//
  1497→// IMPORTANT: This reads from the worktree (not staging area) to match what WriteTemporary
  1498→// captures in checkpoints. If we read staged content but checkpoints capture worktree content,
  1499→// unstaged changes would be in the checkpoint but not counted in PromptAttribution, causing
  1500→// them to be incorrectly attributed to the agent later.
  1501→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(
  1502→	repo *git.Repository,
  1503→	state *SessionState,
  1504→) PromptAttribution {
  1505→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
  1506→	nextCheckpointNum := state.StepCount + 1
  1507→	result := PromptAttribution{CheckpointNumber: nextCheckpointNum}
  1508→
  1509→	// Get last checkpoint tree from shadow branch (if it exists)
  1510→	// For the first checkpoint, no shadow branch exists yet - this is fine,
  1511→	// CalculatePromptAttribution will use baseTree as the reference instead.
  1512→	var lastCheckpointTree *object.Tree
  1513→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1514→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
  1515→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1516→	if err != nil {
  1517→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: no shadow branch yet (first checkpoint)",
  1518→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName))
  1519→		// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1520→	} else {
  1521→		shadowCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
  1522→		if err != nil {
  1523→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: failed to get shadow commit",
  1524→				slog.String("shadow_ref", ref.Hash().String()),
  1525→				slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1526→			// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1527→		} else {
  1528→			lastCheckpointTree, err = shadowCommit.Tree()
  1529→			if err != nil {
  1530→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: failed to get shadow tree",
  1531→					slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1532→				// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1533→			}
  1534→		}
  1535→	}
  1536→
  1537→	// Get base tree for agent lines calculation
  1538→	var baseTree *object.Tree
  1539→	if baseCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(state.BaseCommit)); err == nil {
  1540→		if tree, treeErr := baseCommit.Tree(); treeErr == nil {
  1541→			baseTree = tree
  1542→		} else {
  1543→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: base tree unavailable",
  1544→				slog.String("error", treeErr.Error()))
  1545→		}
  1546→	} else {
  1547→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: base commit unavailable",
  1548→			slog.String("base_commit", state.BaseCommit),
  1549→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1550→	}
  1551→
  1552→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1553→	if err != nil {
  1554→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution skipped: failed to get worktree",
  1555→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1556→		return result
  1557→	}
  1558→
  1559→	// Get worktree status to find ALL changed files
  1560→	status, err := worktree.Status()
  1561→	if err != nil {
  1562→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution skipped: failed to get worktree status",
  1563→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1564→		return result
  1565→	}
  1566→
  1567→	worktreeRoot := worktree.Filesystem.Root()
  1568→
  1569→	// Build map of changed files with their worktree content
  1570→	// IMPORTANT: We read from worktree (not staging area) to match what WriteTemporary
  1571→	// captures in checkpoints. This ensures attribution is consistent.
  1572→	changedFiles := make(map[string]string)
  1573→	for filePath, fileStatus := range status {
  1574→		// Skip unmodified files
  1575→		if fileStatus.Worktree == git.Unmodified && fileStatus.Staging == git.Unmodified {
  1576→			continue
  1577→		}
  1578→		// Skip .entire metadata directory (session data, not user code)
  1579→		if strings.HasPrefix(filePath, paths.EntireMetadataDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(filePath, ".entire/") {
  1580→			continue
  1581→		}
  1582→
  1583→		// Always read from worktree to match checkpoint behavior
  1584→		fullPath := filepath.Join(worktreeRoot, filePath)
  1585→		var content string
  1586→		if data, err := os.ReadFile(fullPath); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // filePath is from git worktree status
  1587→			// Use git's binary detection algorithm (matches getFileContent behavior).
  1588→			// Binary files are excluded from line-based attribution calculations.
  1589→			isBinary, binErr := binary.IsBinary(bytes.NewReader(data))
  1590→			if binErr == nil && !isBinary {
  1591→				content = string(data)
  1592→			}
  1593→		}
  1594→		// else: file deleted, unreadable, or binary - content remains empty string
  1595→
  1596→		changedFiles[filePath] = content
  1597→	}
  1598→
  1599→	// Use CalculatePromptAttribution from manual_commit_attribution.go
  1600→	result = CalculatePromptAttribution(baseTree, lastCheckpointTree, changedFiles, nextCheckpointNum)
  1601→
  1602→	return result
  1603→}
  1604→
  1605→// getStagedFiles returns a list of files staged for commit.
  1606→func getStagedFiles(repo *git.Repository) []string {
  1607→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1608→	if err != nil {
  1609→		return nil
  1610→	}
  1611→
  1612→	status, err := worktree.Status()
  1613→	if err != nil {
  1614→		return nil
  1615→	}
  1616→
  1617→	var staged []string
  1618→	for path, fileStatus := range status {
  1619→		// Check if file is staged (in index)
  1620→		if fileStatus.Staging != git.Unmodified && fileStatus.Staging != git.Untracked {
  1621→			staged = append(staged, path)
  1622→		}
  1623→	}
  1624→	return staged
  1625→}
  1626→
  1627→// getLastPrompt retrieves the most recent user prompt from a session's shadow branch.
  1628→// Returns empty string if no prompt can be retrieved.
  1629→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getLastPrompt(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) string {
  1630→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1631→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
  1632→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1633→	if err != nil {
  1634→		return ""
  1635→	}
  1636→
  1637→	// Extract session data to get prompts for commit message generation
  1638→	// Pass agent type to handle different transcript formats (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini)
  1639→	// Pass 0 for checkpointTranscriptStart since we're extracting all prompts, not calculating token usage
  1640→	sessionData, err := s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, nil, state.AgentType, "", 0)
  1641→	if err != nil || len(sessionData.Prompts) == 0 {
  1642→		return ""
  1643→	}
  1644→
  1645→	// Return the last prompt (most recent work before commit)
  1646→	return sessionData.Prompts[len(sessionData.Prompts)-1]
  1647→}
  1648→
  1649→// HandleTurnEnd dispatches strategy-specific actions emitted when an agent turn ends.
  1650→// The primary job is to finalize all checkpoints from this turn with the full transcript.
  1651→//
  1652→// During a turn, PostCommit writes provisional transcript data (whatever was available
  1653→// at commit time). HandleTurnEnd replaces that with the complete session transcript
  1654→// (from prompt to stop event), ensuring every checkpoint has the full context.
  1655→//
  1656→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
  1657→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) HandleTurnEnd(state *SessionState) error {
  1658→	// Finalize all checkpoints from this turn with the full transcript.
  1659→	//
  1660→	// IMPORTANT: This is best-effort - errors are logged but don't fail the hook.
  1661→	// Failing here would prevent session cleanup and could leave state inconsistent.
  1662→	// The provisional transcript from PostCommit is already persisted, so the
  1663→	// checkpoint isn't lost - it just won't have the complete transcript.
  1664→	errCount := s.finalizeAllTurnCheckpoints(state)
  1665→	if errCount > 0 {
  1666→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1667→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "HandleTurnEnd completed with errors (best-effort)",
  1668→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1669→			slog.Int("error_count", errCount),
  1670→		)
  1671→	}
  1672→	return nil
  1673→}
  1674→
  1675→// finalizeAllTurnCheckpoints replaces the provisional transcript in each checkpoint
  1676→// created during this turn with the full session transcript.
  1677→//
  1678→// This is called at turn end (stop hook). During the turn, PostCommit wrote whatever
  1679→// transcript was available at commit time. Now we have the complete transcript and
  1680→// replace it so every checkpoint has the full prompt-to-stop context.
  1681→//
  1682→// Returns the number of errors encountered (best-effort: continues processing on error).
  1683→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) finalizeAllTurnCheckpoints(state *SessionState) int {
  1684→	if len(state.TurnCheckpointIDs) == 0 {
  1685→		return 0 // No mid-turn commits to finalize
  1686→	}
  1687→
  1688→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1689→
  1690→	logging.Info(logCtx, "finalizing turn checkpoints with full transcript",
  1691→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1692→		slog.Int("checkpoint_count", len(state.TurnCheckpointIDs)),
  1693→	)
  1694→
  1695→	errCount := 0
  1696→
  1697→	// Read full transcript from live transcript file
  1698→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
  1699→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: no transcript path, skipping",
  1700→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1701→		)
  1702→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1703→		return 1 // Count as error - all checkpoints will be skipped
  1704→	}
  1705→
  1706→	fullTranscript, err := os.ReadFile(state.TranscriptPath)
  1707→	if err != nil || len(fullTranscript) == 0 {
  1708→		msg := "finalize: empty transcript, skipping"
  1709→		if err != nil {
  1710→			msg = "finalize: failed to read transcript, skipping"
  1711→		}
  1712→		logging.Warn(logCtx, msg,
  1713→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1714→			slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
  1715→			slog.Any("error", err),
  1716→		)
  1717→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1718→		return 1 // Count as error - all checkpoints will be skipped
  1719→	}
  1720→
  1721→	// Extract prompts and context from the full transcript
  1722→	prompts := extractUserPrompts(state.AgentType, string(fullTranscript))
  1723→	contextBytes := generateContextFromPrompts(prompts)
  1724→
  1725→	// Redact secrets before writing — matches WriteCommitted behavior.
  1726→	// The live transcript on disk contains raw content; redaction must happen
  1727→	// before anything is persisted to the metadata branch.
  1728→	fullTranscript, err = redact.JSONLBytes(fullTranscript)
  1729→	if err != nil {
  1730→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: transcript redaction failed, skipping",
  1731→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1732→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1733→		)
  1734→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1735→		return 1 // Count as error - all checkpoints will be skipped
  1736→	}
  1737→	for i, p := range prompts {
  1738→		prompts[i] = redact.String(p)
  1739→	}
  1740→	contextBytes = redact.Bytes(contextBytes)
  1741→
  1742→	// Open repository and create checkpoint store
  1743→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1744→	if err != nil {
  1745→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: failed to open repository",
  1746→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1747→		)
  1748→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1749→		return 1 // Count as error - all checkpoints will be skipped
  1750→	}
  1751→	store := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
  1752→
  1753→	// Update each checkpoint with the full transcript
  1754→	for _, cpIDStr := range state.TurnCheckpointIDs {
  1755→		cpID, parseErr := id.NewCheckpointID(cpIDStr)
  1756→		if parseErr != nil {
  1757→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: invalid checkpoint ID, skipping",
  1758→				slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpIDStr),
  1759→				slog.String("error", parseErr.Error()),
  1760→			)
  1761→			errCount++
  1762→			continue
  1763→		}
  1764→
  1765→		updateErr := store.UpdateCommitted(context.Background(), checkpoint.UpdateCommittedOptions{
  1766→			CheckpointID: cpID,
  1767→			SessionID:    state.SessionID,
  1768→			Transcript:   fullTranscript,
  1769→			Prompts:      prompts,
  1770→			Context:      contextBytes,
  1771→			Agent:        state.AgentType,
  1772→		})
  1773→		if updateErr != nil {
  1774→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: failed to update checkpoint",
  1775→				slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpIDStr),
  1776→				slog.String("error", updateErr.Error()),
  1777→			)
  1778→			errCount++
  1779→			continue
  1780→		}
  1781→
  1782→		logging.Info(logCtx, "finalize: checkpoint updated with full transcript",
  1783→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpIDStr),
  1784→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1785→		)
  1786→	}
  1787→
  1788→	// Clear turn checkpoint IDs. Do NOT update CheckpointTranscriptStart here — it was
  1789→	// already set correctly by PostCommit: condenseAndUpdateState sets it to the total
  1790→	// transcript lines when condensing, and carryForwardToNewShadowBranch resets it to 0
  1791→	// when carry-forward is active. Overwriting here would break carry-forward by making
  1792→	// sessionHasNewContent think the transcript is fully consumed (no growth).
  1793→	state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1794→
  1795→	return errCount
  1796→}
  1797→
  1798→// filesChangedInCommit returns the set of files changed in a commit by diffing against its parent.
  1799→func filesChangedInCommit(commit *object.Commit) map[string]struct{} {
  1800→	result := make(map[string]struct{})
  1801→
  1802→	commitTree, err := commit.Tree()
  1803→	if err != nil {
  1804→		return result
  1805→	}
  1806→
  1807→	var parentTree *object.Tree
  1808→	if commit.NumParents() > 0 {
  1809→		parent, err := commit.Parent(0)
  1810→		if err != nil {
  1811→			return result
  1812→		}
  1813→		parentTree, err = parent.Tree()
  1814→		if err != nil {
  1815→			return result
  1816→		}
  1817→	}
  1818→
  1819→	if parentTree == nil {
  1820→		// Initial commit — all files are new
  1821→		if iterErr := commitTree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
  1822→			result[f.Name] = struct{}{}
  1823→			return nil
  1824→		}); iterErr != nil {
  1825→			return result
  1826→		}
  1827→		return result
  1828→	}
  1829→
  1830→	changes, err := parentTree.Diff(commitTree)
  1831→	if err != nil {
  1832→		return result
  1833→	}
  1834→	for _, change := range changes {
  1835→		name := change.To.Name
  1836→		if name == "" {
  1837→			name = change.From.Name
  1838→		}
  1839→		result[name] = struct{}{}
  1840→	}
  1841→	return result
  1842→}
  1843→
  1844→// subtractFiles returns files that are NOT in the exclude set.
  1845→func subtractFiles(files []string, exclude map[string]struct{}) []string {
  1846→	var remaining []string
  1847→	for _, f := range files {
  1848→		if _, excluded := exclude[f]; !excluded {
  1849→			remaining = append(remaining, f)
  1850→		}
  1851→	}
  1852→	return remaining
  1853→}
  1854→
  1855→// carryForwardToNewShadowBranch creates a new shadow branch at the current HEAD
  1856→// containing the remaining uncommitted files and all session metadata.
  1857→// This enables the next commit to get its own unique checkpoint.
  1858→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) carryForwardToNewShadowBranch(
  1859→	logCtx context.Context,
  1860→	repo *git.Repository,
  1861→	state *SessionState,
  1862→	remainingFiles []string,
  1863→) {
  1864→	store := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
  1865→
  1866→	// Don't include metadata directory in carry-forward. The carry-forward branch
  1867→	// only needs to preserve file content for comparison - not the transcript.
  1868→	// Including the transcript would cause sessionHasNewContent to always return true
  1869→	// because CheckpointTranscriptStart is reset to 0 for carry-forward.
  1870→	result, err := store.WriteTemporary(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteTemporaryOptions{
  1871→		SessionID:         state.SessionID,
  1872→		BaseCommit:        state.BaseCommit,
  1873→		WorktreeID:        state.WorktreeID,
  1874→		ModifiedFiles:     remainingFiles,
  1875→		MetadataDir:       "",
  1876→		MetadataDirAbs:    "",
  1877→		CommitMessage:     "carry forward: uncommitted session files",
  1878→		IsFirstCheckpoint: false,
  1879→	})
  1880→	if err != nil {
  1881→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward failed",
  1882→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1883→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1884→		)
  1885→		return
  1886→	}
  1887→	if result.Skipped {
  1888→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward skipped (no changes)",
  1889→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1890→		)
  1891→		return
  1892→	}
  1893→
  1894→	// Update state for the carry-forward checkpoint.
  1895→	// CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0 is intentional: each checkpoint is self-contained with
  1896→	// the full transcript. This trades storage efficiency for simplicity:
  1897→	// - Pro: Each checkpoint is independently readable without needing to stitch together
  1898→	//   multiple checkpoints to understand the session history
  1899→	// - Con: For long sessions with multiple partial commits, each checkpoint includes
  1900→	//   the full transcript, which could be large
  1901→	// An alternative would be incremental checkpoints (only new content since last condensation),
  1902→	// but this would complicate checkpoint retrieval and require careful tracking of dependencies.
  1903→	state.StepCount = 1
  1904→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0
  1905→	state.LastCheckpointID = ""
  1906→	// NOTE: TurnCheckpointIDs is intentionally NOT cleared here. Those checkpoint
  1907→	// IDs from earlier in the turn still need finalization with the full transcript
  1908→	// when HandleTurnEnd runs at stop time.
  1909→
  1910→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-commit: carried forward remaining files",
  1911→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1912→		slog.Int("remaining_files", len(remainingFiles)),
  1913→	)
  1914→}
  1915→
  1916→// appendCheckpointToTrail links a checkpoint to the trail for the current branch.
  1917→// Best-effort: silently returns on any error (trails are non-critical metadata).
  1918→func appendCheckpointToTrail(repo *git.Repository, cpID id.CheckpointID, commitSHA plumbing.Hash, prompts []string) {
  1919→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
  1920→	if branchName == "" {
  1921→		return
  1922→	}
  1923→	defaultBranch := GetDefaultBranchName(repo)
  1924→	if branchName == defaultBranch {
  1925→		return
  1926→	}
  1927→
  1928→	store := trail.NewStore(repo)
  1929→	existing, err := store.FindByBranch(branchName)
  1930→	if err != nil || existing == nil {
  1931→		return
  1932→	}
  1933→
  1934→	var summary string
  1935→	if len(prompts) > 0 {
  1936→		summary = truncateForSummary(prompts[len(prompts)-1], 200)
  1937→	}
  1938→
  1939→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // best-effort: trail checkpoint linking is non-critical
  1940→	store.AddCheckpoint(existing.TrailID, trail.CheckpointRef{
  1941→		CheckpointID: cpID.String(),
  1942→		CommitSHA:    commitSHA.String(),
  1943→		CreatedAt:    time.Now().UTC(),
  1944→		Summary:      summary,
  1945→	})
  1946→}
  1947→
  1948→// truncateForSummary truncates a string to maxLen, adding "..." if truncated.
  1949→// Takes the first line only to keep summaries concise.
  1950→func truncateForSummary(s string, maxLen int) string {
  1951→	line, _, _ := strings.Cut(s, "\n")
  1952→	line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
  1953→	if len(line) <= maxLen {
  1954→		return line
  1955→	}
  1956→	if maxLen <= 3 {
  1957→		return line[:maxLen]
  1958→	}
  1959→	return line[:maxLen-3] + "..."
  1960→}
  1961→
```

```
     1→// Package trail provides types and helpers for managing trail metadata.
     2→// Trails are branch-centric work tracking abstractions stored on the
     3→// entire/trails orphan branch. They answer "why/what" (human intent)
     4→// while checkpoints answer "how/when" (machine snapshots).
     5→package trail
     6→
     7→import (
     8→	"crypto/rand"
     9→	"encoding/hex"
    10→	"fmt"
    11→	"regexp"
    12→	"strings"
    13→	"time"
    14→)
    15→
    16→const idLength = 6 // 6 bytes = 12 hex chars
    17→
    18→// ID is a 12-character hex identifier for trails.
    19→type ID string
    20→
    21→// EmptyID represents an unset or invalid trail ID.
    22→const EmptyID ID = ""
    23→
    24→// idRegex validates the format: exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.
    25→var idRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{12}$`)
    26→
    27→// GenerateID creates a new random 12-character hex trail ID.
    28→func GenerateID() (ID, error) {
    29→	bytes := make([]byte, idLength)
    30→	if _, err := rand.Read(bytes); err != nil {
    31→		return EmptyID, fmt.Errorf("failed to generate random trail ID: %w", err)
    32→	}
    33→	return ID(hex.EncodeToString(bytes)), nil
    34→}
    35→
    36→// ValidateID checks if a string is a valid trail ID format.
    37→func ValidateID(s string) error {
    38→	if !idRegex.MatchString(s) {
    39→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid trail ID %q: must be 12 lowercase hex characters", s)
    40→	}
    41→	return nil
    42→}
    43→
    44→// String returns the trail ID as a string.
    45→func (id ID) String() string {
    46→	return string(id)
    47→}
    48→
    49→// IsEmpty returns true if the trail ID is empty or unset.
    50→func (id ID) IsEmpty() bool {
    51→	return id == EmptyID
    52→}
    53→
    54→// Path returns the sharded storage path for this trail ID.
    55→// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.
    56→// Example: "a3b2c4d5e6f7" -> "a3/b2c4d5e6f7"
    57→func (id ID) Path() string {
    58→	if len(id) < 3 {
    59→		return string(id)
    60→	}
    61→	return string(id[:2]) + "/" + string(id[2:])
    62→}
    63→
    64→// Status represents the lifecycle status of a trail.
    65→type Status string
    66→
    67→const (
    68→	StatusDraft      Status = "draft"
    69→	StatusOpen       Status = "open"
    70→	StatusInProgress Status = "in_progress"
    71→	StatusInReview   Status = "in_review"
    72→	StatusDone       Status = "done"
    73→	StatusClosed     Status = "closed"
    74→)
    75→
    76→// ValidStatuses returns all valid trail statuses in lifecycle order.
    77→func ValidStatuses() []Status {
    78→	return []Status{
    79→		StatusDraft,
    80→		StatusOpen,
    81→		StatusInProgress,
    82→		StatusInReview,
    83→		StatusDone,
    84→		StatusClosed,
    85→	}
    86→}
    87→
    88→// IsValid returns true if the status is a recognized trail status.
    89→func (s Status) IsValid() bool {
    90→	for _, vs := range ValidStatuses() {
    91→		if s == vs {
    92→			return true
    93→		}
    94→	}
    95→	return false
    96→}
    97→
    98→// Metadata represents the metadata for a trail, matching the web PR format.
    99→type Metadata struct {
   100→	TrailID     ID         `json:"trail_id"`
   101→	Branch      string     `json:"branch"`
   102→	Base        string     `json:"base"`
   103→	Title       string     `json:"title"`
   104→	Description string     `json:"description"`
   105→	Status      Status     `json:"status"`
   106→	Author      string     `json:"author"`
   107→	Assignees   []string   `json:"assignees"`
   108→	Labels      []string   `json:"labels"`
   109→	CreatedAt   time.Time  `json:"created_at"`
   110→	UpdatedAt   time.Time  `json:"updated_at"`
   111→	MergedAt    *time.Time `json:"merged_at,omitempty"`
   112→}
   113→
   114→// Discussion holds the discussion/comments for a trail.
   115→type Discussion struct {
   116→	Comments []Comment `json:"comments"`
   117→}
   118→
   119→// Comment represents a single comment on a trail.
   120→type Comment struct {
   121→	ID        string         `json:"id"`
   122→	Author    string         `json:"author"`
   123→	Body      string         `json:"body"`
   124→	CreatedAt time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
   125→	Replies   []CommentReply `json:"replies,omitempty"`
   126→}
   127→
   128→// CommentReply represents a reply to a comment.
   129→type CommentReply struct {
   130→	ID        string    `json:"id"`
   131→	Author    string    `json:"author"`
   132→	Body      string    `json:"body"`
   133→	CreatedAt time.Time `json:"created_at"`
   134→}
   135→
   136→// commonBranchPrefixes are stripped from branch names when humanizing.
   137→var commonBranchPrefixes = []string{
   138→	"feature/",
   139→	"fix/",
   140→	"bugfix/",
   141→	"chore/",
   142→	"hotfix/",
   143→	"release/",
   144→}
   145→
   146→// CheckpointRef links a checkpoint to a trail.
   147→type CheckpointRef struct {
   148→	CheckpointID string    `json:"checkpoint_id"`
   149→	CommitSHA    string    `json:"commit_sha"`
   150→	CreatedAt    time.Time `json:"created_at"`
   151→	Summary      string    `json:"summary,omitempty"`
   152→}
   153→
   154→// Checkpoints holds the list of checkpoint references for a trail.
   155→type Checkpoints struct {
   156→	Checkpoints []CheckpointRef `json:"checkpoints"`
   157→}
   158→
   159→// HumanizeBranchName converts a branch name into a human-readable title.
   160→// It strips common prefixes (feature/, fix/, etc.), replaces dashes/underscores
   161→// with spaces, and capitalizes the first word.
   162→func HumanizeBranchName(branch string) string {
   163→	name := branch
   164→	for _, prefix := range commonBranchPrefixes {
   165→		if strings.HasPrefix(name, prefix) {
   166→			name = strings.TrimPrefix(name, prefix)
   167→			break
   168→		}
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	// Replace - and _ with spaces
   172→	name = strings.NewReplacer("-", " ", "_", " ").Replace(name)
   173→
   174→	// Trim spaces and capitalize first letter
   175→	name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
   176→	if name == "" {
   177→		return branch
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	// Capitalize first character
   181→	return strings.ToUpper(name[:1]) + name[1:]
   182→}
   183→
```
