    30→
    31→// listCheckpoints returns all checkpoints from the metadata branch.
    32→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ListCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    33→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listCheckpoints() ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    34→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    37→	}
    38→
    39→	committed, err := store.ListCommitted(context.Background())
    40→	if err != nil {
    41→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list committed checkpoints: %w", err)
    42→	}
    43→
    44→	// Convert from checkpoint.CommittedInfo to strategy.CheckpointInfo
    45→	result := make([]CheckpointInfo, 0, len(committed))
    46→	for _, c := range committed {
    47→		result = append(result, CheckpointInfo{
    48→			CheckpointID:     c.CheckpointID,
    49→			SessionID:        c.SessionID,
    50→			CreatedAt:        c.CreatedAt,
    51→			CheckpointsCount: c.CheckpointsCount,
    52→			FilesTouched:     c.FilesTouched,
    53→			Agent:            c.Agent,
    54→			IsTask:           c.IsTask,
    55→			ToolUseID:        c.ToolUseID,
    56→			SessionCount:     c.SessionCount,
    57→			SessionIDs:       c.SessionIDs,
    58→		})
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	return result, nil
    62→}
    63→
    64→// getCheckpointsForSession returns all checkpoints for a session ID.
    65→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointsForSession(sessionID string) ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    66→	all, err := s.listCheckpoints()
    67→	if err != nil {
    68→		return nil, err
    69→	}
    70→
    71→	var result []CheckpointInfo
    72→	for _, cp := range all {
    73→		if cp.SessionID == sessionID || strings.HasPrefix(cp.SessionID, sessionID) {
    74→			result = append(result, cp)
    75→		}
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	if len(result) == 0 {
    79→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no checkpoints for session: %s", sessionID)
    80→	}
    81→
    82→	return result, nil
    83→}
    84→
    85→// getCheckpointLog returns the transcript for a specific checkpoint ID.
    86→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ReadCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    87→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointLog(checkpointID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, error) {
    88→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    89→	if err != nil {
    90→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	content, err := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), checkpointID)
    94→	if err != nil {
    95→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
    96→	}
    97→	if content == nil {
    98→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checkpoint not found: %s", checkpointID)
    99→	}
   100→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   101→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no transcript found for checkpoint: %s", checkpointID)
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	return content.Transcript, nil
   105→}
   106→
   107→// CondenseSession condenses a session's shadow branch to permanent storage.
   108→// checkpointID is the 12-hex-char value from the Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   109→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpoint_id[:2]>/<checkpoint_id[2:]>/
   110→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteCommitted for the git operations.
   111→//
   112→// For mid-session commits (no Stop/SaveStep called yet), the shadow branch may not exist.
   113→// In this case, data is extracted from the live transcript instead.
   114→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSession(repo *git.Repository, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, state *SessionState, committedFiles map[string]struct{}) (*CondenseResult, error) {
   115→	// Get shadow branch (may not exist for mid-session commits)
   116→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   117→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   118→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   119→	hasShadowBranch := err == nil
   120→
   121→	var sessionData *ExtractedSessionData
   122→	if hasShadowBranch {
   123→		// Extract session data from the shadow branch (with live transcript fallback).
   124→		// Use tracked files from session state instead of collecting all files from tree.
   125→		// Pass agent type to handle different transcript formats (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini).
   126→		// Pass live transcript path so condensation reads the current file rather than a
   127→		// potentially stale shadow branch copy (SaveStep may have been skipped if the
   128→		// last turn had no code changes).
   129→		// Pass CheckpointTranscriptStart for accurate token calculation (line offset for Claude, message index for Gemini).
   130→		sessionData, err = s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, state.FilesTouched, state.AgentType, state.TranscriptPath, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   131→		if err != nil {
   132→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to extract session data: %w", err)
   133→		}
   134→	} else {
   135→		// No shadow branch: mid-session commit before Stop/SaveStep.
   136→		// Extract data directly from live transcript.
   137→		if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
   138→			return nil, errors.New("shadow branch not found and no live transcript available")
   139→		}
   140→		sessionData, err = s.extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript(state)
   141→		if err != nil {
   142→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to extract session data from live transcript: %w", err)
   143→		}
   144→	}
   145→
   146→	// For 1:1 checkpoint model: filter files_touched to only include files actually
   147→	// committed in this specific commit. This ensures each checkpoint represents
   148→	// exactly the files in that commit, not all files mentioned in the transcript.
   149→	if len(committedFiles) > 0 {
   150→		// Track if we had files before filtering to distinguish between:
   151→		// - Session had files but none were committed (don't fallback)
   152→		// - Session had no files to begin with (mid-session commit, fallback OK)
   153→		hadFilesBeforeFiltering := len(sessionData.FilesTouched) > 0
   154→
   155→		if hadFilesBeforeFiltering {
   156→			// Filter to intersection of transcript-extracted files and committed files
   157→			filtered := make([]string, 0, len(sessionData.FilesTouched))
   158→			for _, f := range sessionData.FilesTouched {
   159→				if _, ok := committedFiles[f]; ok {
   160→					filtered = append(filtered, f)
   161→				}
   162→			}
   163→			sessionData.FilesTouched = filtered
   164→		}
   165→
   166→		// Only use committedFiles as fallback for genuine mid-session commits where
   167→		// no files were tracked yet (extraction returned empty). Do NOT fallback when
   168→		// the session had files that simply didn't overlap with the commit - that
   169→		// indicates an unrelated session that shouldn't have its files_touched
   170→		// overwritten with unrelated committed files.
   171→		if len(sessionData.FilesTouched) == 0 && !hadFilesBeforeFiltering {
   172→			sessionData.FilesTouched = make([]string, 0, len(committedFiles))
   173→			for f := range committedFiles {
   174→				sessionData.FilesTouched = append(sessionData.FilesTouched, f)
   175→			}
   176→		}
   177→	}
   178→
   179→	// Get checkpoint store
   180→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   181→	if err != nil {
   182→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	// Get author info
   186→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
   187→	// Calculate attribution. When no shadow branch exists (agent committed mid-turn
   188→	// before SaveStep), pass nil ref — the function uses HEAD as the shadow tree
   189→	// since the agent's commit IS HEAD (no user edits between agent work and commit).
   190→	attribution := calculateSessionAttributions(repo, ref, sessionData, state)
   191→	// Get current branch name
   192→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   193→
   194→	// Generate summary if enabled
   195→	var summary *cpkg.Summary
   196→	if settings.IsSummarizeEnabled() && len(sessionData.Transcript) > 0 {
   197→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   198→		summarizeCtx := logging.WithComponent(logCtx, "summarize")
   199→
   200→		// Scope transcript to this checkpoint's portion.
   201→		// For Claude Code (JSONL), CheckpointTranscriptStart is a line offset.
   202→		// For Gemini (JSON), CheckpointTranscriptStart is a message index.
   203→		var scopedTranscript []byte
   204→		if state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   205→			scopedTranscript = geminicli.SliceFromMessage(sessionData.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   206→		} else {
   207→			scopedTranscript = transcript.SliceFromLine(sessionData.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   208→		}
   209→		if len(scopedTranscript) > 0 {
   210→			var err error
   211→			summary, err = summarize.GenerateFromTranscript(summarizeCtx, scopedTranscript, sessionData.FilesTouched, state.AgentType, nil)
   212→			if err != nil {
   213→				logging.Warn(summarizeCtx, "summary generation failed",
   214→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   215→					slog.String("error", err.Error()))
   216→				// Continue without summary - non-blocking
   217→			} else {
   218→				logging.Info(summarizeCtx, "summary generated",
   219→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID))
   220→			}
   221→		}
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	// Write checkpoint metadata using the checkpoint store
   225→	if err := store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), cpkg.WriteCommittedOptions{
   226→		CheckpointID:                checkpointID,
   227→		SessionID:                   state.SessionID,
   228→		Strategy:                    StrategyNameManualCommit,
   229→		Branch:                      branchName,
   230→		Transcript:                  sessionData.Transcript,
   231→		Prompts:                     sessionData.Prompts,
   232→		Context:                     sessionData.Context,
   233→		FilesTouched:                sessionData.FilesTouched,
   234→		CheckpointsCount:            state.StepCount,
   235→		EphemeralBranch:             shadowBranchName,
   236→		AuthorName:                  authorName,
   237→		AuthorEmail:                 authorEmail,
   238→		Agent:                       state.AgentType,
   239→		TurnID:                      state.TurnID,
   240→		TranscriptIdentifierAtStart: state.TranscriptIdentifierAtStart,
   241→		CheckpointTranscriptStart:   state.CheckpointTranscriptStart,
   242→		TokenUsage:                  sessionData.TokenUsage,
   243→		ExportData:                  sessionData.ExportData,
   244→		InitialAttribution:          attribution,
   245→		Summary:                     summary,
   246→	}); err != nil {
   247→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to write checkpoint metadata: %w", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	return &CondenseResult{
   251→		CheckpointID:         checkpointID,
   252→		SessionID:            state.SessionID,
   253→		CheckpointsCount:     state.StepCount,
   254→		FilesTouched:         sessionData.FilesTouched,
   255→		Prompts:              sessionData.Prompts,
   256→		TotalTranscriptLines: sessionData.FullTranscriptLines,
   257→	}, nil
   258→}
   259→
   260→func calculateSessionAttributions(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef *plumbing.Reference, sessionData *ExtractedSessionData, state *SessionState) *cpkg.InitialAttribution {
   261→	// Calculate initial attribution using accumulated prompt attribution data.
   262→	// This uses user edits captured at each prompt start (before agent works),
   263→	// plus any user edits after the final checkpoint (shadow → head).
   264→	//
   265→	// When shadowRef is nil (agent committed mid-turn before SaveStep),
   266→	// HEAD is used as the shadow tree. This is correct because the agent's
   267→	// commit IS HEAD — there are no user edits between agent work and commit.
   268→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   269→
   270→	headRef, headErr := repo.Head()
   271→	if headErr != nil {
   272→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD",
   273→			slog.String("error", headErr.Error()))
   274→		return nil
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	headCommit, commitErr := repo.CommitObject(headRef.Hash())
   278→	if commitErr != nil {
   279→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD commit",
   280→			slog.String("error", commitErr.Error()))
   281→		return nil
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	headTree, treeErr := headCommit.Tree()
   285→	if treeErr != nil {
   286→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD tree",
   287→			slog.String("error", treeErr.Error()))
   288→		return nil
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	// Get shadow tree: from shadow branch if available, otherwise HEAD (agent committed directly).
   292→	var shadowTree *object.Tree
   293→	if shadowRef != nil {
   294→		shadowCommit, shadowErr := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef.Hash())
   295→		if shadowErr != nil {
   296→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get shadow commit",
   297→				slog.String("error", shadowErr.Error()),
   298→				slog.String("shadow_ref", shadowRef.Hash().String()))
   299→			return nil
   300→		}
   301→		var shadowTreeErr error
   302→		shadowTree, shadowTreeErr = shadowCommit.Tree()
   303→		if shadowTreeErr != nil {
   304→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get shadow tree",
   305→				slog.String("error", shadowTreeErr.Error()))
   306→			return nil
   307→		}
   308→	} else {
   309→		// No shadow branch: agent committed mid-turn. Use HEAD as shadow
   310→		// because the agent's work is the commit itself.
   311→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: using HEAD as shadow (no shadow branch)")
   312→		shadowTree = headTree
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	// Get base tree (state before session started)
   316→	var baseTree *object.Tree
   317→	attrBase := state.AttributionBaseCommit
   318→	if attrBase == "" {
   319→		attrBase = state.BaseCommit // backward compat
   320→	}
   321→	if baseCommit, baseErr := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(attrBase)); baseErr == nil {
   322→		if tree, baseTErr := baseCommit.Tree(); baseTErr == nil {
   323→			baseTree = tree
   324→		} else {
   325→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: base tree unavailable",
   326→				slog.String("error", baseTErr.Error()))
   327→		}
   328→	} else {
   329→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: base commit unavailable",
   330→			slog.String("error", baseErr.Error()),
   331→			slog.String("attribution_base", attrBase))
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	// Log accumulated prompt attributions for debugging
   335→	var totalUserAdded, totalUserRemoved int
   336→	for i, pa := range state.PromptAttributions {
   337→		totalUserAdded += pa.UserLinesAdded
   338→		totalUserRemoved += pa.UserLinesRemoved
   339→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution data",
   340→			slog.Int("checkpoint", pa.CheckpointNumber),
   341→			slog.Int("user_added", pa.UserLinesAdded),
   342→			slog.Int("user_removed", pa.UserLinesRemoved),
   343→			slog.Int("agent_added", pa.AgentLinesAdded),
   344→			slog.Int("agent_removed", pa.AgentLinesRemoved),
   345→			slog.Int("index", i))
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	attribution := CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated(
   349→		baseTree,
   350→		shadowTree,
   351→		headTree,
   352→		sessionData.FilesTouched,
   353→		state.PromptAttributions,
   354→	)
   355→
   356→	if attribution != nil {
   357→		logging.Info(logCtx, "attribution calculated",
   358→			slog.Int("agent_lines", attribution.AgentLines),
   359→			slog.Int("human_added", attribution.HumanAdded),
   360→			slog.Int("human_modified", attribution.HumanModified),
   361→			slog.Int("human_removed", attribution.HumanRemoved),
   362→			slog.Int("total_committed", attribution.TotalCommitted),
   363→			slog.Float64("agent_percentage", attribution.AgentPercentage),
   364→			slog.Int("accumulated_user_added", totalUserAdded),
   365→			slog.Int("accumulated_user_removed", totalUserRemoved),
   366→			slog.Int("files_touched", len(sessionData.FilesTouched)))
   367→	}
   368→
   369→	return attribution
   370→}
   371→
   372→// extractSessionData extracts session data from the shadow branch.
   373→// filesTouched is the list of files tracked during the session (from SessionState.FilesTouched).
   374→// agentType identifies the agent (e.g., "Gemini CLI", "Claude Code") to determine transcript format.
   375→// liveTranscriptPath, when non-empty and readable, is preferred over the shadow branch copy.
   376→// This handles the case where SaveStep was skipped (no code changes) but the transcript
   377→// continued growing — the shadow branch copy would be stale.
   378→// checkpointTranscriptStart is the line offset (Claude) or message index (Gemini) where the current checkpoint began.
   379→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionData(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef plumbing.Hash, sessionID string, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType, liveTranscriptPath string, checkpointTranscriptStart int) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   380→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef)
   381→	if err != nil {
   382→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   383→	}
   384→
   385→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   386→	if err != nil {
   387→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	data := &ExtractedSessionData{}
   391→	// sessionID is already an "entire session ID" (with date prefix)
   392→	metadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   393→
   394→	// Extract transcript — prefer the live file when available, fall back to shadow branch.
   395→	// The shadow branch copy may be stale if the last turn ended without code changes
   396→	// (SaveStep is only called when there are file modifications).
   397→	var fullTranscript string
   398→	if liveTranscriptPath != "" {
   399→		if liveData, readErr := os.ReadFile(liveTranscriptPath); readErr == nil && len(liveData) > 0 { //nolint:gosec // path from session state
   400→			fullTranscript = string(liveData)
   401→		}
   402→	}
   403→	if fullTranscript == "" {
   404→		// Fall back to shadow branch copy
   405→		if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   406→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   407→				fullTranscript = content
   408→			}
   409→		} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   410→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   411→				fullTranscript = content
   412→			}
   413→		}
   414→	}
   415→
   416→	// Process transcript based on agent type
   417→	if fullTranscript != "" {
   418→		data.Transcript = []byte(fullTranscript)
   419→		data.FullTranscriptLines = countTranscriptItems(agentType, fullTranscript)
   420→		data.Prompts = extractUserPrompts(agentType, fullTranscript)
   421→		data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   422→	}
   423→
   424→	// Use tracked files from session state (not all files in tree)
   425→	data.FilesTouched = filesTouched
   426→
   427→	// Read export data from local metadata directory (e.g., OpenCode SQLite export).
   428→	// This is written by lifecycle.go during TurnEnd and must be stored in the
   429→	// committed checkpoint so resume/rewind can re-import the session.
   430→	exportRelPath := metadataDir + "/" + paths.ExportDataFileName
   431→	if exportAbsPath, absErr := paths.AbsPath(exportRelPath); absErr == nil {
   432→		if exportBytes, readErr := os.ReadFile(exportAbsPath); readErr == nil && len(exportBytes) > 0 { //nolint:gosec // path from session metadata
   433→			data.ExportData = exportBytes
   434→		}
   435→	}
   436→
   437→	// Calculate token usage from the extracted transcript portion
   438→	if len(data.Transcript) > 0 {
   439→		data.TokenUsage = calculateTokenUsage(agentType, data.Transcript, checkpointTranscriptStart)
   440→	}
   441→
   442→	return data, nil
   443→}
   444→
   445→// extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript extracts session data directly from the live transcript file.
   446→// This is used for mid-session commits where no shadow branch exists yet.
   447→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   448→	data := &ExtractedSessionData{}
   449→
   450→	// Read the live transcript
   451→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
   452→		return nil, errors.New("no transcript path in session state")
   453→	}
   454→
   455→	liveData, err := os.ReadFile(state.TranscriptPath)
   456→	if err != nil {
   457→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read live transcript: %w", err)
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	if len(liveData) == 0 {
   461→		return nil, errors.New("live transcript is empty")
   462→	}
   463→
   464→	fullTranscript := string(liveData)
   465→	data.Transcript = liveData
   466→	data.FullTranscriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, fullTranscript)
   467→	data.Prompts = extractUserPrompts(state.AgentType, fullTranscript)
   468→	data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   469→
   470→	// Extract files from transcript since state.FilesTouched may be empty for mid-session commits
   471→	// (no SaveStep/Stop has been called yet to populate it)
   472→	if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 {
   473→		data.FilesTouched = state.FilesTouched
   474→	} else {
   475→		// Use the shared helper which includes subagent transcripts
   476→		data.FilesTouched = s.extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   477→	}
   478→
   479→	// Read export data from local metadata directory
   480→	metadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(state.SessionID)
   481→	exportRelPath := metadataDir + "/" + paths.ExportDataFileName
   482→	if exportAbsPath, absErr := paths.AbsPath(exportRelPath); absErr == nil {
   483→		if exportBytes, readErr := os.ReadFile(exportAbsPath); readErr == nil && len(exportBytes) > 0 { //nolint:gosec // path from session metadata
   484→			data.ExportData = exportBytes
   485→		}
   486→	}
   487→
   488→	// Calculate token usage from the extracted transcript portion
   489→	if len(data.Transcript) > 0 {
   490→		data.TokenUsage = calculateTokenUsage(state.AgentType, data.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   491→	}
   492→
   493→	return data, nil
   494→}
   495→
   496→// countTranscriptItems counts lines (JSONL) or messages (JSON) in a transcript.
   497→// For Claude Code and JSONL-based agents, this counts lines.
   498→// For Gemini CLI and JSON-based agents, this counts messages.
   499→// Returns 0 if the content is empty or malformed.
   500→func countTranscriptItems(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) int {
   501→	if content == "" {
   502→		return 0
   503→	}
   504→
   505→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   506→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   507→		transcript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript([]byte(content))
   508→		if err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   509→			return len(transcript.Messages)
   510→		}
   511→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return 0
   512→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   513→			return 0
   514→		}
   515→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   516→	}
   517→
   518→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   519→	allLines := strings.Split(content, "\n")
   520→	// Trim trailing empty lines (from final \n in JSONL)
   521→	for len(allLines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(allLines[len(allLines)-1]) == "" {
   522→		allLines = allLines[:len(allLines)-1]
   523→	}
   524→	return len(allLines)
   525→}
   526→
   527→// extractUserPrompts extracts all user prompts from transcript content.
   528→// Returns prompts with IDE context tags stripped (e.g., <ide_opened_file>).
   529→func extractUserPrompts(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) []string {
   530→	if content == "" {
   531→		return nil
   532→	}
   533→
   534→	// OpenCode uses JSONL with a different per-line schema than Claude Code
   535→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeOpenCode {
   536→		prompts, err := opencode.ExtractAllUserPrompts([]byte(content))
   537→		if err == nil && len(prompts) > 0 {
   538→			cleaned := make([]string, 0, len(prompts))
   539→			for _, prompt := range prompts {
   540→				if stripped := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(prompt); stripped != "" {
   541→					cleaned = append(cleaned, stripped)
   542→				}
   543→			}
   544→			return cleaned
   545→		}
   546→		return nil
   547→	}
   548→
   549→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   550→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   551→		prompts, err := geminicli.ExtractAllUserPrompts([]byte(content))
   552→		if err == nil && len(prompts) > 0 {
   553→			// Strip IDE context tags for consistency with Claude Code handling
   554→			cleaned := make([]string, 0, len(prompts))
   555→			for _, prompt := range prompts {
   556→				if stripped := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(prompt); stripped != "" {
   557→					cleaned = append(cleaned, stripped)
   558→				}
   559→			}
   560→			return cleaned
   561→		}
   562→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return nil
   563→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   564→			return nil
   565→		}
   566→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   567→	}
   568→
   569→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   570→	return extractUserPromptsFromLines(strings.Split(content, "\n"))
   571→}
   572→
   573→// calculateTokenUsage calculates token usage from raw transcript data.
   574→// startOffset is the line number (Claude Code) or message index (Gemini CLI)
   575→// where the current checkpoint began, allowing calculation for only the portion
   576→// of the transcript since the last checkpoint.
   577→func calculateTokenUsage(agentType agent.AgentType, data []byte, startOffset int) *agent.TokenUsage {
   578→	if len(data) == 0 {
   579→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   580→	}
   581→
   582→	// OpenCode uses JSONL with token info on assistant messages (different schema from Claude Code)
   583→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeOpenCode {
   584→		return opencode.CalculateTokenUsageFromBytes(data, startOffset)
   585→	}
   586→
   587→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   588→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   589→		// Attempt to parse as Gemini JSON
   590→		transcript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript(data)
   591→		if err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   592→			return geminicli.CalculateTokenUsage(data, startOffset)
   593→		}
   594→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return empty usage
   595→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   596→			return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   597→		}
   598→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   599→	}
   600→
   601→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   602→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
   603→	if err != nil || len(lines) == 0 {
   604→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   605→	}
   606→	// Slice transcript lines to only include checkpoint portion
   607→	if startOffset > 0 && startOffset < len(lines) {
   608→		lines = lines[startOffset:]
   609→	}
   610→	return claudecode.CalculateTokenUsage(lines)
   611→}
   612→
   613→// extractUserPromptsFromLines extracts user prompts from JSONL transcript lines.
   614→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the results.
   615→func extractUserPromptsFromLines(lines []string) []string {
   616→	var prompts []string
   617→	for _, line := range lines {
   618→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   619→		if line == "" {
   620→			continue
   621→		}
   622→
   623→		var entry map[string]interface{}
   624→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry); err != nil {
   625→			continue
   626→		}
   627→
   628→		// Check for user message (supports both "human" and "user" types)
   629→		msgType, ok := entry["type"].(string)
   630→		if !ok || (msgType != "human" && msgType != "user") {
   631→			continue
   632→		}
   633→
   634→		// Extract message content
   635→		message, ok := entry["message"].(map[string]interface{})
   636→		if !ok {
   637→			continue
   638→		}
   639→
   640→		// Handle string content
   641→		if content, ok := message["content"].(string); ok && content != "" {
   642→			cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content)
   643→			if cleaned != "" {
   644→				prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   645→			}
   646→			continue
   647→		}
   648→
   649→		// Handle array content (e.g., multiple text blocks from VSCode)
   650→		if arr, ok := message["content"].([]interface{}); ok {
   651→			var texts []string
   652→			for _, item := range arr {
   653→				if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   654→					if m["type"] == "text" {
   655→						if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   656→							texts = append(texts, text)
   657→						}
   658→					}
   659→				}
   660→			}
   661→			if len(texts) > 0 {
   662→				cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(strings.Join(texts, "\n\n"))
   663→				if cleaned != "" {
   664→					prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   665→				}
   666→			}
   667→		}
   668→	}
   669→	return prompts
   670→}
   671→
   672→// generateContextFromPrompts generates context.md content from a list of prompts.
   673→func generateContextFromPrompts(prompts []string) []byte {
   674→	if len(prompts) == 0 {
   675→		return nil
   676→	}
   677→
   678→	var buf strings.Builder
   679→	buf.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   680→	buf.WriteString("## User Prompts\n\n")
   681→
   682→	for i, prompt := range prompts {
   683→		// Truncate very long prompts for readability
   684→		displayPrompt := prompt
   685→		if len(displayPrompt) > 500 {
   686→			displayPrompt = displayPrompt[:500] + "..."
   687→		}
   688→		buf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("### Prompt %d\n\n", i+1))
   689→		buf.WriteString(displayPrompt)
   690→		buf.WriteString("\n\n")
   691→	}
   692→
   693→	return []byte(buf.String())
   694→}
   695→
   696→// CondenseSessionByID force-condenses a session by its ID and cleans up.
   697→// This is used by "entire doctor" to salvage stuck sessions.
   698→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSessionByID(sessionID string) error {
   699→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "condense-by-id")
   700→
   701→	// Load session state
   702→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   703→	if err != nil {
   704→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   705→	}
   706→	if state == nil {
   707→		return fmt.Errorf("session not found: %s", sessionID)
   708→	}
   709→
   710→	// Open repository
   711→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   712→	if err != nil {
   713→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   714→	}
   715→
   716→	// Generate a checkpoint ID
   717→	checkpointID, err := id.Generate()
   718→	if err != nil {
   719→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   720→	}
   721→
   722→	// Check if shadow branch exists (required for condensation)
   723→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   724→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   725→	_, refErr := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   726→	hasShadowBranch := refErr == nil
   727→
   728→	if !hasShadowBranch {
   729→		// No shadow branch means no checkpoint data to condense.
   730→		// Just clean up the state file.
   731→		logging.Info(ctx, "no shadow branch for session, clearing state only",
   732→			slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   733→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   734→		)
   735→		if err := s.clearSessionState(sessionID); err != nil {
   736→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
   737→		}
   738→		return nil
   739→	}
   740→
   741→	// Condense the session
   742→	result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state, nil)
   743→	if err != nil {
   744→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to condense session: %w", err)
   745→	}
   746→
   747→	logging.Info(ctx, "session condensed by ID",
   748→		slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   749→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   750→		slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   751→	)
   752→
   753→	// Update session state: reset step count and transition to idle
   754→	state.StepCount = 0
   755→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   756→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   757→	state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   758→	state.AttributionBaseCommit = state.BaseCommit
   759→	state.PromptAttributions = nil
   760→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   761→
   762→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   763→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   764→	}
   765→
   766→	// Clean up shadow branch if no other sessions need it
   767→	if err := s.cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(repo, shadowBranchName, sessionID); err != nil {
   768→		logging.Warn(ctx, "failed to clean up shadow branch",
   769→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   770→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   771→		)
   772→		// Non-fatal: condensation succeeded, shadow branch cleanup is best-effort
   773→	}
   774→
   775→	return nil
   776→}
   777→
   778→// cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused deletes a shadow branch if no other active sessions reference it.
   779→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(_ *git.Repository, shadowBranchName, excludeSessionID string) error {
   780→	// List all session states to check if any other session uses this shadow branch
   781→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   782→	if err != nil {
   783→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
   784→	}
   785→
   786→	for _, state := range allStates {
   787→		if state.SessionID == excludeSessionID {
   788→			continue
   789→		}
   790→		otherShadow := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   791→		if otherShadow == shadowBranchName && state.StepCount > 0 {
   792→			// Another session still needs this shadow branch
   793→			return nil
   794→		}
   795→	}
   796→
   797→	// No other sessions need it, delete the shadow branch via CLI
   798→	// (go-git v5's RemoveReference doesn't persist with packed refs/worktrees)
   799→	if err := DeleteBranchCLI(shadowBranchName); err != nil {
   800→		// Branch already gone is not an error
   801→		if errors.Is(err, ErrBranchNotFound) {
   802→			return nil
   803→		}
   804→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove shadow branch: %w", err)
   805→	}
   806→	return nil
   807→}
   808→

    22→func newTrailCmd() *cobra.Command {

    25→		Short: "Manage trails for your branches",
    26→		Long: `Trails are branch-centric work tracking abstractions. They describe the
    27→"why" and "what" of your work, while checkpoints capture the "how" and "when".
    28→
    29→Running 'entire trail' without a subcommand shows the trail for the current
    30→branch, or lists all trails if no trail exists for the current branch.`,
    31→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {

    36→	cmd.AddCommand(newTrailListCmd())

    40→	cmd.AddCommand(newTrailDisableCmd())

    45→// runTrailShow shows the trail for the current branch, or falls through to list.
    46→func runTrailShow(w io.Writer) error {
    47→	branch, err := GetCurrentBranch()
    48→	if err != nil {
    49→		return runTrailListAll(w, "", false)
    50→	}
    51→
    52→	repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()
    53→	if err != nil {
    54→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	store := trail.NewStore(repo)
    58→	metadata, err := store.FindByBranch(branch)
    59→	if err != nil || metadata == nil {
    60→		return runTrailListAll(w, "", false)
    61→	}
    62→
    63→	printTrailDetails(w, metadata)
    64→	return nil
    65→}
    66→
    67→func printTrailDetails(w io.Writer, m *trail.Metadata) {
    68→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Trail: %s\n", m.Title)
    69→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "  ID:      %s\n", m.TrailID)
    70→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Branch:  %s\n", m.Branch)
    71→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Base:    %s\n", m.Base)
    72→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Status:  %s\n", m.Status)
    73→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Author:  %s\n", m.Author)
    74→	if m.Description != "" {
    75→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Description: %s\n", m.Description)
    76→	}
    77→	if len(m.Labels) > 0 {
    78→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Labels:  %s\n", strings.Join(m.Labels, ", "))
    79→	}
    80→	if len(m.Assignees) > 0 {
    81→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Assignees: %s\n", strings.Join(m.Assignees, ", "))
    82→	}
    83→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Created: %s\n", m.CreatedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))
    84→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Updated: %s\n", m.UpdatedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))
    85→}

    87→func newTrailListCmd() *cobra.Command {
    88→	var statusFilter string
    89→	var jsonOutput bool
    90→
    91→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    92→		Use:   "list",
    93→		Short: "List all trails",
    94→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    95→			return runTrailListAll(cmd.OutOrStdout(), statusFilter, jsonOutput)
    96→		},
    97→	}
    98→
    99→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&statusFilter, "status", "", "Filter by status (draft, open, in_progress, in_review, done, closed)")
   100→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&jsonOutput, "json", false, "Output as JSON")
   101→
   102→	return cmd
   103→}
   104→
   105→func runTrailListAll(w io.Writer, statusFilter string, jsonOutput bool) error {
   106→	repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()
   107→	if err != nil {
   108→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   109→	}
   110→
   111→	store := trail.NewStore(repo)
   112→	trails, err := store.List()
   113→	if err != nil {
   114→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list trails: %w", err)
   115→	}
   116→
   117→	if trails == nil {
   118→		trails = []*trail.Metadata{}
   119→	}
   120→
   121→	// Apply status filter
   122→	if statusFilter != "" {
   123→		status := trail.Status(statusFilter)
   124→		if !status.IsValid() {
   125→			return fmt.Errorf("invalid status %q: valid values are %s", statusFilter, formatValidStatuses())
   126→		}
   127→		var filtered []*trail.Metadata
   128→		for _, t := range trails {
   129→			if t.Status == status {
   130→				filtered = append(filtered, t)
   131→			}
   132→		}
   133→		trails = filtered
   134→	}
   135→
   136→	// Sort by updated_at descending
   137→	sort.Slice(trails, func(i, j int) bool {
   138→		return trails[i].UpdatedAt.After(trails[j].UpdatedAt)
   139→	})
   140→
   141→	if jsonOutput {
   142→		enc := json.NewEncoder(w)
   143→		enc.SetIndent("", "  ")
   144→		if err := enc.Encode(trails); err != nil {
   145→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode JSON: %w", err)
   146→		}
   147→		return nil
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	if len(trails) == 0 {
   151→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "No trails found.")
   152→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
   153→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Commands:")
   154→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  entire trail create   Create a trail for the current branch")
   155→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  entire trail list     List all trails")
   156→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  entire trail update   Update trail metadata")
   157→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  entire trail enable   Enable automatic trail creation")
   158→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  entire trail disable  Disable automatic trail creation")
   159→		return nil
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	// Table output
   163→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "%-30s %-40s %-13s %-15s %s\n", "BRANCH", "TITLE", "STATUS", "AUTHOR", "UPDATED")
   164→	for _, t := range trails {
   165→		branch := truncate(t.Branch, 30)
   166→		title := truncate(t.Title, 40)
   167→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "%-30s %-40s %-13s %-15s %s\n",
   168→			branch, title, t.Status, truncate(t.Author, 15), timeAgo(t.UpdatedAt))
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	return nil
   172→}
   173→
   174→func newTrailCreateCmd() *cobra.Command {
   175→	var title, description, base, branch, status string
   176→
   177→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   178→		Use:   "create",
   179→		Short: "Create a trail for the current or a new branch",
   180→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {

   182→		},

   185→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&title, "title", "", "Trail title")

   187→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&base, "base", "", "Base branch (defaults to detected default branch)")
   188→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&branch, "branch", "", "Branch for the trail (defaults to current branch)")
   189→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&status, "status", "", "Initial status (defaults to draft)")
   190→

   194→//nolint:cyclop // sequential steps for creating a trail — splitting would obscure the flow
   195→func runTrailCreate(w, errW io.Writer, title, description, base, branch, statusStr string) error {
   196→	repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()
   197→	if err != nil {
   198→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	// Determine branch
   202→	if branch == "" {
   203→		branch, err = GetCurrentBranch()
   204→		if err != nil {
   205→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to determine current branch: %w", err)
   206→		}
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	// Check if on default branch
   210→	if isDefault, _, defaultErr := IsOnDefaultBranch(); defaultErr == nil && isDefault {
   211→		fmt.Fprintln(errW, "Cannot create a trail on the default branch.")
   212→		fmt.Fprintln(errW, "Create or switch to a feature branch first.")
   213→		return NewSilentError(errors.New("cannot create trail on default branch"))
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	// Determine base branch
   217→	if base == "" {
   218→		base = strategy.GetDefaultBranchName(repo)
   219→		if base == "" {
   220→			base = defaultBaseBranch
   221→		}
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	// Check if trail already exists for this branch
   225→	store := trail.NewStore(repo)
   226→	existing, err := store.FindByBranch(branch)
   227→	if err == nil && existing != nil {
   228→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "Trail already exists for branch %q (ID: %s)\n", branch, existing.TrailID)
   229→		return nil
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	// Determine title
   233→	if title == "" {
   234→		defaultTitle := trail.HumanizeBranchName(branch)
   235→
   236→		// Interactive mode if flags not provided
   237→		if !hasFlag("title") {
   238→			var inputTitle string
   239→			form := NewAccessibleForm(
   240→				huh.NewGroup(
   241→					huh.NewInput().
   242→						Title("Trail title").
   243→						Placeholder(defaultTitle).
   244→						Value(&inputTitle),
   245→				),
   246→			)
   247→			if formErr := form.Run(); formErr != nil {
   248→				return fmt.Errorf("form cancelled: %w", formErr)
   249→			}
   250→			if inputTitle != "" {
   251→				title = inputTitle
   252→			} else {
   253→				title = defaultTitle
   254→			}
   255→		} else {
   256→			title = defaultTitle
   257→		}
   258→	}
   259→
   260→	// Determine status
   261→	var trailStatus trail.Status
   262→	if statusStr != "" {
   263→		trailStatus = trail.Status(statusStr)
   264→		if !trailStatus.IsValid() {
   265→			return fmt.Errorf("invalid status %q: valid values are %s", statusStr, formatValidStatuses())
   266→		}
   267→	} else {
   268→		trailStatus = trail.StatusDraft
   269→	}
   270→
   271→	// Generate trail ID
   272→	trailID, err := trail.GenerateID()
   273→	if err != nil {
   274→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate trail ID: %w", err)
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	// Get git author
   278→	authorName, _ := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
   279→
   280→	now := time.Now()
   281→	metadata := &trail.Metadata{
   282→		TrailID:     trailID,
   283→		Branch:      branch,
   284→		Base:        base,
   285→		Title:       title,
   286→		Description: description,
   287→		Status:      trailStatus,
   288→		Author:      authorName,
   289→		Assignees:   []string{},
   290→		Labels:      []string{},
   291→		CreatedAt:   now,
   292→		UpdatedAt:   now,
   293→	}
   294→
   295→	if err := store.Write(metadata, nil, nil); err != nil {
   296→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create trail: %w", err)
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Created trail %q for branch %s (ID: %s)\n", title, branch, trailID)
   300→	return nil
   301→}

   303→func newTrailUpdateCmd() *cobra.Command {
   304→	var statusStr, title, description, branch string
   305→	var labelAdd, labelRemove []string
   306→
   307→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   308→		Use:   "update",
   309→		Short: "Update trail metadata",
   310→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   311→			return runTrailUpdate(cmd.OutOrStdout(), statusStr, title, description, branch, labelAdd, labelRemove)
   312→		},
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&statusStr, "status", "", "Update status")
   316→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&title, "title", "", "Update title")
   317→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&description, "description", "", "Update description")
   318→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&branch, "branch", "", "Branch to update trail for (defaults to current)")
   319→	cmd.Flags().StringSliceVar(&labelAdd, "add-label", nil, "Add label(s)")
   320→	cmd.Flags().StringSliceVar(&labelRemove, "remove-label", nil, "Remove label(s)")
   321→
   322→	return cmd

   324→
   325→func runTrailUpdate(w io.Writer, statusStr, title, description, branch string, labelAdd, labelRemove []string) error {
   326→	repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()
   327→	if err != nil {
   328→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   329→	}
   330→
   331→	// Determine branch
   332→	if branch == "" {
   333→		branch, err = GetCurrentBranch()
   334→		if err != nil {
   335→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to determine current branch: %w", err)
   336→		}
   337→	}
   338→
   339→	store := trail.NewStore(repo)
   340→	metadata, err := store.FindByBranch(branch)
   341→	if err != nil {
   342→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to find trail: %w", err)
   343→	}
   344→	if metadata == nil {
   345→		return fmt.Errorf("no trail found for branch %q", branch)
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	// Validate status if provided
   349→	if statusStr != "" {
   350→		status := trail.Status(statusStr)
   351→		if !status.IsValid() {
   352→			return fmt.Errorf("invalid status %q: valid values are %s", statusStr, formatValidStatuses())
   353→		}
   354→	}
   355→
   356→	err = store.Update(metadata.TrailID, func(m *trail.Metadata) {
   357→		if statusStr != "" {
   358→			m.Status = trail.Status(statusStr)
   359→		}
   360→		if title != "" {
   361→			m.Title = title
   362→		}
   363→		if description != "" {
   364→			m.Description = description
   365→		}
   366→		for _, l := range labelAdd {
   367→			if !containsString(m.Labels, l) {
   368→				m.Labels = append(m.Labels, l)
   369→			}
   370→		}
   371→		for _, l := range labelRemove {
   372→			m.Labels = removeString(m.Labels, l)
   373→		}
   374→	})
   375→	if err != nil {
   376→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update trail: %w", err)
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Updated trail for branch %s\n", branch)
   380→	return nil
   381→}

   396→func runTrailEnable(w io.Writer) error {
   397→	s, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   398→	if err != nil {
   399→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   400→	}
   401→
   402→	if s.StrategyOptions != nil {
   403→		delete(s.StrategyOptions, "trails")

   405→
   406→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(s); err != nil {
   407→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   408→	}
   409→
   410→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Trails enabled.")

   414→func newTrailDisableCmd() *cobra.Command {

   418→		Use:   "disable",
   419→		Short: "Disable automatic trail creation and pushing",
   420→		Long: `Disable trail auto-creation on session start and pushing the trails branch
   421→on git push. Existing trails are preserved.
   422→
   423→By default, writes to .entire/settings.local.json. Use --project to write to
   424→.entire/settings.json instead.`,
   425→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   426→			return runTrailDisable(cmd.OutOrStdout(), useProjectSettings)
   427→		},
   428→	}

   431→	return cmd

   433→
   434→func runTrailDisable(w io.Writer, useProjectSettings bool) error {
   435→	s, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   436→	if err != nil {
   437→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   438→	}
   439→
   440→	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
   441→		s.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]any)
   442→	}
   443→	s.StrategyOptions["trails"] = false
   444→
   445→	if useProjectSettings {
   446→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(s); err != nil {
   447→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   448→		}
   449→	} else {
   450→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(s); err != nil {
   451→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   452→		}
   453→	}
   454→
   455→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Trails disabled.")
   456→	return nil
   457→}
   458→
   459→// defaultBaseBranch is the fallback base branch name when it cannot be determined.
   460→const defaultBaseBranch = "main"
   461→
   462→// AutoCreateTrail creates a trail automatically for the current branch if one doesn't exist.
   463→// This is called during session start for non-main branches.
   464→// If prompt is non-empty, its first line is used as the trail title instead of the branch name.
   465→func AutoCreateTrail(repo *git.Repository, branchName, baseBranch, prompt string) error {
   466→	store := trail.NewStore(repo)
   467→
   468→	existing, err := store.FindByBranch(branchName)
   469→	if err == nil && existing != nil {
   470→		return nil // Trail already exists
   471→	}
   472→
   473→	trailID, err := trail.GenerateID()
   474→	if err != nil {
   475→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate trail ID: %w", err)
   476→	}
   477→
   478→	authorName, _ := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
   479→	now := time.Now()
   480→
   481→	title := titleFromPrompt(prompt)
   482→	if title == "" {
   483→		title = trail.HumanizeBranchName(branchName)
   484→	}
   485→
   486→	metadata := &trail.Metadata{
   487→		TrailID:   trailID,
   488→		Branch:    branchName,
   489→		Base:      baseBranch,
   490→		Title:     title,
   491→		Status:    trail.StatusInProgress,
   492→		Author:    authorName,
   493→		Assignees: []string{},
   494→		Labels:    []string{},
   495→		CreatedAt: now,
   496→		UpdatedAt: now,
   497→	}
   498→
   499→	if err := store.Write(metadata, nil, nil); err != nil {
   500→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create trail: %w", err)
   501→	}
   502→
   503→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created trail for branch: %s\n", branchName)
   504→	return nil
   505→}
   506→
   507→// titleFromPrompt extracts a trail title from the user's prompt.
   508→// Uses the first line, trimmed and truncated to 80 characters.
   509→// Returns empty string if prompt is empty.
   510→func titleFromPrompt(prompt string) string {
   511→	if prompt == "" {
   512→		return ""
   513→	}
   514→	line, _, _ := strings.Cut(prompt, "\n")
   515→	title := strings.TrimSpace(line)
   516→	if len(title) > 80 {
   517→		title = title[:77] + "..."
   518→	}
   519→	return title
   520→}
   521→
   522→// hasFlag is a simple helper that checks os.Args for --flag presence.
   523→// Used to distinguish between "flag not provided" and "flag provided with empty value".
   524→func hasFlag(name string) bool {
   525→	for _, arg := range os.Args {
   526→		if arg == "--"+name || strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--"+name+"=") {
   527→			return true
   528→		}
   529→	}
   530→	return false
   531→}
   532→
   533→func truncate(s string, maxLen int) string {
   534→	if len(s) <= maxLen {
   535→		return s
   536→	}
   537→	if maxLen <= 3 {
   538→		return s[:maxLen]
   539→	}
   540→	return s[:maxLen-3] + "..."
   541→}
   542→
   543→func formatValidStatuses() string {
   544→	statuses := trail.ValidStatuses()
   545→	names := make([]string, len(statuses))
   546→	for i, s := range statuses {
   547→		names[i] = string(s)
   548→	}
   549→	return strings.Join(names, ", ")
   550→}
   551→
   552→func containsString(slice []string, s string) bool {
   553→	for _, v := range slice {
   554→		if v == s {
   555→			return true
   556→		}
   557→	}
   558→	return false
   559→}
   560→
   561→func removeString(slice []string, s string) []string {
   562→	var result []string
   563→	for _, v := range slice {
   564→		if v != s {
   565→			result = append(result, v)
   566→		}
   567→	}
   568→	return result
   569→}
   570→

    28→// isNotFoundError checks if an error represents a "not found" condition in go-git.
    29→// This includes entry not found, file not found, directory not found, and object not found.
    30→func isNotFoundError(err error) bool {
    31→	return errors.Is(err, object.ErrEntryNotFound) ||
    32→		errors.Is(err, object.ErrFileNotFound) ||
    33→		errors.Is(err, object.ErrDirectoryNotFound) ||
    34→		errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrObjectNotFound) ||
    35→		errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrReferenceNotFound)
    36→}
    37→
    38→// commitOrHead attempts to create a commit. If the commit would be empty (files already
    39→// committed), it returns HEAD hash instead. This handles the case where files were
    40→// modified during a session but already committed by the user before the hook runs.
    41→func commitOrHead(repo *git.Repository, worktree *git.Worktree, msg string, author *object.Signature) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
    42→	commitHash, err := worktree.Commit(msg, &git.CommitOptions{Author: author})
    43→	if errors.Is(err, git.ErrEmptyCommit) {
    44→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No changes to commit (files already committed)\n")
    45→		head, err := repo.Head()
    46→		if err != nil {
    47→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
    48→		}
    49→		return head.Hash(), nil
    50→	}
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to commit: %w", err)
    53→	}
    54→	return commitHash, nil
    55→}
    56→
    57→// AutoCommitStrategy implements the auto-commit strategy:
    58→// - Code changes are committed to the active branch (like commit strategy)
    59→// - Session logs are committed to a shadow branch (like manual-commit strategy)
    60→// - Code commits can reference the shadow branch via trailers
    61→type AutoCommitStrategy struct {
    62→	// checkpointStore manages checkpoint data on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
    63→	checkpointStore *checkpoint.GitStore
    64→	// checkpointStoreOnce ensures thread-safe lazy initialization
    65→	checkpointStoreOnce sync.Once
    66→	// checkpointStoreErr captures any error during initialization
    67→	checkpointStoreErr error
    68→}
    69→
    70→// getCheckpointStore returns the checkpoint store, initializing it lazily if needed.
    71→// Thread-safe via sync.Once.
    72→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) getCheckpointStore() (*checkpoint.GitStore, error) {
    73→	s.checkpointStoreOnce.Do(func() {
    74→		repo, err := OpenRepository()
    75→		if err != nil {
    76→			s.checkpointStoreErr = fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
    77→			return
    78→		}
    79→		s.checkpointStore = checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
    80→	})
    81→	return s.checkpointStore, s.checkpointStoreErr
    82→}
    83→
    84→// NewAutoCommitStrategy creates a new AutoCommitStrategy instance.
    85→//
    86→
    87→func NewAutoCommitStrategy() Strategy {
    88→	return &AutoCommitStrategy{}
    89→}
    90→
    91→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) Name() string {
    92→	return StrategyNameAutoCommit
    93→}
    94→
    95→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) Description() string {
    96→	return "Auto-commits code to active branch with metadata on entire/checkpoints/v1"
    97→}
    98→
    99→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) ValidateRepository() error {
   100→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   101→	if err != nil {
   102→		return fmt.Errorf("not a git repository: %w", err)
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	_, err = repo.Worktree()
   106→	if err != nil {
   107→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to access worktree: %w", err)
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	return nil
   111→}
   112→
   113→// PrePush is called by the git pre-push hook before pushing to a remote.
   114→// It pushes the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch alongside the user's push.
   115→// Configuration options (stored in .entire/settings.json under strategy_options.push_sessions):
   116→//   - "auto": always push automatically
   117→//   - "prompt" (default): ask user with option to enable auto
   118→//   - "false"/"off"/"no": never push
   119→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) PrePush(remote string) error {
   120→	if err := pushSessionsBranchCommon(remote, paths.MetadataBranchName); err != nil {
   121→		return err
   122→	}
   123→	return PushTrailsBranch(remote)
   124→}
   125→
   126→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) SaveStep(ctx StepContext) error {
   127→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   128→	if err != nil {
   129→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   130→	}
   131→
   132→	// Generate checkpoint ID for this commit
   133→	cpID, err := id.Generate()
   134→	if err != nil {
   135→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   136→	}
   137→	// Step 1: Commit code changes to active branch with checkpoint ID trailer
   138→	// We do code first to avoid orphaned metadata if this step fails.
   139→	// If metadata commit fails after this, the code commit exists but GetRewindPoints
   140→	// already handles missing metadata gracefully (skips commits without metadata).
   141→	codeResult, err := s.commitCodeToActive(repo, ctx, cpID)
   142→	if err != nil {
   143→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit code to active branch: %w", err)
   144→	}
   145→
   146→	// If no code commit was created (no changes), skip metadata creation
   147→	// This prevents orphaned metadata commits that don't correspond to any code commit
   148→	if !codeResult.Created {
   149→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   150→		logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint skipped (no changes)",
   151→			slog.String("strategy", "auto-commit"),
   152→			slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   153→		)
   154→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipped checkpoint (no changes since last commit)\n")
   155→		return nil
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	// Step 2: Commit metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch using sharded path
   159→	// Path is <checkpointID[:2]>/<checkpointID[2:]>/ for direct lookup
   160→	_, err = s.commitMetadataToMetadataBranch(repo, ctx, cpID)
   161→	if err != nil {
   162→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch: %w", err)
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	// Link checkpoint to trail (best-effort)
   166→	appendCheckpointToTrail(repo, cpID, codeResult.CommitHash, nil)
   167→
   168→	// Log checkpoint creation
   169→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   170→	logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint saved",
   171→		slog.String("strategy", "auto-commit"),
   172→		slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   173→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
   174→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(ctx.ModifiedFiles)),
   175→		slog.Int("new_files", len(ctx.NewFiles)),
   176→		slog.Int("deleted_files", len(ctx.DeletedFiles)),
   177→	)
   178→
   179→	return nil
   180→}
   181→
   182→// commitCodeResult contains the result of committing code to the active branch.
   183→type commitCodeResult struct {
   184→	CommitHash plumbing.Hash
   185→	Created    bool // True if a new commit was created, false if skipped (no changes)
   186→}
   187→
   188→// commitCodeToActive commits code changes to the active branch.
   189→// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer for metadata lookup that survives amend/rebase.
   190→// Returns the result containing commit hash and whether a commit was created.
   191→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) commitCodeToActive(repo *git.Repository, ctx StepContext, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (commitCodeResult, error) {
   192→	// Check if there are any code changes to commit
   193→	if len(ctx.ModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(ctx.NewFiles) == 0 && len(ctx.DeletedFiles) == 0 {
   194→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No code changes to commit to active branch\n")
   195→		// Return current HEAD hash but mark as not created
   196→		head, err := repo.Head()
   197→		if err != nil {
   198→			return commitCodeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   199→		}
   200→		return commitCodeResult{CommitHash: head.Hash(), Created: false}, nil
```

     1→package strategy

     3→import (
     4→	"time"

     6→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"

    10→)
    11→
    12→const (
    13→	// logsOnlyScanLimit is the maximum number of commits to scan for logs-only points.
    14→	logsOnlyScanLimit = 50
    15→
    16→	// maxFirstPromptRunes is the maximum rune length for FirstPrompt stored in session state.
    17→	maxFirstPromptRunes = 100
    18→)
    19→
    20→// truncatePromptForStorage collapses whitespace and truncates a user prompt
    21→// for storage in FirstPrompt fields.
    22→func truncatePromptForStorage(prompt string) string {
    23→	return stringutil.TruncateRunes(stringutil.CollapseWhitespace(prompt), maxFirstPromptRunes, "...")
    24→}
    25→
    26→// SessionState is an alias for session.State.
    27→// Previously this was a separate struct with manual conversion functions.
    28→type SessionState = session.State
    29→
    30→// PromptAttribution is an alias for session.PromptAttribution.
    31→type PromptAttribution = session.PromptAttribution
    32→
    33→// CheckpointInfo represents checkpoint metadata stored on the sessions branch.
    34→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpoint_id[:2]>/<checkpoint_id[2:]>/
    35→type CheckpointInfo struct {
    36→	CheckpointID     id.CheckpointID `json:"checkpoint_id"` // 12-hex-char from Entire-Checkpoint trailer, used as directory path
    37→	SessionID        string          `json:"session_id"`
    38→	CreatedAt        time.Time       `json:"created_at"`
    39→	CheckpointsCount int             `json:"checkpoints_count"`
    40→	FilesTouched     []string        `json:"files_touched"`
    41→	Agent            agent.AgentType `json:"agent,omitempty"` // Human-readable agent name (e.g., "Claude Code")
    42→	IsTask           bool            `json:"is_task,omitempty"`
    43→	ToolUseID        string          `json:"tool_use_id,omitempty"`
    44→	SessionCount     int             `json:"session_count,omitempty"` // Number of sessions (1 if omitted)
    45→	SessionIDs       []string        `json:"session_ids,omitempty"`   // All session IDs in this checkpoint
    46→}
    47→
    48→// CondenseResult contains the result of a session condensation operation.
    49→type CondenseResult struct {
    50→	CheckpointID         id.CheckpointID // 12-hex-char from Entire-Checkpoint trailer, used as directory path
    51→	SessionID            string
    52→	CheckpointsCount     int
    53→	FilesTouched         []string
    54→	Prompts              []string // User prompts from the condensed session
    55→	TotalTranscriptLines int      // Total lines in transcript after this condensation
    56→}
    57→
    58→// ExtractedSessionData contains data extracted from a shadow branch.
    59→type ExtractedSessionData struct {

  "content": "# Plan: LLM-Generated Trail Titles & Descriptions\n\n## Context\n\nParts A (checkpoints.json) and B (prompt-based titles) are already implemented and passing CI. However, `titleFromPrompt()` just truncates the first line of the user's prompt, which produces mediocre titles like \"implement the login flow for...\" instead of semantic titles like \"User authentication login flow\".\n\nThe user wants an LLM to generate proper trail titles and descriptions from the session transcript. This should happen **at first condensation** (PostCommit), not TurnStart, because:\n- Rich context is available (prompts + transcript + files touched)\n- Only happens once per trail (first commit)\n- No concurrency issues (runs synchronously in the hook process)\n- Piggybacks on existing summarize flow\n\n## Approach\n\n### C1. Add `GenerateTrailTitle` to the summarize package\n\n**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/summarize/trail_title.go` (new file)\n\nAdd a prompt template and generation function for trail titles:\n\n```go\nconst trailTitlePromptTemplate = `Analyze this development session transcript and generate a concise title and description for a code review / pull request.\n\n<transcript>\n%s\n</transcript>\n\nReturn a JSON object with this exact structure:\n{\n  \"title\": \"Short imperative title (max 80 chars, e.g. 'Add user authentication login flow')\",\n  \"description\": \"1-3 sentence description of what was accomplished and why\"\n}\n\nGuidelines:\n- Title should be imperative mood, like a commit message subject\n- Title should capture the core intent, not implementation details\n- Description should explain the \"what\" and \"why\", not the \"how\"\n- Be concise and specific\n- Return ONLY the JSON object, no markdown formatting`\n\ntype TrailTitleResult struct {\n    Title       string `json:\"title\"`\n    Description string `json:\"description\"`\n}\n\nfunc GenerateTrailTitle(ctx context.Context, transcriptBytes []byte, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType) (*TrailTitleResult, error)\n```\n\n- Reuses `BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes()` and `FormatCondensedTranscript()` to prepare transcript\n- Uses `ClaudeGenerator`'s subprocess pattern but with haiku model and the trail title prompt\n- Parses JSON response into `TrailTitleResult`\n- Returns error if generation fails (caller handles best-effort)\n\nImplementation: Extract the Claude CLI invocation into a lower-level helper (or just inline it like `ClaudeGenerator.Generate` does) using `haiku` as the model. Reuse `stripGitEnv()` and `extractJSONFromMarkdown()`.\n\n### C2. Add `generateTrailTitleFromTranscript` helper in strategy\n\n**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`\n\nAdd helper that wraps the summarize call and updates the trail:\n\n```go\nfunc generateTrailTitleFromTranscript(repo *git.Repository, branchName string, transcript []byte, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType) {\n    if !settings.IsSummarizeEnabled() {\n        return\n    }\n\n    store := trail.NewStore(repo)\n    existing, err := store.FindByBranch(branchName)\n    if err != nil || existing == nil {\n        return\n    }\n\n    // Only generate once: skip if description already set\n    if existing.Description != \"\" {\n        return\n    }\n\n    ctx := context.Background()\n    result, err := summarize.GenerateTrailTitle(ctx, transcript, filesTouched, agentType)\n    if err != nil {\n        logging.Debug(ctx, \"trail title generation failed\", slog.String(\"error\", err.Error()))\n        return\n    }\n\n    // Update trail with generated title and description\n    _ = store.Update(existing.TrailID, func(m *trail.Metadata) {\n        if result.Title != \"\" {\n            m.Title = result.Title\n        }\n        if result.Description != \"\" {\n            m.Description = result.Description\n        }\n    })\n}\n```\n\n### C3. Wire into condensation flow (manual-commit)\n\n**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`\n\nIn `condenseAndUpdateState()` (line ~825), after the existing `appendCheckpointToTrail()` call, add:\n\n```go\n// Generate trail title/description from transcript (best-effort, first condensation only)\nbranchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)\nif branchName != \"\" && branchName != GetDefaultBranchName(repo) {\n    // Use scoped transcript for this checkpoint portion\n    var scopedTranscript []byte\n    if state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {\n        scopedTranscript = geminicli.SliceFromMessage(result.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)\n    } else {\n        scopedTranscript = transcript.SliceFromLine(result.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)\n    }\n    generateTrailTitleFromTranscript(repo, branchName, scopedTranscript, result.FilesTouched, state.AgentType)\n}\n```\n\nWait — `CondenseResult` doesn't have `Transcript`. Let me check... it has `Prompts` and `FilesTouched`. The transcript is in `sessionData` which is local to `CondenseSession()`.\n\n**Better approach**: Add `Transcript []byte` to `CondenseResult` (or pass `sessionData.Transcript` separately). Actually, reading the code more carefully, `condenseAndUpdateState` doesn't have access to the transcript — it only gets `*CondenseResult`.\n\nTwo options:\n1.   "content": "# Plan: LLM-Generated Trail Titles & Descriptions\n\n## Context\n\nParts A (checkpoints.json) and B (prompt-based titles) are already implemented and passing CI. However, `titleFromPrompt()` just truncates the first line of the user's prompt, producing mediocre titles like \"implement the login flow for...\" instead of semantic titles like \"User authentication login flow\".\n\nThe user wants an LLM to generate proper trail titles and descriptions from the session transcript. This happens **at first condensation** (PostCommit) because:\n- Rich context available (prompts + transcript + files touched)\n- Only once per trail (first commit)\n- No concurrency issues (synchronous in hook process)\n- Piggybacks on existing summarize flow\n\n## Implementation\n\n### C1. New file: `cmd/entire/cli/summarize/trail_title.go`\n\nAdd trail title/description generation function reusing existing summarize infrastructure:\n\n```go\npackage summarize\n\nconst trailTitleModel = \"haiku\"\n\nconst trailTitlePromptTemplate = `Analyze this development session transcript and generate a title and description.\n\n<transcript>\n%s\n</transcript>\n\nReturn a JSON object:\n{\n  \"title\": \"Short imperative title (max 80 chars)\",\n  \"description\": \"1-3 sentence description of what was accomplished and why\"\n}\n\nGuidelines:\n- Title: imperative mood, captures core intent (e.g. \"Add user authentication flow\")\n- Description: explain \"what\" and \"why\", not \"how\"\n- Return ONLY the JSON object`\n\ntype TrailTitleResult struct {\n    Title       string `json:\"title\"`\n    Description string `json:\"description\"`\n}\n\nfunc GenerateTrailTitle(ctx context.Context, transcriptBytes []byte, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType) (*TrailTitleResult, error) {\n    // Build condensed transcript (reuse existing BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes + FormatCondensedTranscript)\n    // Invoke Claude CLI with haiku model (reuse stripGitEnv, extractJSONFromMarkdown patterns from claude.go)\n    // Parse JSON response into TrailTitleResult\n}\n```\n\nKey reuse:\n- `BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes()` + `FormatCondensedTranscript()` for transcript formatting\n- `stripGitEnv()` and `extractJSONFromMarkdown()` from `claude.go`\n- Same subprocess pattern: `claude --print --output-format json --model haiku --setting-sources \"\"`\n\n### C2. Add `Transcript` field to `CondenseResult`\n\n**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_types.go`\n\n```go\ntype CondenseResult struct {\n    ...existing fields...\n    Transcript []byte   // Raw transcript bytes for downstream consumers\n}\n```\n\n**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go`\n\nIn `CondenseSession()` return block (~line 250), add:\n```go\nTranscript: sessionData.Transcript,\n```\n\n### C3. Add `generateTrailTitleFromTranscript` helper\n\n**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`\n\n```go\nfunc generateTrailTitleFromTranscript(repo *git.Repository, branchName string, transcript []byte, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType) {\n    if !settings.IsSummarizeEnabled() {\n        return\n    }\n    store := trail.NewStore(repo)\n    existing, err := store.FindByBranch(branchName)\n    if err != nil || existing == nil {\n        return\n    }\n    // Only generate once: skip if description already set\n    if existing.Description != \"\" {\n        return\n    }\n    ctx := context.Background()\n    result, err := summarize.GenerateTrailTitle(ctx, transcript, filesTouched, agentType)\n    if err != nil {\n        logging.Debug(ctx, \"trail title generation failed\", slog.String(\"error\", err.Error()))\n        return\n    }\n    //nolint:errcheck // best-effort: trail title generation is non-critical\n    store.Update(existing.TrailID, func(m *trail.Metadata) {\n        if result.Title != \"\" {\n            m.Title = result.Title\n        }\n        if result.Description != \"\" {\n            m.Description = result.Description\n        }\n    })\n}\n```\n\n### C4. Wire into `condenseAndUpdateState` (manual-commit)\n\n**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`\n\nIn `condenseAndUpdateState()`, after the existing `appendCheckpointToTrail()` call (~line 825):\n\n```go\n// Generate trail title/description from transcript (best-effort, first condensation only)\nbranchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)\nif branchName != \"\" && branchName != GetDefaultBranchName(repo) {\n    generateTrailTitleFromTranscript(repo, branchName, result.Transcript, result.FilesTouched, state.AgentType)\n}\n```\n\n### C5. Wire into `SaveStep` (auto-commit)\n\n**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/auto_commit.go`\n\nIn `SaveStep()`, after the existing `appendCheckpointToTrail()` call (~line 166):\n\n```go\n// Generate trail title/description (best-effort, first condensation only)\nbranchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)\nif branchName != \"\" && branchName != GetDefaultBranchName(repo) && ctx.TranscriptPath != \"\" {\n    if transcriptData, readErr := os.ReadFile(ctx.TranscriptPath); readErr == nil {\n        filesTouched := mergeFilesTouched(nil, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles)\n        generateTrailTitleFromTranscript(repo, branchName, transcriptData, filesTouched, ctx.AgentType)\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Files Summary\n\n| File | Change |\n|------|--------|\n| `cmd/entire/cli/summarize/trail_title.go` | **New**: `GenerateTrailTitle()`, prompt template, `TrailTitleResult` type |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_types.go` | Add `Transcript []byte` to `CondenseResult` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go` | Populate `Transcript` in `CondenseResult` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` | Add `generateTrailTitleFromTranscript()`, wire after condensation |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/auto_commit.go` | Wire `generateTrailTitleFromTranscript()` after save |\n\n## Design Decisions\n\n1. **Haiku model** — trail titles are simple; haiku is fast (~1-2s) and cheap\n2. **One-time gate** — `Description != \"\"` ensures LLM is only called on first condensation per trail\n3. **Settings gate** — reuses `settings.IsSummarizeEnabled()`; no new setting\n4. **Best-effort** — all errors logged and swallowed; non-critical metadata\n5. **Synchronous** — runs in PostCommit hook; no goroutines, no race conditions\n6. **Reuses transcript infrastructure** — `BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes` + `FormatCondensedTranscript`\n\n## Verification\n\n```bash\nmise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci\n```\n\nManual test:\n1. Create a branch, enable entire\n2. Run an agent session, make a commit\n3. Check `entire trail` output — should show LLM-generated title and description\n"
}

Wire into `SaveStep` (auto-commit)\n\n**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/auto_commit.go`\n\nIn `SaveStep()`, after the existing `appendCheckpointToTrail()` call (~line 166):\n\n```go\n// Generate trail title/description (best-effort, first condensation only)\nbranchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)\nif branchName != \"\" && branchName != GetDefaultBranchName(repo) && ctx.TranscriptPath != \"\" {\n    if transcriptData, readErr := os.ReadFile(ctx.TranscriptPath); readErr == nil {\n        filesTouched := mergeFilesTouched(nil, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles)\n        generateTrailTitleFromTranscript(repo, branchName, transcriptData, filesTouched, ctx.AgentType)\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Files Summary\n\n| File | Change |\n|------|--------|\n| `cmd/entire/cli/summarize/trail_title.go` | **New**: `GenerateTrailTitle()`, prompt template, `TrailTitleResult` type |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_types.go` | Add `Transcript []byte` to `CondenseResult` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go` | Populate `Transcript` in `CondenseResult` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` | Add `generateTrailTitleFromTranscript()`, wire after condensation |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/auto_commit.go` | Wire `generateTrailTitleFromTranscript()` after save |\n\n## Design Decisions\n\n1. **Haiku model** — trail titles are simple; haiku is fast (~1-2s) and cheap\n2. **One-time gate** — `Description != \"\"` ensures LLM is only called on first condensation per trail\n3. **Settings gate** — reuses `settings.IsSummarizeEnabled()`; no new setting\n4. **Best-effort** — all errors logged and swallowed; non-critical metadata\n5. **Synchronous** — runs in PostCommit hook; no goroutines, no race conditions\n6. **Reuses transcript infrastructure** — `BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes` + `FormatCondensedTranscript`\n\n## Verification\n\n```bash\nmise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci\n```\n\nManual test:\n1. Create a branch, enable entire\n2. Run an agent session, make a commit\n3. Check `entire trail` output — should show LLM-generated title and description\n"
}
