## Summary: Agent CLI Tool Invocation Patterns

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's a complete analysis of how different agent CLI tools can be invoked for non-interactive text generation:

### Agent Types and Registration

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/registry.go`

Three agents are registered in the system:
1. **Claude Code** - Registry key: `"claude-code"` (AgentName), Type: `"Claude Code"` (AgentType)
2. **Gemini CLI** - Registry key: `"gemini"` (AgentName), Type: `"Gemini CLI"` (AgentType)
3. **OpenCode** - Registry key: `"opencode"` (AgentName), Type: `"OpenCode"` (AgentType)

Each agent self-registers via `init()` functions in their respective packages.

### Agent Interface Structure

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go`

The `Agent` interface has four method groups:

1. **Identity** (5 methods)
   - `Name()` → AgentName (registry key)
   - `Type()` → AgentType (display name)
   - `Description()` → string
   - `IsPreview()` → bool
   - `DetectPresence()` → (bool, error)
   - `ProtectedDirs()` → []string (config directories to protect during rewind)

2. **Event Mapping** (2 methods)
   - `HookNames()` → []string (supported hook verbs)
   - `ParseHookEvent()` → *Event (normalized lifecycle event)

3. **Transcript Storage** (3 methods)
   - `ReadTranscript()` → []byte
   - `ChunkTranscript()` → [][]byte (format-aware chunking)
   - `ReassembleTranscript()` → []byte

4. **Legacy** (8 methods) - Session/resume command formatting

### Optional Interfaces for Extended Capabilities

- **TranscriptAnalyzer** - Transcript parsing (extract files, prompts, summaries)
- **TranscriptPreparer** - Pre-read flush requirements (e.g., Claude Code's async flush)
- **TokenCalculator** - Token usage computation
- **SubagentAwareExtractor** - Files/tokens including spawned subagents
- **HookSupport** - Hook installation/management
- **FileWatcher** - File-based session detection

### Non-Interactive CLI Invocation Pattern: Claude Code

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/summarize/claude.go`

This is the **canonical pattern** for programmatically invoking an agent CLI for text generation:

```go
type ClaudeGenerator struct {
    ClaudePath string  // Path to "claude" executable (defaults to "claude" in PATH)
    Model      string  // Model to use (defaults to "sonnet")
    CommandRunner func(ctx context.Context, name string, args ...string) *exec.Cmd
}

func (g *ClaudeGenerator) Generate(ctx context.Context, input Input) (*checkpoint.Summary, error) {
    // 1. Format transcript
    transcriptText := FormatCondensedTranscript(input)
    prompt := fmt.Sprintf(summarizationPromptTemplate, transcriptText)
    
    // 2. Invoke CLI with non-interactive flags
    cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, 
        g.ClaudePath,
        "--print",              // Non-interactive output
        "--output-format", "json",  // Machine-readable format
        "--model", model,
        "--setting-sources", "", // Skip all settings (MCP, hooks, etc.)
    )
    
    // 3. Isolate from git repo
    cmd.Dir = os.TempDir()
    cmd.Env = stripGitEnv(os.Environ())  // Remove GIT_* vars
    
    // 4. Pass prompt via stdin
    cmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(prompt)
    
    // 5. Capture and parse output
    var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
    cmd.Stdout = &stdout
    cmd.Stderr = &stderr
    err := cmd.Run()
    
    // 6. Parse JSON response
    var cliResponse struct { Result string }
    json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &cliResponse)
}
```

**Key characteristics:**
- **Flag:** `--print` for non-interactive generation
- **Output format:** `--output-format json` for machine-readable results
- **Model selection:** `--model sonnet` (or configurable)
- **Settings isolation:** `--setting-sources ""` to avoid loading MCP servers, hooks
- **Process isolation:** Run in `os.TempDir()` with git env vars stripped
- **Input method:** stdin (prompt piped via `strings.NewReader`)
- **Output:** JSON wrapped in a `{"result": "..."}` envelope

### Gemini CLI and OpenCode CLI Status

**Files:**
- `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/gemini.go`
- `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/opencode.go`

**Current situation:**
- **Gemini CLI:** No evidence of non-interactive/print mode in codebase
- **OpenCode CLI:** Has `opencode session delete <id>` and `opencode import <file>` commands, but no generation/printing mode

The codebase only implements programmatic invocation for **Claude Code**.

### OpenCode CLI Command Invocation Pattern

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/cli_commands.go`

For reference, OpenCode uses `exec.CommandContext` with timeout wrapping:

```go
func runOpenCodeSessionDelete(sessionID string) error {
    ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30 * time.Second)
    defer cancel()
    
    cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "opencode", "session", "delete", sessionID)
    output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
    // Handle timeout, parse output, etc.
}

func runOpenCodeImport(exportFilePath string) error {
    ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30 * time.Second)
    defer cancel()
    
    cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "opencode", "import", exportFilePath)
    output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
}
```

### Transcript Format Handling

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/summarize/summarize.go`

Different agents have different transcript formats:

| Agent | Format | Position Unit | Splitting Method |
|-------|--------|---------------|------------------|
| Claude Code | JSONL | Line count | Split at newlines |
| Gemini CLI | JSON (message array) | Message count | Distribute messages across chunks |
| OpenCode | JSONL | Line count | Split at newlines |

The summary generation handles all three via `BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes()` which dispatches to format-specific parsers.

### Trail Title Generation Use Case

**Files:** 
- `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/trail_cmd.go` (line 481-519)
- `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/trail/trail.go` (line 159-182)

For generating trail titles from transcripts (which is likely your use case):

Current implementation uses `trail.HumanizeBranchName()` as fallback:
```go
// From trail_cmd.go AutoCreateTrail()
title := titleFromPrompt(prompt)  // Extract first line of user's prompt
if title == "" {
    title = trail.HumanizeBranchName(branchName)  // Fallback: humanize branch name
}
```

To use Claude for AI-generated titles, you'd adapt the `ClaudeGenerator` pattern from summarize.go with a simpler prompt.

    26→// GeminiCLIAgent implements the Agent interface for Gemini CLI.
    27→//

    29→type GeminiCLIAgent struct{}
    30→
    31→func NewGeminiCLIAgent() agent.Agent {

    34→
    35→// Name returns the agent registry key.

    48→}

    51→
    52→// DetectPresence checks if Gemini CLI is configured in the repository.
    53→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    54→	// Get repo root to check for .gemini directory
    55→	// This is needed because the CLI may be run from a subdirectory
    56→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		// Not in a git repo, fall back to CWD-relative check
    59→		repoRoot = "."
    60→	}
    61→
    62→	// Check for .gemini directory
    63→	geminiDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini")
    64→	if _, err := os.Stat(geminiDir); err == nil {
    65→		return true, nil
    66→	}
    67→	// Check for .gemini/settings.json
    68→	settingsFile := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", "settings.json")
    69→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsFile); err == nil {
    70→		return true, nil
    71→	}
    72→	return false, nil
    73→}
    74→
    75→// GetSessionID extracts the session ID from hook input.
    76→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {
    77→	return input.SessionID
    78→}
    79→
    80→// ProtectedDirs returns directories that Gemini uses for config/state.
    81→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".gemini"} }
    82→
    83→// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Gemini session file.
    84→// Gemini names files as session-<date>-<shortid>.json where shortid is the first 8 chars
    85→// of the session UUID. This searches for an existing file matching the pattern, falling
    86→// back to constructing a filename matching Gemini's convention if no match is found.
    87→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
    88→	// Try to find existing file matching Gemini's naming convention:
    89→	// session-*-<first8chars>.json
    90→	shortID := agentSessionID
    91→	if len(shortID) > 8 {
    92→		shortID = shortID[:8]
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	pattern := filepath.Join(sessionDir, "session-*-"+shortID+".json")
    96→	matches, err := filepath.Glob(pattern)
    97→	if err == nil && len(matches) > 0 {
    98→		// Return the most recent match (last alphabetically, since date is in the name)
    99→		return matches[len(matches)-1]
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	// Fallback: construct filename matching Gemini's convention: session-<timestamp>-<id[:8]>.json
   103→	timestamp := time.Now().UTC().Format("2006-01-02T15-04")
   104→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, "session-"+timestamp+"-"+shortID+".json")
   105→}
   106→
   107→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Gemini stores session transcripts.
   108→// Gemini stores sessions in ~/.gemini/tmp/<project-hash>/chats/
   109→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   110→	// Check for test environment override
   111→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   112→		return override, nil
   113→	}
   114→
   115→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   116→	if err != nil {
   117→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   118→	}
   119→
   120→	// Gemini uses a SHA256 hash of the project path for the directory name
   121→	projectDir := GetProjectHash(repoPath)
   122→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".gemini", "tmp", projectDir, "chats"), nil
   123→}
   124→
   125→// ReadSession reads a session from Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   126→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSON bytes.
   127→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   128→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   129→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   130→	}
   131→
   132→	// Read the raw JSON file
   133→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   134→	if err != nil {
   135→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   139→	modifiedFiles, err := ExtractModifiedFiles(data)
   140→	if err != nil {
   141→		// Non-fatal: we can still return the session without modified files
   142→		modifiedFiles = nil
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   146→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   147→		AgentName:     g.Name(),
   148→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   149→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   150→		NativeData:    data,
   151→		ModifiedFiles: modifiedFiles,
   152→	}, nil
   153→}
   154→
   155→// WriteSession writes a session to Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   156→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSON bytes.
   157→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   158→	if session == nil {
   159→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	// Verify this session belongs to Gemini CLI
   163→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != g.Name() {
   164→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, g.Name())
   165→	}
   166→
   167→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   168→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   172→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	// Write the raw JSON data
   176→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   177→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	return nil
   181→}
   182→
   183→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Gemini CLI session.
   184→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   185→	return "gemini --resume " + sessionID
   186→}
   187→
   188→// GetProjectHash generates a unique hash for a project based on its root path.
   189→// This matches Gemini CLI's getProjectHash() which uses SHA256 of the project root.
   190→func GetProjectHash(projectRoot string) string {
   191→	hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(projectRoot))
   192→	return hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])
   193→}
   194→
   195→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   196→
   197→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current message count of a Gemini transcript.
   198→// Gemini uses JSON format with a messages array, so position is the message count.
   199→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   200→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   201→	if path == "" {
   202→		return 0, nil
   203→	}
   204→
   205→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   206→	if err != nil {
   207→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   208→			return 0, nil
   209→		}
   210→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	if len(data) == 0 {
   214→		return 0, nil
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   218→	if err != nil {
   219→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	return len(transcript.Messages), nil
   223→}
   224→
   225→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given message index.
   226→// For Gemini (JSON format), offset is the starting message index.
   227→// Returns:
   228→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Gemini (from Write/Edit tools)
   229→//   - currentPosition: total number of messages in the transcript
   230→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   231→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   232→	if path == "" {
   233→		return nil, 0, nil
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	data, readErr := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   237→	if readErr != nil {
   238→		if os.IsNotExist(readErr) {
   239→			return nil, 0, nil
   240→		}
   241→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   242→	}
   243→
   244→	if len(data) == 0 {
   245→		return nil, 0, nil
   246→	}
   247→
   248→	transcript, parseErr := ParseTranscript(data)
   249→	if parseErr != nil {
   250→		return nil, 0, parseErr
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	totalMessages := len(transcript.Messages)
   254→
   255→	// Extract files from messages starting at startOffset
   256→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   257→	for i := startOffset; i < len(transcript.Messages); i++ {
   258→		msg := transcript.Messages[i]
   259→		// Only process gemini messages (assistant messages)
   260→		if msg.Type != MessageTypeGemini {
   261→			continue
   262→		}
   263→
   264→		// Process tool calls in this message
   265→		for _, toolCall := range msg.ToolCalls {
   266→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
   267→			isModifyTool := false
   268→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
   269→				if toolCall.Name == name {
   270→					isModifyTool = true
   271→					break
   272→				}
   273→			}
   274→
   275→			if !isModifyTool {
   276→				continue
   277→			}
   278→
   279→			// Extract file path from args map
   280→			var file string
   281→			if fp, ok := toolCall.Args["file_path"].(string); ok && fp != "" {
   282→				file = fp
   283→			} else if p, ok := toolCall.Args["path"].(string); ok && p != "" {
   284→				file = p
   285→			} else if fn, ok := toolCall.Args["filename"].(string); ok && fn != "" {
   286→				file = fn
   287→			}
   288→
   289→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
   290→				fileSet[file] = true
   291→				files = append(files, file)
   292→			}
   293→		}
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	return files, totalMessages, nil
   297→}
   298→
   299→// ChunkTranscript splits a Gemini JSON transcript by distributing messages across chunks.
   300→// Gemini uses JSON format with a {"messages": [...]} structure, so chunking splits
   301→// the messages array while preserving the JSON structure in each chunk.
   302→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   303→	var transcript GeminiTranscript
   304→	if err := json.Unmarshal(content, &transcript); err != nil {
   305→		// Fall back to JSONL chunking if not valid Gemini JSON
   306→		chunks, chunkErr := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   307→		if chunkErr != nil {
   308→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk as JSONL: %w", chunkErr)
   309→		}
   310→		return chunks, nil
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	if len(transcript.Messages) == 0 {
   314→		return [][]byte{content}, nil
   315→	}
   316→
   317→	var chunks [][]byte
   318→	var currentMessages []GeminiMessage
   319→	currentSize := len(`{"messages":[]}`) // Base JSON structure size
   320→
   321→	for i, msg := range transcript.Messages {
   322→		// Marshal message to get its size
   323→		msgBytes, err := json.Marshal(msg)
   324→		if err != nil {
   325→			logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to marshal Gemini message during chunking",
   326→				slog.Int("message_index", i),
   327→				slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   328→			)
   329→			continue
   330→		}
   331→		msgSize := len(msgBytes) + 1 // +1 for comma separator
   332→
   333→		if currentSize+msgSize > maxSize && len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   334→			// Save current chunk
   335→			chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   336→			if err != nil {
   337→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal chunk: %w", err)
   338→			}
   339→			chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   340→
   341→			// Start new chunk
   342→			currentMessages = nil
   343→			currentSize = len(`{"messages":[]}`)
   344→		}
   345→
   346→		currentMessages = append(currentMessages, msg)
   347→		currentSize += msgSize
   348→	}
   349→
   350→	// Add the last chunk
   351→	if len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   352→		chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   353→		if err != nil {
   354→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal final chunk: %w", err)
   355→		}
   356→		chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   357→	}
   358→
   359→	// Ensure we created at least one chunk (could be empty if all messages failed to marshal)
   360→	if len(chunks) == 0 {
   361→		return nil, errors.New("failed to create any chunks: all messages failed to marshal")
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	return chunks, nil
   365→}
   366→
   367→// ReassembleTranscript merges Gemini JSON chunks by combining their message arrays.
   368→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   369→	var allMessages []GeminiMessage
   370→
   371→	for _, chunk := range chunks {
   372→		var transcript GeminiTranscript
   373→		if err := json.Unmarshal(chunk, &transcript); err != nil {
   374→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal chunk: %w", err)
   375→		}
   376→		allMessages = append(allMessages, transcript.Messages...)
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	result, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: allMessages})
   380→	if err != nil {
   381→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal reassembled transcript: %w", err)
   382→	}
   383→	return result, nil
   384→}
   385→
```

     1→// Package agent provides interfaces and types for integrating with coding agents.
     2→// It abstracts agent-specific behavior (hooks, log parsing, session storage) so that
     3→// the same Strategy implementations can work with any coding agent.
     4→package agent
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"io"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// Agent defines the interface for interacting with a coding agent.
    11→// Each agent implementation (Claude Code, Cursor, Aider, etc.) converts its
    12→// native format to the normalized types defined in this package.
    13→//
    14→// The interface is organized into four groups:
    15→//
    16→//   - Identity (5 methods): Name, Type, Description, DetectPresence, ProtectedDirs
    17→//   - Event Mapping (2 methods): HookNames, ParseHookEvent
    18→//   - Transcript Storage (3 methods): ReadTranscript, ChunkTranscript, ReassembleTranscript
    19→//   - Legacy (8 methods): Will be moved to optional interfaces or removed in a future phase
    20→type Agent interface {
    21→	// --- Identity ---
    22→
    23→	// Name returns the agent registry key (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini")
    24→	Name() AgentName
    25→
    26→	// Type returns the agent type identifier (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI")
    27→	// This is stored in metadata and trailers.
    28→	Type() AgentType
    29→
    30→	// Description returns a human-readable description for UI
    31→	Description() string
    32→
    33→	// IsPreview returns whether the agent integration is in preview or stable
    34→	IsPreview() bool
    35→
    36→	// DetectPresence checks if this agent is configured in the repository
    37→	DetectPresence() (bool, error)
    38→
    39→	// ProtectedDirs returns repo-root-relative directories that should never be
    40→	// modified or deleted during rewind or other destructive operations.
    41→	// Examples: [".claude"] for Claude, [".gemini"] for Gemini.
    42→	ProtectedDirs() []string
    43→
    44→	// --- Event Mapping ---
    45→
    46→	// HookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
    47→	// These become subcommands under `entire hooks <agent>`.
    48→	// e.g., ["stop", "user-prompt-submit", "session-start", "session-end"]
    49→	HookNames() []string
    50→
    51→	// ParseHookEvent translates an agent-native hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    52→	// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).
    53→	// This is the core contribution surface for new agent implementations.
    54→	ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error)
    55→
    56→	// --- Transcript Storage ---
    57→
    58→	// ReadTranscript reads the raw transcript bytes for a session.
    59→	ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error)
    60→
    61→	// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks if it exceeds maxSize.
    62→	// Returns a slice of chunks. If the transcript fits in one chunk, returns single-element slice.
    63→	// The chunking is format-aware: JSONL splits at line boundaries, JSON splits message arrays.
    64→	ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
    65→
    66→	// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.
    67→	// Handles format-specific reassembly (JSONL concatenation, JSON message merging).
    68→	ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
    69→
    70→	// --- Legacy methods (will move to optional interfaces in Phase 4) ---
    71→
    72→	// GetSessionID extracts session ID from hook input.
    73→	GetSessionID(input *HookInput) string
    74→
    75→	// GetSessionDir returns where agent stores session data for this repo.
    76→	GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)
    77→
    78→	// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to the session transcript file.
    79→	ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string
    80→
    81→	// ReadSession reads session data from agent's storage.
    82→	ReadSession(input *HookInput) (*AgentSession, error)
    83→
    84→	// WriteSession writes session data for resumption.
    85→	WriteSession(session *AgentSession) error
    86→
    87→	// FormatResumeCommand returns command to resume a session.
    88→	FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string
    89→}
    90→
    91→// HookSupport is implemented by agents with lifecycle hooks.
    92→// This optional interface allows agents like Claude Code and Cursor to
    93→// install and manage hooks that notify Entire of agent events.
    94→type HookSupport interface {
    95→	Agent
    96→
    97→	// InstallHooks installs agent-specific hooks.
    98→	// If localDev is true, hooks point to local development build.
    99→	// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
   100→	// Returns the number of hooks installed.
   101→	InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error)
   102→
   103→	// UninstallHooks removes installed hooks
   104→	UninstallHooks() error
   105→
   106→	// AreHooksInstalled checks if hooks are currently installed
   107→	AreHooksInstalled() bool
   108→}
   109→
   110→// FileWatcher is implemented by agents that use file-based detection.
   111→// Agents like Aider that don't support hooks can use file watching
   112→// to detect session activity.
   113→type FileWatcher interface {
   114→	Agent
   115→
   116→	// GetWatchPaths returns paths to watch for session changes
   117→	GetWatchPaths() ([]string, error)
   118→
   119→	// OnFileChange handles a detected file change and returns session info
   120→	OnFileChange(path string) (*SessionChange, error)
   121→}
   122→
   123→// TranscriptAnalyzer provides format-specific transcript parsing.
   124→// Agents that implement this get richer checkpoints (transcript-derived file lists,
   125→// prompts, summaries). Agents that don't still participate in the checkpoint lifecycle
   126→// via git-status-based file detection and raw transcript storage.
   127→type TranscriptAnalyzer interface {
   128→	Agent
   129→
   130→	// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current position (length) of a transcript.
   131→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), this is the line count.
   132→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), this is the message count.
   133→	// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   134→	GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error)
   135→
   136→	// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given offset.
   137→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), offset is the starting line number.
   138→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), offset is the starting message index.
   139→	// Returns:
   140→	//   - files: list of file paths modified by the agent (from Write/Edit tools)
   141→	//   - currentPosition: the current position (line count or message count)
   142→	//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   143→	ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error)
   144→
   145→	// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given offset.
   146→	ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error)
   147→
   148→	// ExtractSummary extracts a summary of the session from the transcript.
   149→	ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error)
   150→}
   151→
   152→// TranscriptPreparer is called before ReadTranscript to handle agent-specific
   153→// flush/sync requirements (e.g., Claude Code's async transcript writing).
   154→// The framework calls PrepareTranscript before ReadTranscript if implemented.
   155→type TranscriptPreparer interface {
   156→	Agent
   157→
   158→	// PrepareTranscript ensures the transcript is ready to read.
   159→	// For Claude Code, this waits for the async transcript flush to complete.
   160→	PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error
   161→}
   162→
   163→// TokenCalculator provides token usage calculation for a session.
   164→// The framework calls this during step save and checkpoint if implemented.
   165→type TokenCalculator interface {
   166→	Agent
   167→
   168→	// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from the transcript starting at the given offset.
   169→	CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*TokenUsage, error)
   170→}
   171→
   172→// SubagentAwareExtractor provides methods for extracting files and tokens including subagents.
   173→// Agents that support spawning subagents (like Claude Code's Task tool) should implement this
   174→// to ensure subagent contributions are included in checkpoints.
   175→type SubagentAwareExtractor interface {
   176→	Agent
   177→
   178→	// ExtractAllModifiedFiles extracts files modified by both the main agent and any spawned subagents.
   179→	// The subagentsDir parameter specifies where subagent transcripts are stored.
   180→	// Returns a deduplicated list of all modified file paths.
   181→	ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error)
   182→
   183→	// CalculateTotalTokenUsage computes token usage including all spawned subagents.
   184→	// The subagentsDir parameter specifies where subagent transcripts are stored.
   185→	CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) (*TokenUsage, error)
   186→}
   187→

     1→package agent
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"slices"
     6→	"sync"
     7→)
     8→
     9→var (
    10→	registryMu sync.RWMutex
    11→	registry   = make(map[AgentName]Factory)
    12→)
    13→
    14→// Factory creates a new agent instance
    15→type Factory func() Agent
    16→
    17→// Register adds an agent factory to the registry.
    18→// Called from init() in each agent implementation.
    19→func Register(name AgentName, factory Factory) {
    20→	registryMu.Lock()
    21→	defer registryMu.Unlock()
    22→	registry[name] = factory
    23→}
    24→
    25→// Get retrieves an agent by name.
    26→//
    27→
    28→func Get(name AgentName) (Agent, error) {
    29→	registryMu.RLock()
    30→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    31→
    32→	factory, ok := registry[name]
    33→	if !ok {
    34→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent: %s (available: %v)", name, List())
    35→	}
    36→	return factory(), nil
    37→}
    38→
    39→// List returns all registered agent names in sorted order.
    40→func List() []AgentName {
    41→	registryMu.RLock()
    42→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    43→
    44→	names := make([]AgentName, 0, len(registry))
    45→	for name := range registry {
    46→		names = append(names, name)
    47→	}
    48→	slices.Sort(names)
    49→	return names
    50→}
    51→
    52→// DetectAll returns all agents whose DetectPresence reports true.
    53→// Agents are checked in sorted name order (via List()) for deterministic results.
    54→// Returns an empty slice when no agent is detected.
    55→func DetectAll() []Agent {
    56→	names := List() // sorted, lock-safe
    57→
    58→	var detected []Agent
    59→	for _, name := range names {
    60→		ag, err := Get(name)
    61→		if err != nil {
    62→			continue
    63→		}
    64→		if present, err := ag.DetectPresence(); err == nil && present {
    65→			detected = append(detected, ag)
    66→		}
    67→	}
    68→	return detected
    69→}
    70→
    71→// Detect attempts to auto-detect which agent is being used.
    72→// Iterates registered agents in sorted name order for deterministic results.
    73→// Returns the first agent whose DetectPresence reports true.
    74→func Detect() (Agent, error) {
    75→	detected := DetectAll()
    76→	if len(detected) == 0 {
    77→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no agent detected (available: %v)", List())
    78→	}
    79→	return detected[0], nil
    80→}
    81→
    82→// AgentName is the registry key type for agents (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini").
    83→//
    84→//nolint:revive // stuttering is intentional - distinguishes from AgentType when both are used
    85→type AgentName string
    86→
    87→// AgentType is the display name type stored in metadata/trailers (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI").
    88→//
    89→//nolint:revive // stuttering is intentional - distinguishes from AgentName when both are used
    90→type AgentType string
    91→
    92→// Agent name constants (registry keys)
    93→const (
    94→	AgentNameClaudeCode AgentName = "claude-code"
    95→	AgentNameGemini     AgentName = "gemini"
    96→	AgentNameOpenCode   AgentName = "opencode"
    97→)
    98→
    99→// Agent type constants (type identifiers stored in metadata/trailers)
   100→const (
   101→	AgentTypeClaudeCode AgentType = "Claude Code"
   102→	AgentTypeGemini     AgentType = "Gemini CLI"
   103→	AgentTypeOpenCode   AgentType = "OpenCode"
   104→	AgentTypeUnknown    AgentType = "Agent" // Fallback for backwards compatibility
   105→)
   106→
   107→// DefaultAgentName is the registry key for the default agent.
   108→const DefaultAgentName AgentName = AgentNameClaudeCode
   109→
   110→// GetByAgentType retrieves an agent by its type identifier.
   111→//
   112→// Note: This uses a linear search that instantiates agents until a match is found.
   113→// This is acceptable because:
   114→//   - Agent count is small (~2-20 agents)
   115→//   - Agent factories are lightweight (empty struct allocation)
   116→//   - Called infrequently (commit hooks, rewind, debug commands - not hot paths)
   117→//   - Cost is ~400ns worst case vs milliseconds for I/O operations
   118→//
   119→// Only optimize if agent count exceeds 100 or profiling shows this as a bottleneck.
   120→func GetByAgentType(agentType AgentType) (Agent, error) {
   121→	registryMu.RLock()
   122→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
   123→
   124→	for _, factory := range registry {
   125→		ag := factory()
   126→		if ag.Type() == agentType {
   127→			return ag, nil
   128→		}
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent type: %s", agentType)
   132→}
   133→
   134→// AllProtectedDirs returns the union of ProtectedDirs from all registered agents.
   135→func AllProtectedDirs() []string {
   136→	// Copy factories under the lock, then release before calling external code.
   137→	registryMu.RLock()
   138→	factories := make([]Factory, 0, len(registry))
   139→	for _, f := range registry {
   140→		factories = append(factories, f)
   141→	}
   142→	registryMu.RUnlock()
   143→
   144→	seen := make(map[string]struct{})
   145→	var dirs []string
   146→	for _, factory := range factories {
   147→		for _, d := range factory().ProtectedDirs() {
   148→			if _, ok := seen[d]; !ok {
   149→				seen[d] = struct{}{}
   150→				dirs = append(dirs, d)
   151→			}
   152→		}
   153→	}
   154→	slices.Sort(dirs)
   155→	return dirs
   156→}
   157→
   158→// Default returns the default agent.
   159→// Returns nil if the default agent is not registered.
   160→//
   161→//nolint:errcheck // Factory pattern returns interface; error is acceptable to ignore for default
   162→func Default() Agent {
   163→	a, _ := Get(DefaultAgentName)
   164→	return a
   165→}
   166→
```

New file: `cmd/entire/cli/summarize/trail_title.go`\n\nTrail title/description generation, agent-aware:\n\n```go\npackage summarize\n\nconst trailTitlePromptTemplate = `Analyze this development session transcript and generate a title and description.\n\n<transcript>\n%s\n</transcript>\n\nReturn a JSON object:\n{\n  \"title\": \"Short imperative title (max 80 chars)\",\n  \"description\": \"1-3 sentence description of what was accomplished and why\"\n}\n\nGuidelines:\n- Title: imperative mood, captures core intent (e.g. \"Add user authentication flow\")\n- Description: explain the \"what\" and \"why\", not the \"how\"\n- Return ONLY the JSON object`\n\nconst trailTitleModel = \"haiku\"\n\ntype TrailTitleResult struct {\n    Title       string `json:\"title\"`\n    Description string `json:\"description\"`\n}\n\nfunc GenerateTrailTitle(ctx context.Context, transcriptBytes []byte, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType) (*TrailTitleResult, error) {\n    // 1. Get the active agent and check if it implements TextGenerator\n    ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(agentType)\n    if err != nil {\n        return nil, fmt.Errorf(\"agent not found: %w\", err)\n    }\n    gen, ok := ag.(agent.TextGenerator)\n    if !ok {\n        return nil, fmt.Errorf(\"agent %s does not support text generation\", agentType)\n    }\n\n    // 2. Build condensed transcript (reuse existing infrastructure)\n    condensed, err := BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes(transcriptBytes, agentType)\n    if err != nil {\n        return nil, err\n    }\n    input := Input{Transcript: condensed, FilesTouched: filesTouched}\n    transcriptText := FormatCondensedTranscript(input)\n\n    // 3. Build prompt and call agent's TextGenerator\n    prompt := fmt.Sprintf(trailTitlePromptTemplate, transcriptText)\n    rawResult, err := gen.GenerateText(ctx, prompt, trailTitleModel)\n    if err != nil {\n        return nil, err\n    }\n\n    // 4. Parse JSON response\n    cleaned := extractJSONFromMarkdown(rawResult)\n    var result TrailTitleResult\n    if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(cleaned), &result); err != nil {\n        return nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse trail title JSON: %w\", err)\n    }\n    return &result, nil\n}\n```\n\n### C5. Add `generateTrailTitleFromTranscript` helper\n\n**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`\n\n```go\nfunc generateTrailTitleFromTranscript(repo *git.Repository, branchName string, transcript []byte, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType) {\n    if !settings.IsSummarizeEnabled() {\n        return\n    }\n    store := trail.NewStore(repo)\n    existing, err := store.FindByBranch(branchName)\n    if err != nil || existing == nil {\n        return\n    }\n    // Only generate once: skip if description already set\n    if existing.Description != \"\" {\n        return\n    }\n    logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"trail-title\")\n    result, err := summarize.GenerateTrailTitle(logCtx, transcript, filesTouched, agentType)\n    if err != nil {\n        logging.Debug(logCtx, \"trail title generation skipped\",\n            slog.String(\"error\", err.Error()))\n        return\n    }\n    //nolint:errcheck // best-effort: trail title generation is non-critical\n    store.Update(existing.TrailID, func(m *trail.Metadata) {\n        if result.Title != \"\" {\n            m.Title = result.Title\n        }\n        if result.Description != \"\" {\n            m.Description = result.Description\n        }\n    })\n}\n```\n\n### C7. Wire into `condenseAndUpdateState` (manual-commit)\n\n**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`\n\nIn `condenseAndUpdateState()`, after the existing `appendCheckpointToTrail()` call (~line 825):\n\n```go\n// Generate trail title/description from transcript (best-effort, first condensation only)\nbranchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)\nif branchName != \"\" && branchName != GetDefaultBranchName(repo) {\n    generateTrailTitleFromTranscript(repo, branchName, result.Transcript, result.FilesTouched, state.AgentType)\n}\n```\n\n### C8. Agents opt in when their CLI supports non-interactive generation. Claude Code implements it today; Gemini/OpenCode can add it later.\n2. **Haiku model** — trail titles are simple; haiku is fast (~1-2s) and cheap\n3. **One-time gate** — `Description != \"\"` ensures LLM is only called on first condensation per trail\n4. **Settings gate** — reuses `settings.IsSummarizeEnabled()`; no new setting\n5. **Graceful skip** — if agent doesn't implement `TextGenerator`, title stays as prompt-derived; no error shown to user\n6. **No `ClaudeGenerator` refactor** — existing summarize code works; trail titles use `TextGenerator` directly\n\n## Verification\n\n```bash\nmise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci\n```\n\nManual test:\n1. Create a branch, enable entire with `summarize.enabled: true`\n2. Run a Claude Code session, make a commit\n3. Check `entire trail` output — should show LLM-generated title and description\n4. Make a second commit — title should NOT be regenerated (description already set)\n"
